# The Japan Year Book 1930 — Who's Who & Directories

1,851 entries, LLM-transcribed. These entries were transcribed by the vision model of Opus 5.5. Before using any of these details, you must verify specific details with the original source which is linked to whenever possible.


## Appendix A: Who's Who in Japan

### Army and Navy

**Abo, Kiyokazu** Baron (suc. 1909), Admiral, Supreme War Councillor; b. 1879 in Miye-ken; grad. from Naval Academy, '97; Lieut.-Comdr., '00; Comdr., '06; Naval Attache to Japanese Embassy in London and Superintendent of Naval Construction, '13; attached to Naval Board of Command, '15; in command of warship Aki, '15; Vice-Chief of Naval Board of Command, '16; Vice-Min. of Navy, '23; Comdr.-in-Chief of Kure Naval Station, '25; Supreme War Councillor, '26; Comdr.-in-Chief of Yokosuka Naval Station, '27-28; present post, Dec. '28. Add. Ichigaya Tomihisa-cho, Ushigome, Tokyo. — p. 1 ([scan leaf 574](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n574/mode/1up))

**Akiyama, Yoshifuru** Gen. (ret.), ex-Dir. Mil. Edu. Board, '20-23; b. 1855 in Ehime-ken; Sub-Lieut., '79; Major-Gen., '99; Lieut.-Gen., '09; took part in Japan-China War, Boxers Trouble and Russo-Japanese War; Sup't. of Cavalry; then Commander of 13th Div., '13; Com. of Guards Division, '15; Com. of Chosen Garrison, '16; full Gen. in '16; Supreme War Councillor, '17; retired from active service, '23; director of the Hokuyo Middle School (private), Matsuyama, Ehime-ken, since 1925. Add. Matsuyama, Ehime-ken. — p. 1 ([scan leaf 574](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n574/mode/1up))

**Banzai, Rihachiro** Lt.-Gen. (ret.), Crown Mem. House of Peers (nom. 1928); b. 1873 in Tokyo; Sub-Lieut., '92; Major, '03; Colonel, '12; Maj.-Gen., '18; had more than ten years' connection with the Chinese Government to which he was military advisor till '27; is one of the authorities of things Chinese. Add. Yotsuya Aizumi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 1 ([scan leaf 574](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n574/mode/1up))

**Fukuda, Masataro** Gen., ex-Supreme War Councillor; b. 1870 in Nagasaki-ken; Sub-Lieut., '87; Captain, '95; stayed in Berlin and Vienna as Military Attache for years; took part in '04-05 war as a staff officer under Marshal Oyama; Colonel, '07; Maj.-Gen. and brigade commander, '11; Chief of Staff, Mil. Dept. of Kwantung Government; Lieut.-Gen. and Commander of 5th Div., '16; Vice-Chief of Gen. Staff; Gen. in '22; Com.-in-Chief of Formosan Garrison, '18-22; Comdr. of Tokyo Garrison at the time of the Earthquake. Add. Yoyogi, near Tokyo. — p. 1 ([scan leaf 574](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n574/mode/1up))

**Hata, Eitaro** Lieut.-Gen., Comdr. Kwantung Army; b. 1872; grad. from Military Cadets School in 1893; further studied at Military Staff College, graduating in 1903; after holding the posts of Mil. Attache to Japanese Emb. in London, Regimental Comdr., etc. successively was appointed Dir. of Mil. Affairs Bureau, War Office, in '23; Vice-Min. of War, '26-28; Commander of 1st Division, '28-29; present post in June '29. Was on the staff of the Japanese expedition to Tsingtao under General Kamio in 1914 and rendered distinguished services in co-operation with the British forces under Brigadier General Barnardiston. Add. Azabu Mikawadai, Tokyo. — p. 1 ([scan leaf 574](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n574/mode/1up))

**Horiuchi, Saburo** Vice-Adm. (ret.); b. 1869; early entered the Navy and was gradually promoted till attaining the present post; was for some time Dir. of Military Affairs Bureau, Navy Dept. and Comdr. of Training Squadron; Dir. Naval Staff Coll., '22-23, etc. Add. Navy Dep't., Tokyo. — p. 1 ([scan leaf 574](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n574/mode/1up))

**Kato, Kanji** Admiral, Grand Chamberlain and Privy Councillor; b. 1881; Lieut., '97; Captain, '11; attached to the Embassy at London; Private Secretary to Navy Minister; commanded the Japanese fleet detailed in Vladivostok during the Russian revolution, '15-16; attended the Washington Conference as Chief Naval Adviser, '21-22; Vice-Adm. and Vice-Chief of Naval Gen. Staff, '22-23; Director of Naval Staff College, '23-24; Com.-in-Chief of Yokosuka Naval Station, '24-25; Com.-in-Chief of 1st Squadron and also of Combined Fleet, '26-29; Adm., '27; present post in Jan., 29. Add. Tokyo. — p. 1 ([scan leaf 574](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n574/mode/1up))

**Kawai, Misao** Gen. (ret.), Privy Councillor; b. 1864 in Oita; Sub-Lt. of Infantry, '86; grad. from Staff Coll., '97; further studied in Germany; took part in the Russo-Japanese war as a staff officer; Div. Com. and Com.-in-Chief of Kwantung Garrison, '21-22; full Gen. and Supreme War Councillor, '22; Chief of General Staff, '23-26; retired from active service and appointed to present post in '27. Add. Tokyo. — p. 2 ([scan leaf 575](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n575/mode/1up))

**Komatsu, Teruhisa** Lieut. (Navy); 1st Marquis, the House being newly created in '10 to keep up the House of the late Prince Komatsu who died without an heir; b. 1888, 4th son of the late Prince Yoshihisa Kitashirakawa and younger brother of the late Prince Narihisa who died in Paris, '23; grad. at Naval Academy; married Prince Shimazu's sister, '09; visited Europe, '25-26. Add. Asakusa-ku, Tokyo. — p. 2 ([scan leaf 575](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n575/mode/1up))

**Mayeda, Toshinari** Marquis, head of the great House of Kaga (founded in 16th cent.), Colonel (Infantry); b. 1885, son of a scion of the family and was adopted by it as heir. Attached to the British Army in France, '15-16; was on the suite of Prince Higashi Fushimi on a visit to King George on behalf of the Emperor in '18; to Paris in connection with the Peace Conference, '20, where Marchioness Nami-ko died; married Kiku-ko, daughter of the late Count Sakai, '28; Mil. Attache to the Japanese Embassy, London, since '28. Add. Japanese Embassy, London. — p. 2 ([scan leaf 575](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n575/mode/1up))

**Mizuno, Kotoku** Captain (ret.); b. 1877 at Matsuyama; grad. from Naval Academy, '99; Sub-Lieut., '00 and Captain, '05; took part in the Japan-Russia war as a torpedo-boat commander; travelled in Europe in '16 for inspection of the post-war military affairs and again in '19 for a similar purpose; retired from the active service in '21; has since taken to writing as a warm advocate of peace and disarmament movement. Add. Komazawa, near Tokyo. — p. 2 ([scan leaf 575](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n575/mode/1up))

**Nagaoka, Gaishi** M.P., Lt.-Gen. (ret.); b. 1858 in Yamaguchi; Sub-Lieut., '73; studied at Mil. Staff Coll., '83-85; Maj.-Gen., '02; Vice-Chief of General Staff during Russo-Japanese War; Bureau Chief in War Office; Lieut.-Gen., '09 and Comdr. of 16th Div.; retired from active service, '15; toured Europe and America, '20; was Vice-Pres. of the Imp. Aero. Ass'n. till '27. Add. Sendagaya Harajuku, Tokyo. — p. 2 ([scan leaf 575](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n575/mode/1up))

**Nara, Takeji** Gen., Chief Aide-de-Camp to the Emperor; b. 1868 in Tochigi-ken; Sub-Lieut. of Artillery, '89; took part in the Sino-Japanese and Russo-Japanese Wars; Chief of Staff and then Commander of Japanese Garrison in China, '13-17; General in '17; represented the Japanese Army in the Paris Peace Conference, '18; accompanied the Crown Prince (the present Emperor) to Europe, '21. Add. Imp. Household Dept. — p. 2 ([scan leaf 575](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n575/mode/1up))

**Nomura, Kichisaburo** Vice-Adm., Comdr. of Training Squadron; b. 1877 in Wakayama-ken; grad. from Naval Cadet School, '98; on the Commission for bringing home the Mikasa from England, '01; took part in the Russo-Japanese war; Attache in Austria-Hungary and Germany, '08-11; Attache at Washington, '15-18; Adjutant to Chief Naval Delegate to the Peace Conference at Paris and the Disarmament Conference at Washington; Rear-Adm. and Commander of the squadron detailed to China, '22-25; Dir. of Education Bureau (Navy Dept.), '25-26; Vice-Chief of Naval Board of Command, '26-28; present post, '29. Add. Sendagaya, Tokyo. — p. 2 ([scan leaf 575](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n575/mode/1up))

**Ogasawara, Naganari** Viscount (cr. '84 by heritage), Rear-Adm. (ret.), Court Councillor; b. 1867 of the family that formerly held the fief of Karatsu clan; grad. from the Naval Academy, '88; took part in both Sino-Japanese and Russo-Japanese Wars. Add. Yoyohata, near Tokyo. — p. 2 ([scan leaf 575](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n575/mode/1up))

**Okada, Keisuke** Adm., Supreme War Councillor; b. 1868 in Fukui-ken; grad. from the Naval Academy, 1890 and appointed Sub-Lieut., promoted to Lieut., '94, Commander, '04 and Captain, '80, and at the same time Dir. of Torpedo School; Rear-Adm. and Chief of Ordnance Dept. of Sasebo Arsenal, '12; Vice-Adm. and Chief of Sasebo Arsenal, '16; Chief of Fleet Administration Dept., '20-23; Vice-Minister of Navy, '23-24; full Admiral, '24; Supreme War Councillor, '24; Com.-in-Chief of 1st Squadron and also of Combined Squadron, '24-26; Com.-in-Chief of Yokosuka Naval Station, '26; Minister of Navy, '27-29; present post in July, '29. Add. Navy Dept., Tokyo. — p. 3 ([scan leaf 576](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n576/mode/1up))

**Oku, Yasukata** Marshal, Count (cr. Baron '95; Count '09), Mem. Supreme Military Council; b. 1846 in Fukuoka-ken; entered the Army, '71; won distinction in the Civil War of '77 for having cut through the besieging lines of the rebels round Kumamoto; commanded 5th Army Division in the Japan-China War; Com. of Tokyo Bay Defence, Com. of Eastern Military Districts, and held other high posts; full Gen., '03; attended the Durbar at Delhi! led the 2nd Army in Russo-Japanese War; Chief of the General Staff, '06-12; Marshal in '11. Add. Ushigome Haraikatamachi, Tokyo. — p. 3 ([scan leaf 576](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n576/mode/1up))

**Osumi, Mineo** Vice-Adm., Comdr.-in-Chief of 2nd Squadron; b. 187[illegible] in Aichi-ken; grad. from Naval Academy, '98; an hero of the memorable Port Arthur blockade in the Russo-Japanese War; steadily promoted and by '20 he was a Rear-Adm. and a senior officer of the General Staff, meanwhile having been Adjutant to Navy Minister, Commander of the Asahi, Naval Attache in France, etc.; attended the Washington Disarmament Conference as assis. to the chief naval delegate; also the second League of Nations Conference; Vice-Adm., '25; Vice-Minister of Navy, '25-28; present post in Dec. '28. Add. Navy Dept., Tokyo. — p. 3 ([scan leaf 576](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n576/mode/1up))

**Saigo, Yorinori** 2nd Marquis, Mem. House of Peers, Col. (ret.); b. 1878 son of the late Saigo Yorimichi (d. 1902), one of the notables associated with the Restoration. Add. Kamimeguro, near Tokyo. — p. 3 ([scan leaf 576](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n576/mode/1up))

**Saito, Minoru** Viscount (Baron '07; Visc. '25), Adm. (ret.), Gov.-Gen. of Chosen; b. 1858 in Iwate-ken; entered Navy, '73; at the time of Sino-Japanese War held post of naval aide-de-camp to His Majesty; Comdr., '97 and Capt., '98; Vice-Min. of Navy under Adm. Count Yamamoto same year; Rear-Adm., '00; Vice-Adm., '04; full Adm., '12; granted 2nd class Golden Kite and Grand Cordon of Rising Sun for his service in connection with the Russo-Japanese War; Minister of Navy, '13-14; Gov.-Gen. of Chosen, '19-27; represented Japan in the 2nd Naval Armament Limitation Conference at Geneva, '27; Privy Councillor, Dec. '27; again Gov.-Gen. of Chosen, Aug. '29. Add. Seoul (Chosen). — p. 3 ([scan leaf 576](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n576/mode/1up))

**Shibuya, Ariaki** Lieut.-Gen. (ret.); b. 1856 in Wakayama; Sub-Lieut. of Cavalry, '79; entered Military Staff Coll. in '84; served for years as Aide-de-Camp to the Crown Prince, etc.; Lieut.-Gen., '08; Inspector of Commissariat Dept.; Dir., Imp. Mews and then Court Councillor in '21. Add. Azabu Gazenbo, Tokyo. — p. 3 ([scan leaf 576](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n576/mode/1up))

**Shirakawa, Yoshinori** Gen., Supreme War Councillor; b. 1870 in Ehime-ken; Sub-Lt. Infantry, '19; Capt., '98; Col., '09; Maj.-Gen., '15; took part in the Russo-Japanese War; Lt.-Gen. and Dir. of Military Staff Coll., '18; dispatched to Siberia as Comdr. of 11th Div., '21-22; Vice-Min. of War, '22-24; Commander of Kwantung Army, '25-27; Minister of War, '27-29; present post in July '29. Add. War Office, Tokyo. — p. 3 ([scan leaf 576](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n576/mode/1up))

**Soga, Sukenori** Lieut.-Gen. (ret.), Visc. (cr. '84), ex-President of nationalized Nippon Railway Co., Mem. House of Peers ('91-15); b. 1843 in Saga-ken; early entered the Army; took distinguished part as Commander of Brigade on the occasion of Civil War of '77; subsequently a Div. Commander at various places; was for a while Vice-Chief of the General Staff; retired from active service about '87; was once chief military tutor and Aide-de-Camp to the Crown Prince. Add. Surugadai, Tokyo. — p. 3 ([scan leaf 576](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n576/mode/1up))

**Sugano, Shoichi** Gen. (ret.); b. 1870 in Yamaguchi-ken; studied at the Staff Coll.; Sub-Lt., '92; Lt.-Gen., '19; stayed in Belgium and England as Attache; Dir. of Mil. Affairs Bureau, War Office, and then Div. Com., etc.; Vice-Min. of War, '21-22; Comdr. of Taiwan Army, '24-27; was on the staff of Gen. Nogi's Army in the 1904 war; Supreme War Councillor, '27-28. Add. War Office, Tokyo. — p. 4 ([scan leaf 577](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n577/mode/1up))

**Takarabe, Hyo** Adm., Minister of Navy; b. 1867 in Hyuga; grad. from Naval Cadet School, '90; was on staff at the headquarters in '94-95 and '04-05 wars; Capt., '95; Vice-Adm., '13 and Vice-Min. of Navy, '13-14; was placed on waiting list in connection with the Naval Scandal, '14; Comdr. of 3rd Squadron, '17; Comdr. of Port Arthur Naval Station, '15-00; Comdr. of Sasebo Naval Station, '18-22; then similar post at Yokosuka; Minister of Navy, '23-27; then Supreme War Councillor; again Minister of Navy in July '29. Add. Shiba Shirokane Sanko-cho, Tokyo. — p. 4 ([scan leaf 577](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n577/mode/1up))

**Takeshita, Isamu** Adm., Supreme War Councillor; b. 1869 in Kagoshima-ken; Sub-Lieut., '90; Commander, '07; Capt., '11; Rear-Adm., '13; Vice-Adm., '17; in the meantime Naval attache to Washington; represented the Navy in the Paris Peace Conference, '18; tutor to the Crown Prince and accompanied him to Europe in '21; Vice-Chief of Nav. Gen. Staff, '21-22; full Admiral and Comdr. of First Fleet, '23; Comdr.-in-Chief of Kure Naval Station, '24-25. Add. Navy Dept., Tokyo. — p. 4 ([scan leaf 577](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n577/mode/1up))

**Taniguchi, Shoshin** Vice-Adm., Comdr.-in-Chief of 1st Squadron and also of Combined Fleet since '28; b. 1870 in Hiroshima; grad. at the Naval Academy, '92; appointed Sub-Lieutenant same year and steadily promoted, being made Rear-Adm. in '17 and Comdr. of Makong naval port; was in the meantime adjutant to the Navy Minister, in command of a battle-cruiser, and Dir. of Personnel Bureau, Navy Dept., successively; Vice-Adm. in '23; Dir. of Naval Academy, and then Comdr.-in-Chief of 2nd Squadron, '25-26; Comdr.-in-Chief of Kure Naval Station, '26-28. Add. Navy Department, Tokyo. — p. 4 ([scan leaf 577](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n577/mode/1up))

**Togo, Heihachiro** Count (cr. '07), Admiral of Fleet, Mem. of Board of Marshals & Fleet Admirals, O.M. (Br.), 1st Class Golden Kite and Grand Order of Chrysanthemum; b. 1847, a son of petty retainer of the Lord of Kagoshima. He commenced sailor's career at 16 and at 21 first came under fire, in fighting with the late Enomoto's Kwaiten; studied in England, '71-73; in the Japan-China War commanded the cruiser Naniwa and sank the Chinese transport Kowsing, a British steamer flying the British flag; Rear-Adm. after the war; Vice-Adm. in '90; Comdr.-in-Chief of the Combined Fleet in the '04-05 War and hero of the Japan Sea battle fought on 27th May, '05; full Admiral, '04; Chief of Naval Staff, '05; Supreme War Councillor, '09; Adm. of Fleet in '12. Attended King George's Coronation on the suite of Prince Higashi-Fushimi; was at a time Lord Tutor to the Crown Prince (Emperor Taisho). Add. Koj. Kami-rokuban-cho, Tokyo. — p. 4 ([scan leaf 577](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n577/mode/1up))

**Uchiyama, Kojiro** Baron, Gen. (ret. '22); b. 1859 in Tottori; Sub-Lieut. (Art.), '79; attached to Legations in Russia and France; commanded artillery corps in the Russo-Japanese War; attache at Petrograd till '07; Commander of Yura Fort, '08; then a Divisional Commander; Chief Aide-de-Camp to the Emperor, '12-22; General, '15. Add. Harajuku, Sendagaya, Tokyo. — p. 4 ([scan leaf 577](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n577/mode/1up))

**Ugaki, Issei** Gen., Minister of War; b. 1863; Sub-Lieutenant in '93; Lt.-Gen. in '19, having been in the meanwhile Dir. of Staff Coll., attache at Petrograd, Vice-Min. of War, etc.; Minister of War, '25-27; Supreme War Councillor, '27-29; present post in July '29. Add. Yotsuya Naito-machi, Tokyo. — p. 4 ([scan leaf 577](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n577/mode/1up))

**Uyehara, Yusaku** Baron (cr. '97), Marshal, Mem. of Board of Marshals & Fleet Admirals; b. 1856 in Hyuga; Sub-Lieut. (Eng.), '79; studied in France, '89; staff officer to 1st Army in the Sino-Japanese War; attended the Coronation ceremony of the Tsar and also the Hague Peace Conference; chief staff under Marshal Nozu in Russo-Japanese War; Comdr. of 7th Division, '08; Minister of War, '12-13; Chief of Mil. Edu. Board, '14; Field Marshal in '19; Chief of General Staff, '18-23. Add. Oimachi, near Tokyo. — p. 5 ([scan leaf 578](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n578/mode/1up))

**Yamanashi, Hanzo** Gen. (ret.), Gov.-Gen. of Chosen (1927-'29); b. 1865 in Kanagawa-ken; Sub-Lt. (Infantry), '86; Capt., '95; Major., 1900, Colonel, '09; Maj.-Gen., '15; in the meantime instructor at the Military and Naval Colleges, Regimental and then Brigade Com., Section Chief of the Gen. Staff Office, and Chief of the Inspection Dept., Military Education Board in '16; Chief Staff of the Besieging Army of Tsingtao during the Great War; Lt.-Gen. and Vice-Min. of War under Gen. Tanaka in '17; Minister of War, '21-23; full Gen., '22; Gov.-General of Chosen, Dec. '27; retired in Aug. '29. Add. Tokyo. — p. 5 ([scan leaf 578](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n578/mode/1up))

**Yamamoto, Gombei** Admiral (ret.) Count, (cr. Baron '02; Count '07), G.C.M.G. (England), ex-Premier; b. 1852 in Satsuma and fought in the War of the Restoration on the Imperial side; one of the pioneer graduates of the Naval Academy, class '77; was on board a naval training vessel that visited America early in Meiji era; round-the-world voyage on board a German warship; Lieut., '80; as a commissioner was sent to England, '85 to bring home the cruiser Naniwa built there; dispatched to Europe and America in '87 on suite of the then Vice-Minister of the Navy (Adm. Kabayama); Captain, '90; Personal Secretary to the Minister till '95; Rear-Adm. and Bureau Director, '95; Vice-Adm., '96; Minister of Navy, '98-06; full Admiral, '04; despatched to England and U.S.A. on suite of Prince Fushimi, '07; formed a Cabinet in '13 succeeding the 3rd Katsura Ministry; resigned '14 owing to the Siemen's Scandal; formed the "Earthquake Cabinet," '23-24. Add. Takanawa, Tokyo. — p. 5 ([scan leaf 578](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n578/mode/1up))

**Yamashita, Gentaro** Adm. (ret.); b. 1863 in Yamagata-ken; grad. from Naval Academy, '83; 2nd Lieut., '85; Commander, '98; Captain, '06; Rear-Adm., '08; Vice-Adm., '12; Dir. of Naval Academy, '10-14; Deputy Chief of the Naval Staff Board and Mem. of Admirals' Council, '14-15; Comdr.-in-Chief of Sasebo Admiralty, '15-17; Admiral and Comdr.-in-Chief of First Squadron, '18; Chief of Naval Board of Command, '20-24; Supreme War Councillor, '25-27. Add. Koj. Hirakawa-cho, Tokyo. — p. 5 ([scan leaf 578](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n578/mode/1up))

**Yashiro, Rokuro** Baron (cr. '16), Adm. (ret.), Privy Councillor; b. 1860 in Aichi; Ensign in '85; Captain in '01; Second in Command of the Yashima; commander of the Asama in Russo-Japanese War; Dir. of Naval Staff College, '12-13; Com. of Maizuru Admiralty, '13-14; Minister of Navy, '14-15; Admiral, '16; Com. of 2nd Squadron, '17; after retiring from active service was appointed Privy Councillor in '26; was once Attache to the Legation at Petrograd. Add. Azabu Kogaicho, Tokyo. — p. 5 ([scan leaf 578](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n578/mode/1up))

### Arts

**Araki, Teijiro** (Jippo), painter in Japanese style, Mem. of Imp. Art Academy; b. 1872 in Nagasaki-ken; studied under Kampo, his father-in-law; teacher of Peers' Sch. and Women's Higher Normal Sch. Add. Nagasaki-machi, near Tokyo. — p. 5 ([scan leaf 578](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n578/mode/1up))

**Asakura, Fumio** sculptor, Prof. at Tokyo Fine Art School, ex-Mem. of Imp. Fine Art Acad.; b. 1883 in Oita-ken; grad. at Tokyo Fine Art Sch., '12; visited Borneo and studied the customs of natives, '11. Add. Shitaya Ueno, Tokyo. — p. 6 ([scan leaf 579](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n579/mode/1up))

**Fujishima, Takeji** painter in Western style, Prof. at Tokyo Fine Art Sch., Mem. of Imp. Fine Art Acad.; b. 1867 in Kagoshima-ken; stud. Western style under Nakamaru Seijuro, Matsuoka Hisashi, and Yamamoto Hosui; appointed assist.-prof. at Tokyo Fine Art Sch., '96; visited France for three years; appointed Prof. at the same, '09. Add. Hongo Akebono-cho, Tokyo. — p. 6 ([scan leaf 579](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n579/mode/1up))

**Ito, Hajime** (Shinsui), painter, Jap. style; b. 1898 in Tokyo; studied under Kaburagi Kiyokata, and is a master of Genre School. Add. Oi-machi, near Tokyo. — p. 6 ([scan leaf 579](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n579/mode/1up))

**Kaburagi, Ken-ichi** (Kiyokata), painter in Japanese style, Mem. of Imp. Fine Art Acad. (nom. '29); b. 1878 in Tokyo; stud. under Shibata Shinsai and Mizuno Toshikata and is a master of Genre school. Add. Hongo Tatsuoka-cho, Tokyo. — p. 6 ([scan leaf 579](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n579/mode/1up))

**Kanokogi, Takeshiro** Painter, Founder & Dir. of a private art school in Kyoto; b. 1874 in Okayama; studied painting under Koyama and Matsubara; went to U.S.A. in '00 and then to France where he studied under Jean Paul Laurens in whose studio he succeeded in attaining the first seat of Concours. Add. Shimokamo, Kyoto. — p. 6 ([scan leaf 579](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n579/mode/1up))

**Kawai, Yoshisaburo** (Gyokudo), Painter in Japanese style, Mem. Imp. Fine Art Acad., artist to the Court and Prof. at Tokyo Fine Art School; b. 1873 in Gifu-ken; studied painting of Kano school under Hashimoto Gaho. Add. Ueno, Tokyo. — p. 6 ([scan leaf 579](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n579/mode/1up))

**Komuro, Sadajiro** (Suiun), Painter in Japanese style, Mem. of Imp. Art Acad.; b. 1874 in Gumma-ken; studied under Tazaki Soun for eight years, and besides studied Chinese lit. and history; is a master of the Chinese School. Add. Koj. Nakarokuban-cho, Tokyo. — p. 6 ([scan leaf 579](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n579/mode/1up))

**Masaki, Naohiko** Dir. Tokyo Fine Art School, since '01; b. 1862 in Kyoto; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '82; Personal Sec. to Min. of Edu., '97. Add. Ushigome Yarai, Tokyo. — p. 6 ([scan leaf 579](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n579/mode/1up))

**Motoyama, Haku-un** sculptor; b. 1871 in Kochi; studied under Prof. Takamura Ko-un and at Tokyo Fine Art School, '95; filled for a short while the chair of assist. prof. at the alma mater; has since been engaged in casting bronze images of modern notables in the grand work of the Restoration, and has already finished images of Count Goto, Prince Ito, and others. Add. Koishikawa Haramachi, Tokyo. — p. 6 ([scan leaf 579](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n579/mode/1up))

**Nakamura, Sakutaro** (Fusetsu), painter in foreign style, Mem. of Imp. Fine Art Acad.; b. 1866 in Tokyo; stud. Chinese style painting under Makabe Unkyo and foreign style under Kono Jiro; stud. in France for four years; is a leading member of Pacific Painting Association. Add. Shitaya Kaminegishi-machi, Tokyo. — p. 6 ([scan leaf 579](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n579/mode/1up))

**Ohashi, Suiseki** gold medalist; b. 1865 in Gifu-ken; first studied painting under the late Watanabe Shokwa and then followed the Chinese style of southern school; won high-grade medals at domestic and foreign exhibitions, at Paris, '00, St. Louis, '03, and Anglo-Japanese Exhibition in London, '10. Add. Ogaki, Mino. — p. 6 ([scan leaf 579](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n579/mode/1up))

**Okada, Shin-ichiro** Prof. at Tokyo Fine Art School, Lecturer in Architecture, Waseda Univ.; b. in Saitama-ken; grad. in engineering, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '06; has designed the Kabukiza Theatre and many other buildings. Add. Ushigome Kagura-cho, Tokyo. — p. 6 ([scan leaf 579](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n579/mode/1up))

**Okada, Saburosuke** painter in foreign style, Prof. at Tokyo Fine Art School, Mem. Imp. Art Acad.; b. 1869 in Saga-ken; studied under Kuroda Kiyoteru and Kume Keiichiro; appointed assistant-prof. at Tokyo Fine Art. School, '96; visited France, '97-02, and appointed Prof. in the same year. Add. Ueno, Tokyo. — p. 6 ([scan leaf 579](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n579/mode/1up))

**Okuma, Ujihiro** sculptor; b. 1856 in Saitama; grad. from the Art. Dept. of the former Engineering Coll. in '80; studied in Italy, '95-99; cast bronze statues of Omura, Princes Kitashirakawa and Arisugawa, Prince Ito, etc.; has won gold or silver medals at home and abroad. Add. Koishikawa Takehayacho, Tokyo. — p. 7 ([scan leaf 580](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n580/mode/1up))

**Shimizu, Kamezo** master glyptic art and Prof. at Tokyo Fine Art School, in charge of the Metal Dept., from which he was graduated in '96; teacher till '15 at a Technical School in Kagawa; studied old arts and Buddhist philosophy at Nara Horyuji for three years; appointed to the present chair in '19; undertook the casting of metal decorative figures presented by the City of Tokyo to the Emperor and Empress at their silver wedding in '25. Add. Fine Art School, Uyeno, Tokyo. — p. 7 ([scan leaf 580](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n580/mode/1up))

**Shimomura, Harusaburo** (Kanzan), Painter in Japanese style, Dir. of Japan Fine Art Institute; b. 1873 in Wakayama; stud. under Kano Hogai and Hashimoto Gaho; grad. at Tokyo Fine Art School, '95; assistant prof. of the alma mater, '95-01; visited Europe, '07. Add. Shitaya Kamimisaki-minami-cho, Tokyo. — p. 7 ([scan leaf 580](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n580/mode/1up))

**Takamura, Ko-un** master-artist in wood carving, Prof. in Tokyo Fine Art School; b. 1852 in Tokyo; studied under Takamura Koun, a celebrated Buddhist image carver in Tokyo who died '79; is the maker of the original of the bronze image of Nanko erected in front of the Imp. Palace and of Senior Saigo at Ueno Park. Won a gold medal at the Paris Fair, '00; frequently honored to carve in presence of their Majesties. Art Commissioner to Imp. Household. Add. Komagome Hayashi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 7 ([scan leaf 580](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n580/mode/1up))

**Takeuchi, Tsunekichi** (Seiho), Mem. Imp. Art Acad., Painter to the Court, Prof. at Kyoto Fine Art Academy, Mem. of the Salon, Paris, in '22; is one of the living master painters in Japanese style; b. 1864 at Kyoto and studied under the late Bairei; visited Europe, '00. Add. Miike-dori, Yanagikoji, Kyoto. — p. 7 ([scan leaf 580](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n580/mode/1up))

**Tanabe, Itaru** painter in Western style, assist.-prof. of Tokyo Fine Art School; b. 1886 in Tokyo; grad. at Tokyo Fine Art Sch., '10; visited France, '23; sat on the Exhib. Committee of Imp. Acad. Exhibition, '25 and then assist.-prof. of alma mater. Add. Sugamo-machi, near Tokyo. — p. 7 ([scan leaf 580](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n580/mode/1up))

**Wada, Eisaku** painter in Western style, Prof. at Tokyo Fine Art Sch.; b. 1874 in Kagoshima-ken; grad. from the School in '96 and Assist.-Prof. at his alma mater for some years; sent to France where he studied under Raphael Collin; is a Mem. of Imperial Academy of Art. Add. Azabu Kasumicho, Tokyo. — p. 7 ([scan leaf 580](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n580/mode/1up))

**Wada, Sanzo** painter in Western style, Mem. of Imp. Fine Art Acad.; b. 1883 in Fukuoka-ken; grad. at Tokyo Fine Art School, '04; visited France, '10-15. Add. Akasaka Fukuyoshi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 7 ([scan leaf 580](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n580/mode/1up))

**Yokoyama, Hidemaro** (Taikan), a leading painter in Japanese style, Dir. of Japan Fine Art Institute; b. 1868 in Mito; grad. at Tokyo Fine Art School; after grad. studied under Gaho; once was Prof. at the alma mater; visited Europe and China. Add. Shitaya Kaya-cho, Tokyo. — p. 7 ([scan leaf 580](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n580/mode/1up))

### Banking

**Aiko, Kanesato** former Dir. of Fifteenth Bank, Ltd., Dir. of Taisho Bank, Ltd., etc.; b. 1863 in Kagoshima; stayed in China and Korea, and studied English and Chinese, etc.; was a member of Kagoshima City Assembly, and also of Kagoshima Chamber of Commerce. Add. Tokyo Club and Tokyo Bankers Club. — p. 7 ([scan leaf 580](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n580/mode/1up))

**Aoki, Tetsutaro** Pres. of Takasago Com. and Ind. Bank, Ltd.; b. 1867 in Okayama city; grad. from the political course, Law Dept., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '89, and on graduation, entered the service of the Finance Dept.; entered the Bank of Japan, '90; transferred to Yokohama Specie Bank, '91; stayed about ten years in Europe and America in charge of the Branches in New York, San Francisco and London, returning home in '05; left the Specie Bank in '09; was the Government Commercial Agent at Hongkong, '10-13; then Dir. of Japan Industrial Bank, Ltd., which he resigned in '18. Add. Kami-Osaki, near Tokyo. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 581](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n581/mode/1up))

**Ariga, Mitsutoyo** Pres. of the Chosen Development Bank, Ltd.; b. 1873 in Nagano-ken; grad. from the Tokyo Hogakuin (now Chuo Univ.); was in service of the Chosen Government-General. Add. Seishomonnai, Seoul. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 581](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n581/mode/1up))

**Aso, Jiro** ex-Dir. of Bank of Japan; b. 1871 in Osaka; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., in Law, class '94; has since been in the service of the Bank, having been Dir. of its branches at Nagoya, Osaka, etc. and also Dir. of the Loan and the Banking Dept. at the head office. Add. Azabu Toriizaka, Tokyo. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 581](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n581/mode/1up))

**Ayai, Tadahiko** Aud. of former Murai Sav. Bank, Shibaura Iron Works, and Chief of Acct. Sec. of Tokyo Electric Co.; b. 1871 in Oita-ken; edu. at Post and Telegraph School in Tokyo; was in service of Communications Dept. for years; went to U.S.A. in '90 to accomplish his study; after returning home joined the Murai Co. Add. Takada, near Tokyo. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 581](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n581/mode/1up))

**Baba, Ei-ichi** D.L., Gov. of Japan Hypothec Bank since '27; Crown Mem. House of Peers; b. 1879; edu. at Law in Tokyo Imp. Univ., '03; passed Higher Civil Serv. Exam.; Taxation Superintendent and then Sec. to Korean Govt.-Gen.; Pres. of Legislation Bureau in the Kato (Adm.) Cabinet, '22-24. Add. Hongo Maruyama-Fukuyama-cho, Tokyo. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 581](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n581/mode/1up))

**Fujita, Heitaro** 2nd Baron, one of the millionaires in Osaka; b. 1869 in Osaka; son of late Denzaburo Fujita who started in Osaka the Fujita Firm to undertake contract business with the Government, besides undertaking mining and engineering works. Educated at Cambridge Univ. Add. Amijima, Kita-ku, Osaka. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 581](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n581/mode/1up))

**Fujita, Tokujiro** Dir. of Fujita Bank (Osaka) and other cos. run by the Fujita family, being younger brother of Baron Fujita; b. 1880; grad. from Columbia Univ., '07. Add. Kiyamachi, Kyoto. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 581](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n581/mode/1up))

**Fukai, Eigo** Deputy-Gov. of Nippon Ginko; b. 1871 in Gumma-ken; edu. at the Doshisha College (Kyoto); was on the staff of the Kokumin Shimbun, '98-05; Private Secy. to Gov. of the Bank of Japan, '06-12; then a Bureau Chief and later a director, becoming Deputy-Gov. in '28, accompanied the Peace Envoys to Versailles in 1918 as an unofficial member. Add. Akasaka Hikawa-cho, Tokyo. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 581](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n581/mode/1up))

**Hara, Tomitaro** Pres. of 2nd Bank, head of the Hara Firm, Yokohama; b. 1870 in Gifu-ken; studied at Waseda Univ.; was adopted by the Hara family as husband of its daughter; is a Director of the Yokohama Fire Insurance Co. and a leading businessman in Yokohama; noted as collector of art objects. Add. Bentendori, Yokohama. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 581](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n581/mode/1up))

**Hayakawa, Yoshitaro** Pres. of Hayakawa Bill Broker Bank; b. 1885 in Tokyo; grad. from Keio, '06; served as military interpreter and then as chief of Loan Dept. of the Tokai Bank, Ltd., before he promoted the present Bank in '18. Add. Ushigome Funagawara, Tokyo. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 581](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n581/mode/1up))

**Hijikata, Kyucho** Governor of the Bank of Japan; b. 1870; grad. from the Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '93; entered the service of the Bank of Japan with which he was connected for years finally becoming its director; Pres. of Industrial Bank of Japan, '18-22; re-entered the Bank of Japan in '26, being appointed its Deputy-Governor; Governor of same in '28. Add. Sendagaya, Tokyo. — p. 8 ([scan leaf 581](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n581/mode/1up))

**Hiro-oka, Keizo** Pres. Kashima Bank, Osaka, head of the Kashima family, one of the millionaire houses in that city; b. 1876 in Tokyo, 2nd son of Visc. Hitotsuyanagi and adopted by the family; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., '00; his activity afterward has been confined to various interests of his father-in-law whose daughter he married. Add. Kashima Bank, Osaka. — p. 9 ([scan leaf 582](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n582/mode/1up))

**Hoshino, Sho** Pres. of Kawasaki Dai-Hyaku Bank; b. 1876 in Hiroshima; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '05; entered the Bank of Japan, and served at its London branch, '09-12; returned home and became the examiner of the Bank's Western branches; Dir. of its Kyoto branch, '13; private secretary to the Governor of the Bank, '15-17; Dir. of the Nagoya branch, '18; Dir. of the Osaka branch which resigned in '27. Add. Akasaka Daimachi, Tokyo. — p. 9 ([scan leaf 582](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n582/mode/1up))

**Ichinomiya, Reitaro** Vice-Pres. of Yokohama Specie Bank; b. 1870 in Tokyo; studied at the Tokyo Senshu Gakko and entered the service of the Bank; Manager of its New York Branch before he was called back to the Head Office as Dir.; present post at the time of its reorganization in '22. Add. Yokohama. — p. 9 ([scan leaf 582](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n582/mode/1up))

**Ikeda, Seihin** Mng.-Dir. of Mitsui Bank; b. 1868 at Yonezawa; studied at Keio and Harvard; assist. Dir. of Mitsui Bank's Osaka Branch, '98-0[illegible]; toured through Europe and America to inspect banking business in '0[illegible] and again in '29. Add. Azabu Shin-Ryudo, Tokyo. — p. 9 ([scan leaf 582](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n582/mode/1up))

**Ikuta, Sadayuki** Pres. Showa Bank, Dir. Tokyo Bankers' Association; b. 1870 in Tosa; studied at Keio and then in the U.S.A.; was long in the service of Bank of Japan. Add. Ichigaya-Kagacho, Tokyo. — p. 9 ([scan leaf 582](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n582/mode/1up))

**Imamura, Shigezo** B.A. (Cambridge), Pres. of Imamura Bank, 2nd son of the late Seinosuke Imamura; b. 1877 in Tokyo; studied at Trinity Coll., Cambridge; inherited father's estate in '20 and became Director of the Bank. Add. Shiba Tamachi, Tokyo. — p. 9 ([scan leaf 582](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n582/mode/1up))

**Inouye, Junnosuke** Minister of Finance; b. 1869 in Oita; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '95; entered the Bank of Japan the same year and was promoted to Dir. of Banking Bureau in '05; was the Bank's Agent in London, '08-11; Vice-Pres. of Yokohama Specie Bank, '11-13; then its President till '19; twice Governor of Bank of Japan, '24 and '27-28; Minister of Finance in the shortlived Yamamoto Cabinet, '24 and again in '29. Add. Azabu Mikawadai, Tokyo. — p. 9 ([scan leaf 582](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n582/mode/1up))

**Ishii, Kengo** Vice-Pres. of Dai-ichi (First Bank; b. 1874 in Tokyo; grad. from Tokyo Higher Commercial School, '95; entering the Bank he attained the present post. Add. Ushigome Takajomachi, Tokyo. — p. 9 ([scan leaf 582](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n582/mode/1up))

**Iwasaki, Koyata** 2nd Baron, Pres. of Banking Dept. of Mitsubishi Co.; b. 1879; succeeded in '09 to the title on the decease of his father Yanosuke (1st Baron and brother of the “Sea King”); was educated at Tokyo Imp. University and Cambridge. Add. Surugadai, Tokyo. — p. 9 ([scan leaf 582](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n582/mode/1up))

**Kadono, Rempachiro** Aud. of Mitsui Bank; b. 1868 in Miye-ken; entered the service of the bank in its early days; Mng. of its Yokohama Branch, etc. Add. Shiba Kanasugi-hamacho, Tokyo. — p. 9 ([scan leaf 582](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n582/mode/1up))

**Kajiwara, Chuji** ex-Gov. of Japan Hypothec Bank; b. 1867 in Yamagata-ken; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '97; entered the Bank of Japan and was its London Agent for years; finally becoming director; Vice-Pres. and then Pres. of Yokohama Specie Bank till '22; Gov. of Japan Hypothec Bank, '22-28. Add. Shimo-Osaki, Tokyo. — p. 9 ([scan leaf 582](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n582/mode/1up))

**Kanda, Raizo** Banker, Pres. of the former Kanda Bank; b. 1872 in Nagoya; edu. at the local commercial school and then entered a stockbroker's shop in Nagoya; established himself in Tokyo soon after the Russo-Japanese war and has risen to the present position. Is also proprietor of the Momijiya Stock-broking House. Add. Kanda Bank, Tokyo. — p. 10 ([scan leaf 583](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n583/mode/1up))

**Katayama, Yoshikatsu** Dir. of Chosen Bank; b. 1877 in Kyoto; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ. and passed the Higher Civil Serv. Exam., '05; Secretary of Agr. and Com., '05; sent abroad, '14; promoted to Counsellor and chief of the War Ins. Bureau; removed to the S.M.R.C. as Dir.; resigned in '21; present post in '23. Add. Seoul. — p. 10 ([scan leaf 583](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n583/mode/1up))

**Kato, Takeo** Dir. of Mitsubishi Bank; b. 1877 in Tochigi; grad. from Keio Univ., '01 and then entered service of the Bank; Chief of Osaka Branch before transfer to the head office. Add. Hongo Tatsuokacho, Tokyo. — p. 10 ([scan leaf 583](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n583/mode/1up))

**Kawakami, Naonosuke** ex-Dir. of Hypothec Bank of Japan; b. 1865; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ.; also studied at Berlin Univ.; was long connected with Yokohama Specie Bank. Toured in America. Add. Azabu Higashi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 10 ([scan leaf 583](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n583/mode/1up))

**Kawasaki, Hachiro-emon** Adviser of Kawasaki Savings Bank and other concerns; b. 1866 in Tokyo; grad. from Tokyo Higher Commercial School and further studied practical side of banking in America; was Pres. of the former Kawasaki Bank and also of Kawasaki Savings Bank till '28. Assumed his father's name Hachiro-emon in '13. Add. Azabu Torii-zaka, Tokyo. — p. 10 ([scan leaf 583](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n583/mode/1up))

**Kawashima, Chunosuke** Dir. of Yokohama Specie Bank; b. 1855 in Tokyo; studied French and naval architecture at the Govt. Dockyard; was engaged as interpreter and clerk '71 by a Dutch firm in Yokohama; next entered Yokohama Specie Bank and was its agent at Lyons of the Bank. '79-93; transferred to the Head Office, '94; Agent at Bombay till '95; Agent in Tokyo, '02-06; then recalled to the Head Office. Add. Hongo Fukuyama-cho, Tokyo. — p. 10 ([scan leaf 583](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n583/mode/1up))

**Kikumoto, Naojiro** Dir. of Mitsui Bank; b. 1870 in Miye-ken; edu. at Keio and entered the Bank, '98. Add. Aoyama Minami-cho, Tokyo. — p. 10 ([scan leaf 583](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n583/mode/1up))

**Kimura, Seishiro** ex-Vice-Gov. of Bank of Japan, Mem. House of Peers; b. 1861 in Okayama-ken; edu. at Keio Gijuku from which he graduated in '82; was editor of the Chugai Shogyo for many years; entered in '03 the Bank of Japan where he held Vice-Governorship, '19-26. Add. Shiba Minami Sakuma-cho, Tokyo. — p. 10 ([scan leaf 583](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n583/mode/1up))

**Kodama, Kenji** Pres. of Yokohama Specie Bank since '22; b. 1871 in Tokyo; grad. from Tokyo Higher Commercial School, '93; was Chief of one branch after another; Dir. of the head office and Manager of its Shanghai Branch, '14-22. Add. Koj. Fujimi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 10 ([scan leaf 583](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n583/mode/1up))

**Konoike, Zen-emon** Baron (cr. '11), head of one of the oldest millionaire families in Japan, dating 3 centuries back; runs the Konoike Bank of which he is president; is at the same time the largest shareholder of the Nippon Life Insurance Co., and the Osaka Savings Bank. Add. Osaka. — p. 10 ([scan leaf 583](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n583/mode/1up))

**Makino, Motojiro** Pres. of Fudo Sav. Bank; b. 1874 in Tokyo; grad. at Tokyo Higher Commercial School in '92 and entered business, founded Fudo Sav. Bank and became its President. Add. Azabu Nakano-cho, Tokyo. — p. 10 ([scan leaf 583](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n583/mode/1up))

**Majima, Otohiko** Mng.-Dir. of Mitsui Bank; b. 1871 in Nagano; studied at Peers' School and then Aoyama Gakuin; went to U.S.A. to prosecute his studies; on returning home he entered the 15th Bank, '94; then changed to Mitsui Bank, '98; sent to England by the Bank to study banking business; returned home, '02; again went to England, '05; Mng. of the Yokohama, and then Osaka Branch in '14. Add. Kamakura. — p. 10 ([scan leaf 583](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n583/mode/1up))

**Matsumoto, Ju-i** ex-Vice-Gov. of Japan Industrial Bank; b. 1873 in Fukushima-ken; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '98; passed Higher Civil Serv. Exam.; Comptroller of Taxes; Secy. of Finance Dept. and Section Chief of Tobacco Monopoly; Dir. of Taxation Bureau, Finance Dept., '22-23; Deputy-Gov. of Japan Industrial Bank, '23-27. Add. Sendagaya, Tokyo. — p. 11 ([scan leaf 584](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n584/mode/1up))

**Mogami, Kunizo** Dir. of Yokohama Specie Bank; b. 1875 in Hyogo-ken; grad. from Tokyo Higher Commercial Sch. in 1897 and entered the Yokohama Specie Bank; was its agent at New York, Dairen, Shanghai, London, Tientsin, etc.; was Manager of the Tokyo Branch till '22. Add. Yokohama Specie Bank. — p. 11 ([scan leaf 584](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n584/mode/1up))

**Mori, Hirozo** Dir. of Yasuda Bank, ex-Pres. of Bank of Formosa; b. 1873 in Tottori-ken; grad. from Tokyo Higher Commercial Sch. in '97; was London agent of Yokohama Specie Bank; then Manager of Kobe Branch and later Gen.-Mng. of the Head Office; appointed Vice-Pres. of the Taiwan (Formosa) Bank, '23 and next its President till '27. Add. Tokyo. — p. 11 ([scan leaf 584](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n584/mode/1up))

**Morimura, Ichizaemon** Baron (2nd), Pres. of Morimura Bank & Morimura Firm, Dir. of Yokohama Specie Bank, Chairman of Japan Overseas Trade Association, etc.; b. 1873 in Tokyo, 2nd son of the late Ichizaemon Morimura, pioneer Christian and businessman; studied at Keio and further in U.S.A. for years. Assumed his father's name in '28. Add. Shiba Takanawa-Minami-cho, Tokyo. — p. 11 ([scan leaf 584](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n584/mode/1up))

**Murai, Teinosuke** Ph. D. (Yale), Pres. of the former Murai Bank, Dir. of Japan Fertilizer Co., etc.; b. 1870 in Wakayama-ken; adopted by the Murai family. Add. Shimoshibuya, Tokyo. — p. 11 ([scan leaf 584](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n584/mode/1up))

**Murota, Yoshibumi** Lord-in-Waiting of the Kinkei Hall, Chair. of Board of Directors of Tokiwa Savings Bank, Pres. of Naikoku Savings Bank, & Nippon Military Service Ins. Co., Dir. of Dai-Nippon Artificial Fertilizer Co. & Aud. of Kanegafuchi Spinning Co.; b. 1847 in Mito City; early entered the Foreign Office, and was Minister to Mexico and Peru; retiring from the government service, entered business, being at the same time appointed Lord-in-Waiting of the Kinkei Hall. Add. Kamakura Zaimokuza, Kanagawa-ken. — p. 11 ([scan leaf 584](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n584/mode/1up))

**Nakagawa, Suekichi** M.A. (Yale), Mng.-Dir. of Furukawa Bank; b. 1874 in Shiga-ken; studied at Waseda which he left unfinished; further studied political economy at Yale; entered Furukawa Firm, '08 as Chief Accountant of Ashio Copper Mine; transferred to the Dept. Chief of the Firm. He is an expert in jujutsu. Add. Takanawa-Minamicho, Tokyo. — p. 11 ([scan leaf 584](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n584/mode/1up))

**Nishino, Gen** Mem. House of Peers, Pres. Fifteenth Bank; b. in Ibaraki; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '02; Dir. of Account Bureau of Finance Dept.; sent to London in '05 and again to Europe in '07; Vice-Min. of Finance, '23-24. Add. Ushigome Sadowaracho, Tokyo. — p. 11 ([scan leaf 584](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n584/mode/1up))

**Nishiwaki, Saisaburo** Pres. of Nishiwaki Bank, Dir. of several other banks & cos.; b. 1880 in Niigata of a millionaire family; studied at the Peers' School and Cambridge (class '08). Add. Ushigome Wakamatsu-cho, Tokyo. — p. 11 ([scan leaf 584](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n584/mode/1up))

**Nomura, Tokushichi** Dir. of Nomura Bank, Izuo Real Estate & Building Co., Fukushima Cotton Spinning Co.; b. 1878 in Osaka; grad. from Osaka Higher Commercial School in '93, and entered business. Published The Stock Year Book. Add. Bingo-machi, Higashi-ku, Osaka. — p. 11 ([scan leaf 584](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n584/mode/1up))

**Nonomura, Kingoro** former Dir. of Kawasaki Bank; Dir. of the Kojimachi Bank, etc.; b. 1869 in Oita-ken; grad. from Morioka Middle School and Tokyo English School; began his career as a journalist; later became Educational Adviser to the Korean Govt.; then Private Secretary of Denzaburo Fujita; Manager of Kitahama Bank; sectional chief of Industrial Bank of Japan; Dir. of South Manchuria Railway Co., Ltd., holding the last named post till '14. Add. Kami-ochiai, Tokyo. — p. 11 ([scan leaf 584](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n584/mode/1up))

**Odagiri, Masunosuke** Dir. of Yokohama Specie Bank & Superintendent of its Branches in China; b. 1865 in Tokyo; sent by the Foreign Office to Tientsin in '86 to study Chinese; was long in consular service in China, ultimately rising to Consul-General at Shanghai. Add. Shiba Shirokane Sanko-cho, Tokyo. — p. 12 ([scan leaf 585](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n585/mode/1up))

**Okamoto, Eitaro** Pres. of Cen. Bank for Coop. So. and ex-Vice-Min. Agr. and Com. Dept., '24; b. 1871 in Osaka-fu; edu. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '98; entered the Dept. of Agr. and Com. to be promoted to dir. of various bureaus; once sec. to Anglo-Japanese Fair, Hyde Park; a trip to Europe, '10. Add. Omori Araijuku, Tokyo. — p. 12 ([scan leaf 585](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n585/mode/1up))

**Saito, Tsutomu** Mng. of Nippon Chuya Bank, Dir. Japan Club; b. 1880 in Tokyo-fu; grad. from Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '07; and entering Yokohama Specie Bank served at the New York and then, London, branch; Manager of Japan Industrial Bank, '15. Add. Ushigome Kagacho, Tokyo. — p. 12 ([scan leaf 585](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n585/mode/1up))

**Samejima, Takenosuke** Mem. House of Peers since '96 and Aud. Bank of Japan; b. 1858 in Kagoshima; studied in U.S.A. while young and was appointed a teacher at the Foreign Language School on his return home; clerk to the Foreign Office about '82; Pers. Secy. to the Foreign Minister Count Okuma, next to the Premier, late Prince Ito, lastly Chief Secy. to the Cabinet. Add. Azabu Kogai-cho, Tokyo. — p. 12 ([scan leaf 585](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n585/mode/1up))

**Sasaki, Yunoshin** Pres. of Dai-ichi Bank, Tokyo Sav. Bank, Shibusawa Warehousing Co.; b. 1854 in Tokyo; entered Dai-ichi Bank (1st Bank) and became its Manager and then Director in '96, finally becoming its president; was once chairman of Tokyo Bankers Association. Add. Hongo Yumicho, Tokyo. — p. 12 ([scan leaf 585](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n585/mode/1up))

**Seshimo, Kiyoshi** Mng.-Dir. of Mitsubishi Bank since '17; b. 1874 in Nagano-ken; grad. from the Accountant's School formerly of Tokyo Higher Commercial School, '93; entered the Mitsubishi to become in time Sub-Manager and Manager of its branches at Osaka and Kobe; made a tour in Europe and America to inspect banking business, '03-10. Add. Takanawa-Kitamachi, Tokyo. — p. 12 ([scan leaf 585](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n585/mode/1up))

**Shimura, Gentaro** Mem. House of Peers, etc.; b. 1867 in Yamanashi; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '89 and entered Agr. and Com. Dept.; Counsellor, '93; Section Chief in '97; Vice-Gov. of Japan Hypothec Bank, '02-12; travelled through Europe and America for inspection of business, 99-01; Gov. of Japan Hypothec Bank, '12-21; Pres. of Central Bank for Cooperative Societies (founded '23), '23-29. Add. Koishikawa Kanatomi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 12 ([scan leaf 585](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n585/mode/1up))

**Suzuki, Shimakichi** Vice-Pres. of Franco-Japanese Bank; b. 186[illegible]; grad. from Keio, '89; entered Yokohama Specie Bank, '92; Sub-Manager of its New York Branch, '95; Manager of the Tientsin, Peking and Shanghai Branches in succession, before promotion to Vice-President; resigned the post in '22; Gov. of the Bank of Chosen, '25-28. Add. Tokyo. — p. 12 ([scan leaf 585](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n585/mode/1up))

**Takeuchi, Teizaburo** Dir. of Yasuda Bank; b. 1875 in Hokkaido; was long connected with the Higo Bank in which he rose to section chief and then Mng.-Dir. Add. Yasuda Bank, Tokyo. — p. 12 ([scan leaf 585](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n585/mode/1up))

**Tatsumi, Konojo** Dir. of Franco-Japanese Bank, Dir. of Yokohama Specie Bank; b. 1864 in Wakayama-ken; studied at Keio; early entered the service of the Bank; was for some time Manager of its London Branch. Add. Ushigome Haramachi, Tokyo. — p. 12 ([scan leaf 585](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n585/mode/1up))

**Wakao, Ikutaro** M.P. for Yokohama City, Pres. of Yokohama Wakao Bank, Yokohama Chamber of Commerce; a well-known silk merchant; b. 1884 in Yokohama; grad. from the Yokohama Commercial School. Add. Honcho 4-chome, Yokohama. — p. 12 ([scan leaf 585](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n585/mode/1up))

**Wakao, Kotaro** Pres. of Tokyo Commercial Bank, Pres. of Mitsubishi Commercial Co., Managing-Dir. of Mitsubishi Family Co., and Dir. of Katsuragawa Electrical Industry Co., Ltd.; b. 1896, e.s. of Shohachi Wakao. Add. Hiraoka, Shimo Osaki, Tokyo. — p. 13 ([scan leaf 586](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n586/mode/1up))

**Watanabe, Chifuyu** 2nd Visc., Minister of Justice; b. 1876; adopted as heir by his late uncle Viscount Kunitake Watanabe; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '98; further studied in France, Germany and America; Manager of Japan Industrial Bank for some years; Mng.-Director of Nippon Steel Works and Hokkaido Colliery & Steamship Co.; was for a while proprietor and editor of a Tokyo daily. Mem. House of Peers since '20; present post in '29. Add. Azabu Nishimachi, Tokyo. — p. 13 ([scan leaf 586](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n586/mode/1up))

**Watanabe, Toshijiro** Dir. of Watanabe Bank, Yokohama Canvas Co., etc.; b. 1882, son of Fukusaburo Watanabe, Pres. of the Bank and Dir. of the Yokohama Specie Bank, Yokohama Elec. Co., etc.; grad. at Economics, Tokyo Imp. Univ., 14; toured in U.S.A.,, '15. Add. Nishitobe, Yokohama. — p. 13 ([scan leaf 586](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n586/mode/1up))

**Yamaguchi, Seitaro** Mng.-Dir. of Yamaguchi Bank, Tokyo, since '17; b. 1885 in Niigata-ken; edu. at Harvard, class '10; Mng.-Dir. of Hokuetsu Hydro-Electric Co. Add. Nihombashi Honcho, Tokyo. — p. 13 ([scan leaf 586](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n586/mode/1up))

**Yamamuro, Sobun** Dir. and Mng. of Osaka Branch of Mitsubishi Bank; b. 1880 in Kumamoto-ken; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '07; studied in England and America for three years till 1917; entered the Mitsubishi Bank on return home; manager of its New York Branch, '19; manager of its London Branch, '20-23; the present post since '23. Add. Muko-gun, Mikage-machi, Hyogo-ken. — p. 13 ([scan leaf 586](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n586/mode/1up))

**Yanagiya, Usaburo** Paym. Lieut. (ret.), Adviser to Yasuda Bank, ex-Vice-Gov. of Japan, Hypothec Bank; b. 1867 in Nagasaki-ken; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '90; entered the Bank of Japan immediately after; travelled Europe in '96; Chief of the Issue Dept., etc.; resigned, '13; Dir. and later Vice-Gov. of Japan Hypothec Bank, '13-27. Add. Harajuku, Tokyo. — p. 13 ([scan leaf 586](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n586/mode/1up))

**Yashiro, Norihiko** Dir. of Sumitomo Bank, Sumitomo Trust Co. and Sumitomo Warehousing Co.; b. 1872 in Kagoshima-ken; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '96; chief of London branch of Nihon Yusen Kaisha. Manager of Sumitomo Bank for years, and then present position. Add. Sumiyoshi-machi, near Kobe. — p. 13 ([scan leaf 586](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n586/mode/1up))

**Yukawa, Kankichi** Mng.-Dir. of Sumitomo Bank; b. 1868 in Wakayama-ken; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '90; Secretary to Dept. of Communications and next to Formosan Govt.-Gen.; Principal of the Post and Telegraph School; represented Japan at the International Postal Conference held at Washington, '96; Dir. of the Communication Superintending Bureau; resigned office, '05 and entered Sumitomo Firm. Add. Sumitomo Bank, Osaka. — p. 13 ([scan leaf 586](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n586/mode/1up))

**Yuki, Toyotaro** ex-Mng.-Dir. of Yasuda Holding Co. & Vice-Pres. of Yasuda Bank; b. 1877 in Yamagata-ken; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '03; entering the service of the Bank of Japan was promoted to the Manager of Osaka Branch; studied banking in U.S.A., '06-09; later entered the Yasuda Bank and was Mng.-Dir. of Yasuda Holding Co. and also Vice-Pres. of the Bank, '22-29. Add. Tokyo. — p. 13 ([scan leaf 586](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n586/mode/1up))

### Civil

**Abe, Hisanori** Mem. House of Peers; b. 1879 in Yamaguchi-ken; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '05. After passing higher civil serv. exam. served at both provincial and Central Govts. as Secretary; Secy. and Counsellor to the Home Office and the Legislative Bureau; Gov. of Tottori; then Dept. Chief of the former Census Board; Dir. of the Statistics Bureau; Dept. Chief of the Patent Bureau; Perm. Vice-Min. of Agr. & For. Dept., '25-29; present post in '29. Add. Ikebukuro, near Tokyo. — p. 14 ([scan leaf 587](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n587/mode/1up))

**Adachi, Mine-ichiro** D.L. (Japan), Amb. to France; b. 1869 in Yamagata-ken; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ. in '92; attache in Rome, '93; transferred to Paris, '97; attached to the head office, '03; was on the suite of Peace Envoy to Portsmouth in '05; Senior Counsellor at head office before transfer as Emb. Counsellor to Paris; Minister to Mexico, '13-16; Ambassador to Belgium, '17-27; present post since '27. Add. Jap. Emb., Paris. — p. 14 ([scan leaf 587](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n587/mode/1up))

**Aioi, Yoshitaro** Mem. of Dairen City Assembly, Chairman of Dairen Chamber of Commerce, etc.; b. 1865 in Fukuoka-ken; grad. from the Tokyo Higher Commercial School, '96, and entered the Mitsui Mining Co., and then the Mitsui Bussan Kaisha; later transferred to South Manchuria Railway Co., which he left and started business in Dairen. Add. Kirishima-cho, Dairen City. — p. 14 ([scan leaf 587](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n587/mode/1up))

**Aka-ike, Jo** Crown Mem. House of Peers, Metropolitan Police Superintendent at the time of the Kiyoura Cabinet; b. 1878 in Nagano-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Law, class '07, and further studied at the Univ. Hall; passing the civil service examination held secretaryship at one province and another; Secy. of the Home Office; Gov. of Shizuoka; Bureau Dir. of Home Dept. in the Korean Govt.-General, '23-24. Add. Sendagaya Harajuku, Tokyo. — p. 14 ([scan leaf 587](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n587/mode/1up))

**Akatsuka, Shosuke** ex-Min. in Austria; b. 1872 in Kagoshima; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Law, '98; passed diplomatic service examination, '98; Eleve-Consul at Fusan; Secy. at Berlin and then Washington; Consul at Manila; Secy. in Foreign Office, '90; Consul-General at Mukden, '12-22; Minister to Austria & Hungary, '24-26. Add. Tokyo. — p. 14 ([scan leaf 587](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n587/mode/1up))

**Akita, Kiyoshi** M.P., barrister; b. 1881 in Tokushima; studied law in a private Univ. and was appointed Judge; ex-Prop. of the Niroku (daily paper); Parl. Vice-Min. of Communications, '27-29. Add. Hongo Fujimaye, Tokyo. — p. 14 ([scan leaf 587](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n587/mode/1up))

**Akiyama, Masanosuke** D.L., ex-Dir. of Civil Administration Bureau, Tsingtao ('17-22); b. 1866 in Hiroshima-ken; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '86, to be connected afterward with civil affairs section of War and Navy; Councillor of Korean Govt.-Gen., '13-17. Add. Ushigome Yakuoji Tokyo. — p. 14 ([scan leaf 587](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n587/mode/1up))

**Akiyama, Shohachi** ex-Dir. of Engineering Bureau, Dept. of Railways; b. 1877 in Hiroshima; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., in Mech. Eng.; entered the service of the State Railway as engineer and was despatched abroad for studying his speciality. Add. Ushigome Benten-cho, Tokyo. — p. 14 ([scan leaf 587](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n587/mode/1up))

**Amaoka, Naoyoshi** ex-Dir. of Postal Savings Bureau, Communication Dept.; b. 1880; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ. and passed Higher Civil Service Exam. in '07. Add. Azabu Hommura-cho, Tokyo. — p. 14 ([scan leaf 587](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n587/mode/1up))

**Ando, Masazumi** M.P., formerly journalist; b. 1876 in Tokyo; edu. at Toyo and Waseda Univs. and Tokyo Foreign Language Sch.; was on editorial staff of the Tokyo Asahi as its managing editor, also connected with the Osaka Asahi and other journals in Tokyo and Osaka; was special correspondent of the two Asahis in the Washington conference; Parl. Counsellor of Education Dept., '27-29. Add. Asakusa Matsuba-cho, Tokyo. — p. 14 ([scan leaf 587](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n587/mode/1up))

**Anraku, Kanemichi** ex-Supt. of the Metropolitan Police, Mem. House of Peers; b. 1850 in Kagoshima; was connected with the police service from about 1875; next a local Governor; resigned the post in '08 and entered commercial circles; Pres. of Chiyoda Gas Co. till '11; reappointed Met. Police Supt., '11-13; and for 3rd time in '13-14. Add. Nishiokubo, Tokyo. — p. 15 ([scan leaf 588](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n588/mode/1up))

**Aoki, Arata** Minister to Mexico; b. 1881 in Kumamoto; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Politics and passed the Dip. and Con. Serv. Exam. the following year; Eleve-Consul at Chicago, '09 and then New York; at London, '11; Consul at Suchow, '13; Sec. at Head Office, '16; 3rd Emb. Sec. in Russia, '16; 2nd Sec., '17; Sec. at Head Office in '18 with additional duty as Per. Sec. to the Minister; Sectional Chief, '19-22; 1st Emb. Sec. at Washington, '23; Emb. Coun. in Brazil same year; Con.-Gen. at Honolulu, '24-25; Min. in Mexico, '26. Add. Tokyo. — p. 15 ([scan leaf 588](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n588/mode/1up))

**Aoki, Shuzo** Crown Mem. House of Peers; Perm. Vice-Min. of Railways; b. 1875 in Yamaguchi; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Law, class '02 and passed the higher civil serv. exam. two years after; was long in the service of the State Railways in which he ultimately rose to be Dir. of the Finance Bureau; resigned and became Managing-Dir. of the Chikuho Electric Railway Co. in '20; Deputy-Mayor of Yokohama in '22 and in the terrible disaster there in '23 he lost two children; was Perm. Vice-Min. of Railway Dept., '24-26; again in '29. Add. Nogata-machi, Tokyo. — p. 15 ([scan leaf 588](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n588/mode/1up))

**Arima, Sumibumi** Visc., Sub-Lieut. of Cavalry (ret.); b. 1868 of a former daimyo's family at Maruoka, Echizen; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Agriculture, '97; Chamberlain to the Crown Prince, '00-10. Add. Yotsuya Ukyo-machi, Tokyo. — p. 15 ([scan leaf 588](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n588/mode/1up))

**Ariyoshi, Akira** Ambassador in Brazil since '27; b. 1876 in Kyoto; edu. at the Tokyo Higher Commercial School, class '98; passed the Dip. serv. exam., '98; Consul at Fusan, and then Newchwang; later Secretary of Japanese Embassy at Paris; Con.-Gen. at Shanghai, '09-19; Min. in Berne, '20-26. Add. Rio de Janeiro. — p. 15 ([scan leaf 588](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n588/mode/1up))

**Ariyoshi, Chuichi** Mayor of Yokohama since '25; b. 1870 in Tango; edu. in Law at Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '96; Sec. Home Office till '08; Governor of Chiba-ken, '08-12; Dir. of Gen. Affairs, Korean Res.-Gen. in '10; Gov. of Miyazaki, '11-15; Kanagawa, '15-18, Hyogo-ken, '18-22; Dir.-Gen. of Pol. Affairs, Korean Govt.-Gen., '22-24. Add. Yokohama City Office. — p. 15 ([scan leaf 588](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n588/mode/1up))

**Asada, Tokunori** Mem. House of Peers; b. 1848 in Kyoto; Counsellor to Japanese Legation in Washington, '74; Counsellor of Foreign Office, 79; Dir. of Commercial Bureau, '86; Governor of Nagano and next of Kanagawa, '99; Vice-Min. for Foreign Affairs, '00; that of Communications, which he resigned in '02. Add. Koj. Shimo-Rokubancho, Tokyo. — p. 15 ([scan leaf 588](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n588/mode/1up))

**Ashida, Hitoshi** 1st Secretary of the Embassy in Constantinople since '26; b. 1887 in Kyoto-fu; passed Dip. & Con. Serv. Exam. in '11, and grad in the following year from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ.; Attache in '12 and next 3rd Emb. Sec., '17, in Russia; in France, '18-20; 2nd Emb. Sec., '20; junior commissioner at the various international conferences held at Paris and Geneva; Sec. at the Head Office and Sectional Chief at the Intelligence Dept., '23-25. [Publications; European Diplomacy after Paris Conference, 1923; My Impressions in Russia, 1924; The Powers and Situation of their Home Politics, 1924]. Add. Jap. Embassy, Constantinople. — p. 15 ([scan leaf 588](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n588/mode/1up))

**Awaya, Ken** ex-Perm. Vice-Min. of Education; b. 1883 in Yamaguchi-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Law, class '07; after passing the higher civ. serv. exam. was for a time secretary of Ibaraki Prefectural Office; transferred to the Education Dept. where he was Dir. of Special Education Bureau till Apr. '27; Perm. Vice-Min. of Edu., '27-29. Add. Akasaka Tango-cho, Tokyo. — p. 15 ([scan leaf 588](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n588/mode/1up))

**Baba, Genji** ex-Judge of Court of Cassation; b. 1860; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '85; was appointed first a junior judge in Tokyo Court and Judge of Supreme Court; resigned and began practice '24. Add. Mita Tsunamachi, Tokyo. — p. 16 ([scan leaf 589](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n589/mode/1up))

**Debuchi, Katsuji** Ambassador to Washington since '28; b. 1878 in Iwate-ken; edu. at Tokyo Higher Com. School and Post-Graduate course. class '02, and passed the Dip. and Con. Service exam. the same year; 3rd Emb. Sec. at Berlin, '07-10; 2nd Emb. Sec., '10; Sec. Chief at the head office, '11; 1st Sec. at Peking, '14; at Washington, '18; Counsellor at Berlin, '20; attended as assist. the 2nd League of Nations congress and the Washington Conference; on the Shantung Settlement Com., '22 Emb. Counsellor and then Vice-Min. of Foreign Affairs, '24-28. Add. Japanese Emb., Washington. — p. 16 ([scan leaf 589](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n589/mode/1up))

**Den, Akira** ex-Perm. Vice-Min. Finance Dept. ('24-27); b. 1878; grad. at Politics, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '04; passed higher civil serv. exam.; Sec. of Tobacco Monopoly Bureau, and of the Dept.; stayed in New York as Finance Commissioner; Dir. of Account Bureau before promotion. Add. Yoyogi, Tokyo. — p. 16 ([scan leaf 589](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n589/mode/1up))

**Egi, Senshi** Privy Councillor since '24 and Lord-in-Waiting of the Kinkei Hall; b. 1853, studied Law at Tokyo Imp. Univ. and was in service of Education Dept., '72-77; Dir. of Local Adm. Bureau (Home Office), '90-97; Governor of Ibaraki, Aichi, Hiroshima and Kumamoto till '06 when he resigned. Add. Ushigome Yocho-machi, Tokyo. — p. 16 ([scan leaf 589](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n589/mode/1up))

**Funatsu, Shin-ichiro** former Con.-Gen. at Mukden; b. 1873 in Saga-ken; edu. at the Shoin-gakusha (Saga), and passed the “Foreign student exam.” of the Foreign Office, '94; was Consul-General at Tientsin and then at Mukden, '24-26. Represents Japanese Cotton-mill interest in China. Add. Shanghai, China. — p. 16 ([scan leaf 589](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n589/mode/1up))

**Furuya, Shigetsuna** ex-Min. in Argentina; b. 1876 in Ehime-ken; edu. at Doshisha (Kyoto) and Michigan; passed Dip. & Con. serv. exam. in '02; 3rd Emb. Sec. at London, '08; 2nd Emb. Sec., '10; Sectional chief at the head office, '16; Con.-Gen. at Ottawa, '18; Emb. Counsellor at Washington, '20; Minister to Mexico, '22; Min. to Argentina, Paraguay & Uruguay, '26-28. Add. Buenos Aires. — p. 16 ([scan leaf 589](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n589/mode/1up))

**Futakami, Hyoji** Crown Mem. House of Peers (nom. '24), Chief Secretary of the Privy Council; b. 1878 in Toyama pref.; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '04 and passed the Higher Civil Serv. exam. the same year; was long connected with the Department of Communications as jun. secretary, Counsellor, etc. before transferring to the Privy Council. Add. Shibuya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 16 ([scan leaf 589](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n589/mode/1up))

**Goto, Fumio** ex-Dir. of General Affairs, Formosan Govt.-Gen.; b. 1884 in Oita. Edu. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '08; Sec. or Chief Police Inspector in the provinces till '13; transferred to the Police Bureau, Home Office, the same year, first as sectional chief and then its Director; Dir. Gen. Affairs in Taiwan Gov.-Gen., '24-28. Add. Tokyo. — p. 16 ([scan leaf 589](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n589/mode/1up))

**Hamada, Tsunenosuke** Governor of Kyoto since '26; b. 1871 in Kochi-ken; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '96; served as Counsellor or Chief Police Inspector in the provinces till '07; Gov. of Toyama, '07; of Miyagi till '20; after some years on the retired list was Dir. of Colonial Affairs Bureau, '25-26. Add. Kyoto Pref. Office. — p. 16 ([scan leaf 589](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n589/mode/1up))

**Hanihara, Masanao** ex-Ambassador to Washington; b. 1876 in Yamanashi-ken; grad. from Waseda, '97; attache at Seoul Legation, '99; at Washington, '01; Secy. at the same, '02; then Sec. Chief at Foreign Office; Consul-Gen. at San Francisco, '16-17; Dir. Political Affairs, '17-19; was among the four Delegates to the Washington Conference, '21-22; Vice-Min. of Foreign Affairs, '19-22; Amb. to Washington, '23-24. Add. Tokyo. — p. 16 ([scan leaf 589](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n589/mode/1up))

**Hiranuma, Ki-ichiro** Baron (cr. '26), D.L., Vice-Pres. of Privy Council, Pres. Nippon Univ.; b. 1865 in Okayama-ken; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '88; appointed to the Bench, having filled chair of sectional chief and Procurator in the Tokyo Appeal Court, '95; Bureau Director, 06-11, Vice-Min. of Justice, '11-12; Procurator-Gen. till '21; then Pres. of the Supreme Court; Privy Councillor, '24. Add. Nishi-Okubo, Tokyo. — p. 17 ([scan leaf 590](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n590/mode/1up))

**Hata, Toyosuke** M.P., ex-Parl. Vice-Min. of Navy; b. 1872 in Tokyo; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '96; Governor of various localities; Parl. Vice-Min. of Communications and then of Navy, '24-25. Add. Tsukiji, Tokyo. — p. 17 ([scan leaf 590](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n590/mode/1up))

**Hatta, Yoshiaki** Mem. House of Peers (nom. '29); b. in Tokyo; grad. from Civil Eng., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '08; entered the service of the Govt. Railways, of which he had been Dir. of Construction Bureau till '26; Perm. Vice-Min. of Railways, '27-29. Add. Nishi Sugamo, Tokyo. — p. 17 ([scan leaf 590](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n590/mode/1up))

**Hayashi, Gonsuke** Baron (cr. '07), Ex-Ambassador to the Court of St. James ('20-25); b. 1860 at Aizu; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ. and entered Foreign Office, '85; Consul and Secretary of the Legation, 87-09; Dir. of Commercial Bureau, '98, Minister at Seoul, '99; at Peking, '06-08 and again in '16-18; Ambassador to Rome, '08-16; Gov. of Kwantung Leased Territory, '19-20; Ambassador in England, '20, but returning home in Sept. '25, resigned the post; accompanied Prince Chichibu to England in 1925 as a member of the suite, returning home with the Prince in Jan. '27. Add. Tokyo. — p. 17 ([scan leaf 590](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n590/mode/1up))

**Hayashi, Raizaburo** D.L., formerly Perm. Vice-Minister of Justice ('24-27); b. 1878; grad. from Chuo Univ., '97; Judge till '21; Dir. Bureau of Criminal Affairs. Add. Koj. Yoban-cho, Tokyo. — p. 17 ([scan leaf 590](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n590/mode/1up))

**Hirota, Koki** Minister to the Hague since '27; b. 1878 in Fukuoka; edu. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '05; passed Dip. Serv. Exam., '06; attached the same year to the Chinese Legation; to England, '09; Sectional Chief of the Commercial Bureau, '14; 1st Emb. Sec. at Washington, '18; Sectional Chief of the Intelligence Bureau, '21; Dir. Euro-American Bureau, Foreign Office, '24-27. Add. Japanese Legation, Hague. — p. 17 ([scan leaf 590](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n590/mode/1up))

**Honda, Kumataro** ex-Ambassador to Germany; b. 1874 in Wakayama-ken; edu. at Tokyo Hogaku-in in Law but left unfinished, '94; passed the Foreign Office Clerks Exam., '05 and Dip. & Con. Serv. Exam., '98; Attache in China and next Belgium, '99; Per. Secy. to the Foreign Minister, '01; Member of the Peace Envoy suite to America; 2nd Sec. of Embassy at London, '97; Con.-Gen. at Harbin, '12; Emb. Counsellor at London, '14; Minister to Switzerland, '18; Minister to Austria, '22; Ambassador in '23 and ordered to proceed to Turkey; accredited to Germany, '23-26; resigned in '26. Add. Tokyo. — p. 17 ([scan leaf 590](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n590/mode/1up))

**Hori, Yoshikata** Counsellor at the Legation in Peking since 1926; b. 1885 in Kagoshima; edu. at Tokyo Higher Com. School and its post-graduate course, class '08; passed the Dip. & Con. Serv. Exam. the same year; was in the consular service at New York, '11; Vancouver, '13; Canton, '16; Pers. Secy. to Foreign Minister, then 1st Legation Secy. in China, '17-21; commissioner of political affairs to the Siberian Expedition, '22; Con.-Gen. in London, '23-25. Add. Japanese Legation, Peking. — p. 17 ([scan leaf 590](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n590/mode/1up))

**Horiguchi, Kumaichi** ex-Minister to Brazil; b. 1865 in Niigata-ken; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '93; passed Dip. Serv. Exam. and Eleve-Con. '94; Legation Secretary in Brazil, '99-06; Sweden, '08-09; Mexico, '09-13; and Madrid, '13-18; Min. to Brazil, '18-24; placed on retired list, '25. Add. Tokyo. — p. 17 ([scan leaf 590](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n590/mode/1up))

**Horikiri, Zenjiro** Mayor of Tokyo (elected 1929); b. 1884 in Fukushima Pref.; younger brother of Zembei Horikiri; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '09, and on graduation, entered Home Affairs Dept.; after holding the posts of Appraiser, Secretary, Chairman of Town Planning Committee, etc.; was Director of Reconstruction Bureau, '26-29. Add. Koishikawa Takata Oimatsucho, Tokyo. — p. 18 ([scan leaf 591](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n591/mode/1up))

**Ichiki, Kitokuro** D.L., Minister of Imp. Household; b. 1867 at Shizuoka, studied constitution, administrative politics, etc. in Germany, '90-93; Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., '94; Mem. of House of Peers, '00; Vice-Minister of Home Affairs, '08-11; Chief of Bureau of Legislation, '12-13; Minister of Education and next of Home Affairs, '16-18; Privy Councillor, '17-25; present post in '25. Add. Hongo Akebono-cho, Tokyo. — p. 18 ([scan leaf 591](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n591/mode/1up))

**Ichiki, Otohiko** ex-Mayor of Tokyo ('28-29); b. 1872 in Kagoshima; graduated at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '96; Sec. to Formosan Government, and to Finance Dept.; Chief of Accountant Bureau of the Dept.; Vice-Minister of Finance, '16-18; Minister of Finance in Kato Cabinet, '22; Gov. of Bank of Japan, '23-27. Add. Ushigome Shin-Ogawa-machi, Tokyo. — p. 18 ([scan leaf 591](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n591/mode/1up))

**Ikeda, Hiroshi** ex-Gov. of Kanagawa ('26-29); b. 1881 in Shizuoka; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Law, class '95; passed the Higher Civil Serv. Exam. the following year; secy. and chief police commissioner at Nara, Kanagawa, etc.; next transferred to the Home Office; Deputy Mayor of Tokyo for some years; Dir. of Social Bureau till '25; Governor of Kyoto, '25-26. Add. Kanagawa Pref. Office. — p. 18 ([scan leaf 591](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n591/mode/1up))

**Ikeda, Torajiro** D.L., Dir. of Civil Affairs Bureau, Justice Dept.; b. 1879 in Saga-ken; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., English Law, class '03, and has since been in the service of Justice Dept. as Judge, Pub. Procurator, or Counsellor of the Dept.; went abroad twice on inspection tour; is also Lecturer at his alma mater. Add. Justice Dept., Tokyo. — p. 18 ([scan leaf 591](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n591/mode/1up))

**Inouye, Katsunosuke** 2nd Marquis (suc. '15), Privy Councillor; b. 1860 in Choshu, an adopted son and heir of the late Marquis Inouye (d. '15) who was his uncle; was in the service of Bank of Japan before he got appointment at Foreign Office in '85; Legation secretary in Berlin, '86; secretary and counsellor at the head office, '92; Minister to Germany, '98-07; Envoy to Chile in '10; Ambassador to St. James' Court, '13-16; Grand Master of Ceremonies and Privy Councillor, '28. Add. Azabu Uchidayama, Tokyo. — p. 18 ([scan leaf 591](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n591/mode/1up))

**Inouye, Kyoshiro** 2nd Viscount, D. Eng., Mem. House of Peers; b. in Kumamoto, adopted son of late Viscount Ki Inouye; edu. in Mining at Tokyo Imp. Univ., '97; further studied abroad, '98-04; Prof. at Osaka Higher Tech. School and Kyoto Imp. Univ.; then transferred to Tokyo Imp. Univ.; Dir. of the South Man. Rly. Co. till '22; Minister of Railways, '26-27. Add. Shimo Osaki, Tokyo. — p. 18 ([scan leaf 591](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n591/mode/1up))

**Iriye, Kan-ichi** Dir. of Board of Audit, Imp. Household; b. 1872; brother of Visc. M. Nomura; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ.; Dir. Pension Bureau till '23; Chief Secy. to the Lord-Keeper of Privy Seals, '23-28. Add. Totsuka, Tokyo. — p. 18 ([scan leaf 591](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n591/mode/1up))

**Iriye, Tamemori** Visc., Manager of the Household of H.I.M. the Emperor Dowager, Mem. House of Peers, Chief of Poetry Bureau, Imp. Household (since '14); b. 1868 in Kyoto; was on the suite of the Crown Prince when he visited Europe in '20; Assist. Chamberlain to the Prince Regent till '26; is noted for his Japanese poetry. Add. Ushigome Yocho-machi, Tokyo. — p. 18 ([scan leaf 591](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n591/mode/1up))

**Ishiguro, Tadanori** Visc. (cr. Baron '95), Surg.-Gen.
