(ret.); Privy Councillor since '20; b. 1845 in Niigata-ken; Chief Surgeon of the Osaka Military Hospital on the occasion of the Civil War of Kagoshima, '77; Surgeon-Col., next year, and Surgeon-Gen. in '90; retired in '96; since that time has devoted himself to Red Cross work, of which he is a chief promoter and represented it in the international conference held in Germany in 1890; nom. Member of House of Peers in '02; resigned in '20; Pres. of the Japan Red Cross, '20-22. Add. Ushigome Ageba-cho, Tokyo. — p. 18 ([scan leaf 591](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n591/mode/1up))

**Ishiwara, Kenzo** Privy Councillor (nom. '27); b. 1864 in Okayama; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '80 and entered Justice Dept.; app. judge in '91 which he left the following year to be counsellor to local govts. and Home Office; governor of Yamanashi, Kochi, Hokkaido, etc., lastly of Kanagawa; transferred to the Imperial Household Dept.; Vice-Min. of Imp. Household, '20-22. Add. Shimo Osaki, near Tokyo. — p. 19 ([scan leaf 592](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n592/mode/1up))

**Ito, Hirokuni** 2nd Prince, Grand Master of Ceremonies since '26; b. 1870, 4th son of Gorosaburo Inouye of the former Choshu clan, and was adopted as son and heir by the late Prince Ito; studied in Germany; Deputy Grand Master of Ceremonies, '16; Dir. of Bureau of Imp. Mews, '21-26. Add. Omori, near Tokyo. — p. 19 ([scan leaf 592](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n592/mode/1up))

**Kamiyama, Mitsunoshin** ex-Gov.-Gen. of Taiwan, Mem. House of Peers; b. 1870 in Yamaguchi; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ; was successively Secretary and Judge of the Administrative Litigation Court; Dir. Forestry Bureau, Dept. Agr. & Com.; Gov. of Kumamoto, '12-13; Vice-Minister of Agr. & Com., '14-18; Gov.-Gen. of Taiwan, '26-28. Add. Tokyo. — p. 19 ([scan leaf 592](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n592/mode/1up))

**Kasama, Akio** b. 1886; grad. at Politics, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '09; was in Govt. railway service till '17; then changed to Foreign Office; was member of the Delegation to Peace Conf., '18-21; Technical Delegate to the International Conf. on Transit & Communication at Barcelona, '21; Chief Secy. (European & American Section) of Intelligence Bureau, For. Office, '23; Acting High Commissioner and then Dip. Representative at Constantinople, '23; Charge d'Affaires at Bucharest, '25; Emb. Counsellor in Paris, '26; Chief of Permanent Delegation to the International Labor Organization at Geneva, '27-29; Govt. delegate to Int. Labor Conf., '27 & '28; Govt. delegate to 2nd Int. Conf. on Immigration at Havana, '28; attached to the Foreign Office since his return from Geneva, '29. Add. Foreign Office, Tokyo. — p. 19 ([scan leaf 592](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n592/mode/1up))

**Kawai, Hiroyuki** Emb. Counsellor in Paris; b. 1883 at Odawara; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., Law, class '08 and passed Dip. & Con. serv. exam. the same year; Eleve-consul and next Attache in France, '09-13; transferred to Russia, '13; 3rd Leg. Sec. in Sweden, '14; 2nd Leg. Sec., '17; on the staff of the Peace Envoy, '20; Sectional Chief at the Head Office, '21-22; Emb. Counsellor in Brussels, '23-26; present post since '27. Add. Paris. — p. 19 ([scan leaf 592](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n592/mode/1up))

**Kawashima, Nobutaro** Minister to Greece since '27; b. 1880 in Kyoto-fu; edu. at Tokyo Higher Com. School and post-graduate course, class '07, and after passing the Dip. & Con. serv. exam. was in the service of the Foreign Office; later appointed 3rd Emb. Secretary and then 2nd Legation Secretary; Con.-Gen. at Hamburg, '25-27. Add. Athens. — p. 19 ([scan leaf 592](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n592/mode/1up))

**Komura, Kin-ichi** Marquis (2nd), Vice-Min. of Overseas Affairs Dept.; b. 1882, eldest son of the late Marquis Jutaro Komura; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '07; passed the Dip. Serv. Exam. the same year; attache in China and next at London, '08-10; Secy. at Head Office, '11 and in time appointed Sectional Chief of Political Bureau; Deputy-Dir. of Intelligence Bureau, '23-27; Director of same, '27-29; present post in June '29. Add. Koj. Fujimicho, Tokyo. — p. 19 ([scan leaf 592](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n592/mode/1up))

**Kondo, Motoki** D. Eng., Vice-Adm. (ret.), ex-Inspector Gen. of Naval Construction; b. 1864 in Tokyo; is Director of the Kogyokusha (private school) founded by his father who did much in the cause of naval education. Received Hon. Prize from Imperial Academy of Japan, '13. Add. Ebara-machi, Tokyo. — p. 19 ([scan leaf 592](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n592/mode/1up))

**Konoye, Fumimaro** Prince, one of the proud Fujiwara houses and a "gossekke" (see Ichijo) and was founded by Motozane (1143-66 A.D.). The present head grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '17, and is Member of House of Peers. Add. Shibuya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 20 ([scan leaf 593](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n593/mode/1up))

**Kubota, Seitaro** Pres. of Adm. Litigation Court since '22; b. 1864 in Okayama; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '91; Sec. at local and the Home Office till '94; sent abroad, '98-99 and was transferred to be Secretary of Agr. and Com. to draw up draft of factory law; Dir. Sanitary Affairs Bureau, '03-10 and then Judge of Adm., Lit. Court, etc. Add. Akasaka Reinanzaka, Tokyo. — p. 20 ([scan leaf 593](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n593/mode/1up))

**Kubota, Yuzuru** Baron (cr. '07), Privy Councillor; b. 1847 in Hyogo-ken; entered the civil service in connection with education in which he became Vice-Minister in '94 and finally its chief in '05; Mem. House of Peers, '94-17; present post since '17. Add. Koishikawa Kanatomicho, Tokyo. — p. 20 ([scan leaf 593](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n593/mode/1up))

**Kuratomi, Yuzaburo** Baron (cr. '26), D.C.L., President of the Privy Council since '26; b. 1853 in Fukuoka-ken; studied at now defunct Law Coll. of Justice Dept., '89, and was connected with the Dept. till he was transferred to Korea as Vice-Min. of Justice; Chief of Compilation Com. of the Japanese Criminal Code; Dir. of the Bureau of Legislation, '13-14; nominated Mem. House of Peers; Chief Auditor, Imp. Household Dept. and Privy Councillor, '21. Add. Akasaka Tango-cho, Tokyo. — p. 20 ([scan leaf 593](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n593/mode/1up))

**Kuki, Takakazu** Baron (cr. '05), Privy Councillor; b. 1852 in Settsu; was for several years a high official in the Dept. of Education; Minister at Washington, '84-88; Director of the Imperial Museum at Nara in '90; Exhibition Commissioner, etc. Is a great virtuoso of Japanese painting. Add. Nagatacho, Tokyo. — p. 20 ([scan leaf 593](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n593/mode/1up))

**Kurino, Shin-ichiro** Viscount, ex-Amb. to France; b. 1852 at Fukuoka; Commissioner of the Foreign Office, '81; Junior Secretary, '84; Vice-Chief of the Investigation Bureau, '86; next transferred to the Communication Dept. where he acted as Per. Sec. to the Minister, later becoming Dir. of the Foreign Correspondence Bureau; returned to the Foreign Office in '91 as Dir. of the Investigation Bureau and then that of the Political Bureau; commenced his diplomatic career, first as Minister at Washington, '94, at Rome, '96, at Paris' 97, and at Petrograd, '01, in the stormy days preceding the outbreak of the Russo-Japanese War; appointed Ambassador to France, '06. Add. Akasaka Hinoki-cho, Tokyo. — p. 20 ([scan leaf 593](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n593/mode/1up))

**Kuroda, Nagashige** Marquis, formerly Daimyo of Fukuoka, Privy Councillor, Lord-in-Waiting of Jako Hall; b. 1867 at Fukuoka; studied at Cambridge Univ., '84-89; Master of Ceremonies, '89-90; Vice-Pres. of the House of Peers, '96-24; is President of the Oriental Association. Add. Akasaka Fukuyoshi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 20 ([scan leaf 593](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n593/mode/1up))

**Kurose, Hiroshi** Mayor of Kobe; b. 1883 in Kumamoto-ken; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '09; passed higher civil serv. exam. in '09, chief of Takanawa police station, administrative inspector of Hyogo pref., and then of Yamaguchi, Governor of Yamanashi pref., etc. Add. Yamamoto-cho, Kobe. — p. 20 ([scan leaf 593](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n593/mode/1up))

**Kuwayama, Tetsuo** Mem. House of Peers (nom. '29); b. 1881; studied German Law at Tokyo Imp. Univ. and passed Higher Civil Serv. Exam.; Dir. Petty Insurance Bureau, '22-24; Perm. Vice-Min. of Communications, '24-29. Add. Araijuku Omosawa, near Tokyo. — p. 20 ([scan leaf 593](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n593/mode/1up))

**Machida, Chuji** M.P., Minister of Agr. & Forestry; Pres. of Hochi Shimbun, Dir. of 110th Bank and Kyoto Fire Ins. Co., etc.; b. 1863 in Akita-ken; grad. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. and further studied in Europe and America; Parl. Under-Secy. of Agr. & Com. Dept., '15-17; once connected with the Bank of Japan; is a leader of the Minsei Party; Min. of Agr. and For., '25-27 and again in '29. Add. Ushigome Minami-Enoki-cho, Tokyo. — p. 20 ([scan leaf 593](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n593/mode/1up))

**Maida, Narakichi** b. 1875; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '01; passed Higher Civil serv. exam. and entered the service of the Dept. of Communications, in which he was Dir. of Communications Bureau, '24-25. Add. Koishikawa Takeshima-cho, Tokyo. — p. 21 ([scan leaf 594](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n594/mode/1up))

**Makino, Nobuaki** Count (cr. Baron in '07, Visc. in '18), Lord-Keeper of Privy Seal; b. 1851, second son of the late Toshimitsu Okubo; Priv. Secy. to the then Premier Count Ito (late Prince), '88; a local Governor, '91-92; Vice-Minister of Education, 93; Minister at Rome, '97; then at Vienna; Min. of Education, '06; Privy Councillor in '09; Min. of Agr. & Com., '11-12; Min. of Foreign Affairs, '13-14; represented Japan at Versailles, '18; Min. of Imp. Household, '21-25; present post in '25. Add. Komaba, near Tokyo. — p. 21 ([scan leaf 594](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n594/mode/1up))

**Matsuda, Do-ichi** D.L., Ambassador at Rome; b. 1876 in Tokyo; edu. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., passed Higher Civil Service Exam. and Juridical Serv. Exam., '01-02; jun. Procurator, '02; Foreign Office Counsellor, '02; Attache, '03; 3rd Leg. Secy. at Brussels, '07; Secy. at head office, '12; Sect. Chief of Political Bureau, '13; Emb. Counsellor at Rome, '17; on service at head office, '18; Dir. Com. Bureau, '18; Dir. Treaty Bureau, '19; Emb. Counsellor and Dir. of League of Nations Office in Paris, '20-26; present post since '26. Add. Japanese Embassy, Rome. — p. 21 ([scan leaf 594](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n594/mode/1up))

**Matsudaira, Tsuneo** Ambassador at London since 1929; b. 1877 in Tokyo; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ. (Economics), '02; passed Dip. Serv. Exam.; Legation and then Embassy Sec. at Peking, London, Paris, etc.; Con.-Gen. at Tientsin; was in charge of diplomatic affairs of the Siberian Expedition, '18-19; Dir. of Europe & American Bureau, For. Office; Japanese representative in the Russo-Japanese conf. at Changchung, '22; Vice-Min., '24-25; Ambassador at Washington, '25-28; present post in '29. Add. Japanese Emb., London. — p. 21 ([scan leaf 594](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n594/mode/1up))

**Matsui, Keishiro** Baron (cr. '20), ex-Ambassador at London ('25-28), Crown Mem. House of Peers, Min. of Foreign Affairs in the Kiyoura Cabinet; b. 1868 at Osaka; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '89; Secy. at Seoul, '90-93; Secy. at Washington, '94; at London, '97; at Peking, '02; Counsellor in Paris, '06; transferred to Washington in '08 and then Vice-Min. of Foreign Affairs, '13; a Peace Envoy while Ambassador at Paris, '14-20, and was created Baron in recognition of his service during the War. Add. Tokyo. — p. 21 ([scan leaf 594](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n594/mode/1up))

**Matsunaga, Naokichi** Dir. of Treaty Bureau, Foreign Office; b. 1883 in Saga-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Politics, '08 and passed the Dip. & Con. Serv. Exam. the same year; Eleve-Con. and then Attache in China, '08-10; Consul at Hamburg, '10; 3rd Emb. Secy. at Berlin, '14; recalled in '14 and made acting Chief of Secretariat, '16; Con. at Seattle the same year; Sec. and Sectional Chief at Head Office, '20-23; Con.-Gen. in Ottawa, '23-27; Emb. Counsellor at Brussels, '27-28; present post in '28. Add. Foreign Office. — p. 21 ([scan leaf 594](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n594/mode/1up))

**Matsushima, Hajime** Minister to Poland; b. 1883 in Nagano-ken; grad. from Politics, Tokyo Imp. Univ. and passed Dip. Service Exam., '07; Eleve-Con.; 3rd Sec. Petrograd Embassy, recalled home, '16; Per Sec. of For. Minister and Counsellor to the Office, '17; Sectional Chief of the same, '18; Consul-Gen. at Moscow, Harbin, etc.; represented Japan in the Russo-Japanese Conference at Dairen, '21; Emb. Counsellor at Paris, '24-27; present post since '27. Add. Jap. Legation, Warsaw. — p. 21 ([scan leaf 594](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n594/mode/1up))

**Mayeda, Tamon** Government Delegate to the International Labour Conference; b. in Tokyo; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '09; was once Private Secretary of the Home Minister and later Deputy-Mayor of Tokyo. Add. Nakano-machi, near Tokyo. — p. 22 ([scan leaf 595](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n595/mode/1up))

**Mayeda, Toshisada** Visc., Mem. House of Peers; Lieut. Infantry (ret.); b. 1874 in Echigo; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '03; took part in the Russo-Japanese War ('04-05); Minister of Communications, '22 and next Min. of Railways in the Kiyoura Cabinet. Is also Pres. of Chuo Life Ins. Co., and Dir. of various concerns. Add. Nishi-Okubo, Tokyo. — p. 22 ([scan leaf 595](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n595/mode/1up))

**Miyao, Shunji** ex-Governor of Hokkaido; b. in Niigata-ken; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '68; Sec. of the Finance Dept.; then transferred to Formosan Govt.; Vice-Pres. of the Colonization Bureau, '10; Gov. of Aichi-ken, '21; Gov. of Oriental Development Co., '24; Gov. of Hokkaido, '22-23. Add. Ushigome Yarai, Tokyo. — p. 22 ([scan leaf 595](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n595/mode/1up))

**Miyauchi, Kunitaro** ex-Dir. of Mining Bureau; b. 1876 in Ibaraki-ken; grad. from the Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ. and entered the Dept. of Agr. & Com., '03; passed Higher Civil Serv. Exam.; Secretary to the Patent Bureau and then Private Secy. to the Min. of Agr. & Com.; Dir. of Mining Bureau, '23-28. Add. Koi. Sasugaya-cho, Tokyo. — p. 22 ([scan leaf 595](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n595/mode/1up))

**Miyata, Mitsuo** Crown Mem. House of Peers, ex-Superintendent of Metropolitan Police ('27-29); b. 1878 in Miye-ken; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '05 and passed Higher Civil Serv. Exam.; Secy. to the House of Peers, and Prefectural Gov. till '22; Chief Secretary of Cabinet in Kato (Adm.) Ministry. Add. Shibuya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 22 ([scan leaf 595](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n595/mode/1up))

**Mizumachi, Kesaroku** Pres. of Board of Audit since '24; b. 1863 in Saga; grad. Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '89; Junior Councillor of the Treasury, 91; despatched to Europe to investigate financial and economic situation in '98; filled chair of civil code at the alma mater as add. post till '01; Dir. Finance Bureau, '04; Vice-Min. of Finance in '06; Govt. Financial Agent at London, '06-11; Vice-Gov. of Bank of Japan, '12-19. Add. Koj. Nagata-cho, Tokyo. — p. 22 ([scan leaf 595](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n595/mode/1up))

**Monobe, Nagaho** D. Eng., Engineer to the Home Office, Dir. of the Civil Engineering Experiment Station (Home Office), Prof. of Hydrodynamics at Tokyo Imp. Univ., Mem. of Seismological Research Institute and also of the Scientific Research Council, etc.; b. 1886 in Akita-ken; grad. in Civil Eng., Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '11; has devoted himself to river work since then and has designed improvement plan for a number of unruly rivers in the Main Island, besides supervising the technical side of bridge-work and investigating earthquake-proof work with special reference to civil engineering. Has written a number of theses on the theory of stability of embankments, earthquake-proof engineering works, etc. Was awarded the Imp. Academy prize in '25 Add. Home Office. — p. 22 ([scan leaf 595](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n595/mode/1up))

**Mori, Kengo** Crown Mem. House of Peers (nom. '27), former Govt. Financial Agent at London, Paris and Washington; b. 1876 in Saga-ken; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '00; secretary of the Financial Dept., '00; the London Agent post, '13-26. Add. Koj. Shimo-Nibancho, Tokyo. — p. 22 ([scan leaf 595](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n595/mode/1up))

**Mori, Yasusaburo** Minister to Chile and Bolivia; b. 1880 in Fukuoka-ken; edu. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. in Law, class '07 and passed the Dip. & Con. Serv. Exam. next year; Eleve-Con. at Amoy, '09; at Honolulu, '10; Consul at Lima, '12; at Antung, '16; Con.-Gen. at Tsinan, '19 with additional duty as Sec. to the Administration Dept. of the Tsingtao Army of Occupation; later transferred to Tsingtao; Emb. Counsellor at Rome, '23-27; present post Dec. '27. Add. Japanese Legation, Chile. — p. 22 ([scan leaf 595](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n595/mode/1up))

**Moroi, Rokuro** ex-Minister to Argentina; b. 1872 in Saitama-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Law, class '96 and passed the Higher Civil Serv. Exam. the same year and the Dip. Serv. Exam. in '99; Eleve-Con. at Shanghai, London, etc.; Con., '01; Sec. at the head office, '06; 1st Emb. Sec. to Berlin, '12; recalled home on the outbreak of the War and was in charge of the Correspondence Section; Con.-Gen. at Honolulu, '16; Min. to Argentina, '24-26. Add. Tokyo. — p. 22 ([scan leaf 595](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n595/mode/1up))

**Motoji, Niikuma** D.C.L., Dir. Prison Affair Bureau, Justice Dept.; b. 1876 in Kagoshima-ken; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '02; was Public Procurator of the Court of Appeal, and Judge of the Court of Cassation before his appointment to the present post. Add. Shibuya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 23 ([scan leaf 596](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n596/mode/1up))

**Murakami, Ryukichi** ex-Dir. Fishery Bureau, Dept. Agr. & For.; b. 1877; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '02; passed Higher Civil Serv. Exam.; Prof. of the Fishery Institute and Dir. Patent Bureau. Add. Koi. Myoga-dani, Tokyo. — p. 23 ([scan leaf 596](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n596/mode/1up))

**Mushakoji, Kimitomo** Visc., Minister to Rumania; b. in 1882, ninth descendant of an old courtier house; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Law, class '07 and passed the Diplomatic Serv. Exam.; Secretary, Sectional Chief, and Emb. Counsellor at Brussels and then at Berlin in succession; present post since '25. Add. Bucharest. — p. 23 ([scan leaf 596](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n596/mode/1up))

**Nagai, Matsuzo** Ambassador in Belgium & Minister in Luxemburg; b. 1877 in Aichi-ken; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., '02; passed Dip. & Cons. Serv. Exam.; Eleve-Consul at Tientsin, New York and Washington, '03-07; Consul-Gen. at San Francisco, '08-12; Chief of Telegraph Section (For. Office), '16; Emb. Counsellor in London, '17-21; Dir. Commercial Bureau (For. Office), '21-23; Minister to Sweden, Norway, Denmark and Finland, '23-28; present post since '28. Add. Japanese Embassy, Brussels. — p. 23 ([scan leaf 596](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n596/mode/1up))

**Nagaoka, Haruichi** D.L., Ambassador in Berlin and Minister in Latvia; b. 1877 in Tokyo; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ. in '00 and also from a French Coll. in '04; passed Higher Civil Serv. Exam. and entered Foreign Office; Emb. Secretary at Paris, etc.; Min. to Czecho-Slovakia, '21; Minister to Holland, '24; Dir. of Treaty Bureau, For. Office, '25-26; present post since '26. Add. Japanese Embassy, Berlin. — p. 23 ([scan leaf 596](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n596/mode/1up))

**Nagaoka, Ryuichiro** ex-Dir. Social Affairs Bureau, Home Office; b. in Tokyo; grad. from Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., in '08; was Secretary of the Home Dept. and then Dir. of Civil Eng. Bureau; Director of Social Affairs Bureau, '26-29; Chief of Metropolitan Police Board, in June '29, but retired in a few days after appointment owing to the cabinet change; visited Europe and America on official mission, '27. Add. Shiba Shirokane, Tokyo. — p. 23 ([scan leaf 596](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n596/mode/1up))

**Nambu, Koshin** Mem. House of Peers, Court Counsellor; b. 1864 in Kyoto, adopted by Baron Kameo Nambu; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ. in '90; Secretary of the Home Office, '91; once Dir. Forestry Bureau of the Imp. Household; Dir. of Public Works Bureau, in '07. Add. Azabu Hommura-cho, Tokyo. — p. 23 ([scan leaf 596](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n596/mode/1up))

**Obata, Torikichi** Amb. to Turkey since '25; b. 1873 in Ishikawa-ken; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '97; passed dip. serv. exam., 98 and served at Tientsin, Singapore, Vienna, London, etc.; Secy at Peking, '05; Consul at Chefoo and Tientsin; Counsellor at Peking Legation and then Dir. of Political Bureau, Foreign Office, '16-18; Minister to China, '18-22. Add. Japanese Embassy, Turkey. — p. 23 ([scan leaf 596](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n596/mode/1up))

**Ohara, Senkichi** 2nd Baron, ex-Dir. of Construction Bureau, Imp. Household; b. 1871; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '96; Sec. of House of Peers; was Dir. of the Bureau of Supplies, Imp. Household before transfer to the present post. Add. Koj. Naka-Rokuban-cho, Tokyo. — p. 23 ([scan leaf 596](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n596/mode/1up))

**Ohno, Morie** Minister to Austria & Hungary; b. 1878 at Tokuyama; edu. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '04; passed the Dip. & Con. Serv. Exam., '07; Eleve-Consul in China, '07-10; Attache in Germany, '10, and 3rd Emb. Sec., '11; in Austria, '12-14; 2nd Emb. Sec. in Italy, '16-17; Sec. Chief at the Head Office, '17-19; Con.-Gen. at Hamburg, '19-23; 1st Emb. Sec. and then Coun. in Germany, '23; Counsellor of the Legation in Vienna, '26 and the present post '27. Add. Vienna. — p. 23 ([scan leaf 596](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n596/mode/1up))

**Okano, Noboru** Dr. Eng., ex-Vice-Min. of Railways; b. 1876, brother to Kejiro Okano; edu. in Eng. at Imp. Tokyo Univ. and entered the service of Govt. Rlys. in which he was an Engineer and then Dir. of Way and Works; next Vice-Min. of Railways, '24-25. Add. Jujo, near Tokyo. — p. 24 ([scan leaf 597](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n597/mode/1up))

**Okuyama, Seiji** ex-Minister to Greece, Secy. of League of Nations Ass'n. (Tokyo); b. 1876 in Yamagata-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., in Law, class '03; passed the Dip. & Con. Serv. Exam. soon after and entered the Foreign Office first as eleve-consul, then 3rd Legation Sec., Counsellor at the head office; Minister at Athens, '25-26. Add. League of Nations Ass'n., Tokyo. — p. 24 ([scan leaf 597](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n597/mode/1up))

**Ota, Masahiro** Governor of Kwantung since Aug. '29; b. 1870 in Yamagata-ken; grad. from Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '96; was Chief of the 1st Dept. of the Metropolitan Police Board; Governor of Fukushima, Ishikawa, Kumamoto, Niigata and Aichi Prefectures successively; Chief of Metropolitan Police, '24-27. Add. Kwantung Govt.-General. — p. 24 ([scan leaf 597](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n597/mode/1up))

**Ota, Tamekichi** Minister to Spain & Portugal; b. 1880 in Tottori-ken; passed Higher Civil Serv. Exam. and entered Foreign Office, '03; passed Dip. Serv. Exam. and Eleve-Con., '07; Secretary to the Kwantung Govt. Consul at various places; Legation Secretary and Charge d'Affaires at Mexico, then Con.-Gen. at San Francisco, '18-20; Con.-Gen. at Ottawa, '21-23; Legation Counsellor in Peking, '23-26; present post '27. Add. Japanese Legation, Madrid. — p. 24 ([scan leaf 597](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n597/mode/1up))

**Otori, Fujitaro** 2nd Baron, Mem. House of Peers; b. 1866 in Tokyo, eldest son of the late Baron Keisuke Otori; grad. at Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '90; in service of Formosan Govt. until 1901, when he passed Dip. & Con. Serv. Exam.; Attache to Legation at Seoul same year; Secy. of Legation at The Hague, '03, and Brussels, '05; Peace Commissioner at The Hague, '07; Con.-Gen. at Vladivostok, '09; Secy. Foreign Office, '12; Embassy Counsellor at Rome, '14; at Paris, '16-19; Min. to Mexico, '19-20. Add. Ushigome Haramachi 3-chome, Tokyo. — p. 24 ([scan leaf 597](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n597/mode/1up))

**Oyama, Atsushi** ex-M.P., lawyer, Lord-in-Waiting of Kinkei Hall; b. 1865 in Nagoya; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '89; in juridical service either as Judge or Public Procurator till '04; went abroad for purpose of study, returning home in '06; reinstalled in the former service; Dir. Prison Bureau, '07-12; Vice-Min. of Justice, '12-14. Began practice in '16. Add. Koishikawa Haramachi, Tokyo. — p. 24 ([scan leaf 597](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n597/mode/1up))

**Rinoie, Ryusuke** Mayor of Shimonoseki since '20, ex-Gov. of Nagasaki-ken; b. 1866 at Yamaguchi; grad. Tokyo Imp. Univ., '90; held junior post in the Cabinet; then Home Dept.; Councillor and Secretary, and Gov. of various prefectures till '17. Add. Shimonoseki. — p. 24 ([scan leaf 597](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n597/mode/1up))

**Saburi, Sadao** Embassy Counsellor in London; b. 1879 in Tokyo; brother of Dr. Isshi Saburi; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '05; after passing the Dip. & Con. Serv. Exam. was appointed Attache in China, '05; in Russia, '06; then in France; at Head Office, '08; Sec. in France, 12; at Head Office, '18; Emb. Sec. and then Counsellor at Washington till '24; Dir. of Commercial Bureau, '26; Dir. of Treaty Bureau, '27; attended the Chinese Tariff Revision Conference, '26-27; present post in '72. Add. Japanese Embassy, London. — p. 24 ([scan leaf 597](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n597/mode/1up))

**Saionji, Hachiro** Chief of Imperial Mews; b. 1881 of the Mori family, a former Daimyo, and was adopted as heir by Marquis (now Prince) Saionji; was once Private Secretary to his father-in-law when Premier; also accompanied him as Peace Envoy to Paris, '18; was on the Crown Prince's suite when he visited Europe in '21; Vice-Grand Master of Ceremonies, '24-27. Add. Azabu Iigura-katamachi, Tokyo. — p. 24 ([scan leaf 597](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n597/mode/1up))

**Saito, Hiroshi** Chief of Intelligence Bureau (Foreign Office); b. 1886 in Tokyo; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '10 and passed the Dip. and Con. Exam. and also the Higher Civil Serv. Exam. same year; Attache in America, '10; 3rd Emb. Sec., '15 in England, and appointed Assis. to the Peace Envoy, '19; 2nd Dec. '19; Con. at Seattle, '21; Con.-Gen. in New York, '23-28; Deputy Chief of Intelligence Bureau in '28 and then its Chief in '29; attended as assistant commissioner international conferences in Europe and America. Add. Foreign Office. — p. 25 ([scan leaf 598](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n598/mode/1up))

**Sakenobu, Nobumichi** ex-Minister to Chile; b. 1878 in Akita-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Law, class '04 and soon passed the Dip. & Con. Serv. Exam.; Eleve-Con. at Seoul, Hongkong, etc.; Counsellor at the head office, '08; 3rd Emb. Sec. at Berlin, '10; 2nd Sec., '12; Con-Gen. at Calcutta, '17; Emb. Counsellor in Russia, '21; Chief of Personnel Affairs at the head office, '21; Minister to Chile, '24-28. Add. Foreign Office, Tokyo. — p. 25 ([scan leaf 598](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n598/mode/1up))

**Sakikawa, Saishiro** Dir. of Patent Bureau; b. 1869; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '96; '17-18 entered Dept. Agr. and Com. in which he held the post of Dir. Mining Bureau till '23; present post since '23. Add. Osaki, near Tokyo. — p. 25 ([scan leaf 598](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n598/mode/1up))

**Sato, Aimaro** Court Councillor and Intendant to Prince Fushimi; b. 1857 at Hirosaki; grad. at an American Univ., '81; Chief of the Telegraph Section, Foreign Office, the same year; Sec. at Washington, '87; transferred to London, '91; Section Chief, '93; Sec. at Paris, '96 and next at Berlin; Min. to Mexico, '00; recalled home in '02; on the suite of the Peace Plen. at Portsmouth, '05; Minister to Holland, '06; attended 2nd Peace Conference at the Hague, '07 and Opium Conference at the Hague, '11; transferred to Austro-Hungarian Embassy with additional post of Swiss Legation, in '14; Ambassador to U.S.A., '16-18; present post in '20. Add. Azabu Toriizaka, Tokyo. — p. 25 ([scan leaf 598](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n598/mode/1up))

**Sato, Naotake** Chief of the Imp. League of Nations Office in Geneva; b. 1882 in Okinawa; adopted by Aimaro Sato in 1903; grad. from Tokyo Higher Commercial School and passed Dip. Serv. Exam., '04; Attache, '05; Consul-Gen. in Harbin till '21; Emb. Sec. at Paris, '21-23; Minister to Poland, '23-26. Add. Geneva. — p. 25 ([scan leaf 598](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n598/mode/1up))

**Sawada, Setsuzo** Emb. Counsellor in Washington; b. 1884 in Tottori-ken; passed Dip. Serv. Exam. while a student at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; Sec. to Embassy at London, '17-21; ordered to join the Crown Prince's suite when he visited Europe in '21; Chief of Secretariat at Foreign Office, '22-25; present post in '26. Add. Japanese Emb., Washington. — p. 25 ([scan leaf 598](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n598/mode/1up))

**Seki, Hajime** D.C.L., Mayor of Osaka; b. 1872 in Tokyo; grad. from Tokyo Higher Com. Sch., '93; further studied in Europe, '98-01, communications being his speciality; was Prof. of the Tokyo Higher Com. School and Inspector of the Department of Education; Deputy-Mayor of Osaka, '14; then Mayor in '24. Add. Osaka. — p. 25 ([scan leaf 598](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n598/mode/1up))

**Sekiya, Teizaburo** Vice-Min. of Imp. Household; b. 1875 in Tochigi-ken; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '99; passed Higher Civil Serv. Exam..and entered the Home Office as Secretary; Secretary to Kwantung Govt. and local Govts. at home; Dir. of the Korean Education Dept., '10; Gov. of Shizuoka prefecture, '19-21. Add. Imp. Household Dept. — p. 25 ([scan leaf 598](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n598/mode/1up))

**Shijo, Takahide** (Baron), former Vice-Min. of Com. & Ind. Dept. ('25-29); b. of an ancient family in 1876; grad. in Politics, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '04; entered Dept. of Com. & Agr. in which he successively held post of Secretary, Sectional Chief and finally Dir. of Industrial Bureau. Add. Dept. of Com. & Ind. — p. 25 ([scan leaf 598](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n598/mode/1up))

**Shimizu, Cho** D.C.L., Judge of Adm. Litigation Court; b. 1868 in Kanagawa; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., '94; Secretary of the Home Office and Prof. at the Peers' School and studied in Germany and Austria, '98-99. Add. Yotsuya Aizumicho, Tokyo. — p. 26 ([scan leaf 599](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n599/mode/1up))

**Shimojo, Yasumaro** Dir. of Decoration Board; b. 1885 in Tokyo; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., Law, class '10; served as jun. sec. of the provincial and central government such as chief of the Archives Sec., Cabinet; then Dir. of the Pension Bureau; of the Statistical Bureau, '24-29; present post in '29.. Add. Koj. Shimo-Rokubancho, Tokyo. — p. 26 ([scan leaf 599](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n599/mode/1up))

**Shiratori, Toshio** Secy. of Foreign Office & Sect. Chief of Intelligence Bureau; b. 1887 in Chiba-ken; passed Dip. & Con. Serv. Exam., '13; grad. in Economics, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '19; Eleve-Consul first at Mukden and then Hongkong; attache at Washington, '16; Sec., '18; Sec. at the For. Office, '22-25; Emb. Secretary in Berlin, '26-28; present post in '29. Add. Foreign Office. — p. 26 ([scan leaf 599](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n599/mode/1up))

**Sugimura, Yotaro** Deputy Chief of the League of Nations Office in Geneva, Minister unattached; b. 1884 in Tokyo; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Politics, class '08 and passed the Dip. & Con. Serv. Exam. the same year; Eleve-Con. at Lyons, '08; Attache in France, '12; 3rd Emb. Sec., '14; 3rd Leg. Sec. in China; 2nd Sec., '17; recalled in '19 and appointed Mng. of the Temporary Treaty Revision Committee; Sec. and Coun. and Sectional Chief, the same year; Assis. to the Imp. Envoy to the Washington Conference, '21 and to the Imp.-Delegate to the War-time Law Revision Commission at the Hague, '22; 1st Emb. Sec. in Paris; then Jr. Mem. in the 4th Congress of the League of Nations at Geneva; has also attended similar such international conferences held at Geneva; Deputy-Chief of the Imp. League of Nations Office at Paris and Embassy Counsellor there, '23-27. Add. Geneva, Switzerland. — p. 26 ([scan leaf 599](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n599/mode/1up))

**Takebe, Kin-ichi** Dir. Common Edu. Bureau; b. 1881; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '08; passed Higher Civil Serv. Exam.; Secretary and Section Chief of the Dept. till '22; Dir. Technical Edu. Bureau, '22-27. Add. Nishi-katamachi, Tokyo. — p. 26 ([scan leaf 599](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n599/mode/1up))

**Tanaka, Tokichi** Amb. to U.S.S.R.; b. 1878 in Kyoto-fu; studied at Tokyo Higher Commercial Sch.; Consul at Singapore, Hongkong, Seattle, etc., '03-10; Emb. Counsellor at Washington; Dir. Trade Bureau, '22; Vice-Min. of For. Affairs and Director of Intelligence Bureau, '22-23; present post since '25. Add. Moscow. — p. 26 ([scan leaf 599](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n599/mode/1up))

**Tatsuki, Shichita** Ambassador unattached; b. 1867 in Yamaguchi-ken; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ. and passed Dip. Serv. Exam., '96; Eleve-Consul, Legation Attache and Embassy Sec., successively; Secretary and Section Chief of the For. Office; Emb. Counsellor in Petrograd and then Paris, '09; Min. to Chili, '17; Minister to Netherlands, '20-23; Amb. to Brazil, '23-27. Add. Foreign Office. — p. 26 ([scan leaf 599](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n599/mode/1up))

**Tawara, Mago-ichi** M.P., Minister of Com. & Ind., and a leader of the Minsei Party; b. 1869 in Shimane-ken; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '95; has since been in the civil service, first in Korea and then in the provinces, rising to the post of Gov. of Miye in '12, Miyagi in '14, and Hokkaido, '15-20; Parl. Vice-Min. of Rlys. and Fin., '24-27; present post in '29. Add. Akasaka Shinsaka-machi, Tokyo. — p. 26 ([scan leaf 599](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n599/mode/1up))

**Tokugawa, Kuninori** Marquis, head of the clan of Mito, one of the three Houses of Tokugawa founded by Yorifusa, 11th son of Iycyasu. The Marquis was b. 1886, and is now Vice-Pres. of the Red Cross Society. Add. Honjo Komme, Tokyo. — p. 26 ([scan leaf 599](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n599/mode/1up))

**Tokugawa, Satotaka** Count (cr. '94), of the Tayasu branch of the Tokugawa; b. 1865 in Tokyo; toured in Europe and America, '89-90; nominated Mem. of the House of Peers in '93; Grand Chamberlain to H.I.M. the Emperor, '23-26. Add. Mita, Tokyo. — p. 27 ([scan leaf 600](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n600/mode/1up))

**Tomi-i, Masa-akira** Baron (cr. '26); D.C.L. Privy Councillor; Emeritus Prof. Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1858 at Kyoto; grad. from Lyons Univ. (France), '83; Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., '85; was once Dean of the Law College. Add. Ichigaya Yakoji-machi, Tokyo. — p. 27 ([scan leaf 600](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n600/mode/1up))

**Tsuchiya, Masanao** 2nd Visc., Master of Ceremonies and Chamberlain to the Emperor; b. 1881 of a former Daimyo family; grad. in Literature, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '08; was on the suite of the Crown Prince when he visited Europe in '21. Add. Aoyama-Onden, Tokyo. — p. 27 ([scan leaf 600](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n600/mode/1up))

**Tsukamoto, Kiyoharu** formerly Vice-Minister of Home Affairs; b. 1872 in Hyogo-ken; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '02. After passing the Higher Civil Service examination he was given subordinate posts at the local and central Government as junior Sec. at Fukui, Kanagawa, Kyoto, etc.; then Dir. of Shrines Bureau; Dir. of Legislative Bureau in '24; Perm. Vice-Min. of Home Affairs, '24-26. Add. Akasaka Shinsaka, Tokyo. — p. 27 ([scan leaf 600](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n600/mode/1up))

**Tsurumi, Sakio** ex-Vice-Min. of Agr. and Com.; b. 1873 in Toyama-ken; grad. in Politics, Tokyo Imp. Univ. and entered the Home Office, '99; served in the local Govt. before he was transferred to the Dept. of Agr. & Com., '08; Dir. of the Aquatic Product Bureau, '17; Dir. of the Commercial Bureau, '20-24; twice dispatched abroad; Vice-Minister of the Dept. in the Kiyoura Cabinet; represented Japan in the Economic Conference of the Allies in Paris during the Great War. Add. Aoyama Takagi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 27 ([scan leaf 600](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n600/mode/1up))

**Uchida, Kakichi** Mem. House of Peers; b. 1866 in Tokyo; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '91; entered the Dept. of Communications, in which he held post of Dir. of Shipping Bureau till '10; Dir. of Civil Affairs, Formosa, '11-15; Vice-Min. of Communications, '17-18; Gov.-Gen. of Formosa, '23-24. Add. Omori, near Tokyo. — p. 27 ([scan leaf 600](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n600/mode/1up))

**Uchida, Sadatsuchi** a retired diplomat; b. 1865 at Kokura; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ.; attache in '89; Eleve-Consul at Shanghai in '90; to Seoul in '93; Consul-General in '02; Min. and Con.-General in Brazil in '07; Min. to Sweden, '11-18; Minister Resident in Turkey, '20-23; placed on retired list in '23. Represented Japan in the International League as Senior Commissioner. Add. Shibuya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 27 ([scan leaf 600](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n600/mode/1up))

**Uchida, Yasuya** Count (Baron '07, Visc. '11), ex-Privy Councillor; b. 1865 in Kumamoto-ken; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ.; attache at Washington, '87; Per. Sec. to the Min. of Agr. & Com. and at the Foreign Office; Legation Sec. at London, '93; at Peking, '93-98; Dir. of Political Bureau; Vice-Min. of Foreign Affairs, '00; Minister to Peking, '01-06; Amb. at Vienna, and next at Washington, '09; Minister of Foreign Affairs in '12; again the same portfolio in the Hara and Kato (Adm.) Cabinets; was Ambassador to Petrograd during the Great War; Privy Councillor, '24-29. Countess Uchida was educated at Vassar College, U.S.A. Add. Nishi Okubo, near Tokyo. — p. 27 ([scan leaf 600](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n600/mode/1up))

**Usami, Katsuo** Pres. National Resources Invest. Board; b. 1869 in Yamagata-ken; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '96; Counsellor to various local Governments and then to Home Office; Gov. of Toyama-ken, '08; Chief of Home Affairs Dept., Korea, '10; Governor of Tokyo, '21-25; Pres. of Decoration Bureau, '25-27. Add. Official residence, Shiba Park, Tokyo. — p. 27 ([scan leaf 600](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n600/mode/1up))

**Ushio, Keinosuke** Vice-Min. of Home Affairs; b. 1881; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '05; passed Higher Civil Serv. Exam. and entered Home Office; Dir. Sanitation Bureau till '22; Dir. Local Adm. Bureau, '23-28; present post in '28. Add. Ushigome Yakuoji, Tokyo. — p. 27 ([scan leaf 600](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n600/mode/1up))

**Wakamiya, Sadao** M.P., ex-Vice-Min. of Communications; b. 1875 in Aichi-ken; studied Law at Tokyo Imp. Univ. and entered the Govt. service; Dir. Mercantile Marine Bureau, Communication Dept., during the Great War; Vice-Min. of the Dept., '21-24. Add. Akasaka Omote-cho, Tokyo. — p. 28 ([scan leaf 601](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n601/mode/1up))

**Wakasugi, Kaname** B.A., M.A. (New York Univ.); 1st Secretary to Japanese Emb. (London); b. 1883 at Kumamoto; grad. from the Toa Dobun School in Shanghai, '06; entered the service of the Foreign Office the same year; held junior consular post at various places; passed the Dip. and Con. Service Examination, '17; Consul, '20; Emb. Secretary at Shanghai, Paris, London, and Los Angeles; Secy. of Foreign Office & Sectional Chief of Intelligence Bureau, '25-29; present post '29. Add. Japanese Emb., London. — p. 28 ([scan leaf 601](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n601/mode/1up))

**Yada, Shichitaro** Con.-Gen. at Shanghai; b. 1875 in Shizuoka-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Politics, class '06; Eleve-Con. in China, '07; 3rd Emb. Sec. at Rome, '12; to the head office, '13; 3rd Emb. Sec. at London, '16; Consul at Mukden, '15; Con.-Gen. at London, '19; at San Francisco, '20; present post in '23. Add. Japanese Consul-Gen., Shanghai. — p. 28 ([scan leaf 601](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n601/mode/1up))

**Yamada, Junjiro** Dir. of Sanitation Bureau, Home Office since '22; b. 1883; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '08; passed Higher Civil Serv. Exam. and has since been in the service of Home Office; Dir. of Shrine Bureau, '19-22. Add. Koishikawa Hayashi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 28 ([scan leaf 601](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n601/mode/1up))

**Yamanouchi, Ichiji** Crown Mem. House of Peers (nom. '14) and Lord-in-Waiting of the Kinkei Hall; b. 1865 in Kagoshima; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ. in '88; Secretary and next Section Chief of the Police Bureau, Home Office; Governor of Aomori, '02-04; Dir. of R'ly Bureau, '04-06; Vice-Pres. of the Imp. R'lys., and then Director of R'ly Adm. Dept., '09-12; Governor of Hokkaido, '12; Chief Secy. of the Cabinet in Yamamoto Ministry; Min. of Railways, '23-24. Add. Azabu Fujimi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 28 ([scan leaf 601](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n601/mode/1up))

**Yamaoka, Mannosuke** D.C.L., ex-Dir. Police Bureau (Home Office); b. 1876; grad. from the Nippon Law Coll., '99; passed Judicial Serv. Exam. and long served as judge or procurator; in the meantime sent to Germany for study; Director of Prison Bureau, '25-26; Dir. of Police Bureau, '27-28. Add. Shibuya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 28 ([scan leaf 601](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n601/mode/1up))

**Yamasaki, Keiichi** Minister Plen. & Extr. Unattached; b. 1882 in Kanagawa-ken; grad. from the Special Course of Tokyo Higher Commercial School and passed Dip. Service Exam., '05; Eleve-Con. same year; Consul, Embassy Sec. and then Consul-Gen. in London and in Shanghai; Consul-Gen. in Honolulu, '21-24; Minister in Peru, '25-28. Add. Foreign Office. — p. 28 ([scan leaf 601](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n601/mode/1up))

**Yanagisawa, Yasutoshi** Count, Mem. House of Peers, Mem. International Statistical Society, Hon. Mem. of Royal Statistic Society (England), etc.; b. 1870 in Niigata-ken; grad. at the Peers' College, '94; further studied in Berlin, Vienna, Strasburg, Brussels and Paris, studying statistics; returned home in '00; attended the International Statistical Conference six times as representative of Japan; Pres. of Yanagisawa Statistical Investigation Office, founded by him. Add. Shiba Tamachi, Tokyo. — p. 28 ([scan leaf 601](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n601/mode/1up))

**Yatabe, Yasukichi** Minister to Siam; b. 1882 in Yamaguchi; grad. from special course, Tokyo Higher Com. School, '07; attache in Siam, '08; Consul at Antung, Suchow, Sydney, etc., Secy. of Formosan Gov.-Gen., '17; Sec. of Foreign Office, '19; attended the Seamen's Labor Conference held at Rome, '20 and '21; 1st Sec. at Rome the same year; Emb. Sec. at Washington, '24; then Counsellor of Foreign Office and Sectional Chief of Intelligence Bureau; Consul-Gen. at Tsingtao, '27-28; present post since '28. Add. Bangkok. — p. 28 ([scan leaf 601](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n601/mode/1up))

**Yoshida, Isaburo** Minister in Switzerland; b. 1878 in Kyoto-fu; grad. at German Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '03; passed Dip. & Con. Serv. Exam., '04 and appointed Eleve-Consul at Hongkong the same year; diplomatic attache at Washington, '04; 3rd Embassy Sec. at Washington, '08; on service at the For. Office, '11; 2nd Embassy Sec. in London, '12; 1st Embassy Sec., '16; Mem. Peace Treaty Committee, '20; Emb. Counsellor and transferred to Peking, '21; Mem. Japan-China joint committee for the settlement of Shantung question and so forth; Emb. Counsellor in Washington, '24-25 and then in London, '25-27; present post '28. Add. Japanese Legation, Berne. — p. 29 ([scan leaf 602](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n602/mode/1up))

**Yoshizawa, Kenkichi** Minister in China; b. 1874 in Niigata-ken; grad. at Literature (English Lit.), Tokyo Imp. Univ., '99; passed Dip. Serv. Exam. the same year; attache at Seoul, '99; Eleve-Consul at Amoy, Shanghai, London, etc., '00-06; 1st Secy. at London, 10; Consul-Gen. at Hankow; Sec. of Chinese Legation, '16-19; Dir. Asiatic Bureau, '19-23; present post since '23. Add. Peking. — p. 29 ([scan leaf 602](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n602/mode/1up))

**Yuchi, Kohei** Mem. House of Peers; b. 1870 in Miyazaki-ken; studied at Tokyo Normal Sch. and then Nippon Horitsu Gakko; passed Higher Civil Serv. Exam., '02; Prefectural officer for years and then transferred to the Metropolitan Police Board; was in charge of the Police of Formosa; Gov. of Fukui till '21; Dir. of the Police Affairs Bureau, Home Office, under the Seiyukai Administration. Add. Azabu Hommura, Tokyo. — p. 29 ([scan leaf 602](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n602/mode/1up))

### Education

**Ando, Koko** a famous lady-violinist, Prof. at Tokyo Academy of Music since 1903; sister of Rohan Koda, well-known novelist; grad. from Tokyo Academy of Music; further studied at the Government Musical School in Berlin, Germany. Add. Koishikawa Omote-cho, Tokyo. — p. 29 ([scan leaf 602](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n602/mode/1up))

**Endo, Ryukichi** D. Litt., Founder and Pres. of the Sugamo Middle School, Pres. of Philosophical and Sociological Ass'ns.; b. 1867 in Mayebashi; grad. from the Litt. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ. Works: Chinese Philosophy, Loatze Philosophy, etc. Add. Sugamo, near Tokyo. — p. 29 ([scan leaf 602](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n602/mode/1up))

**Fukuhara, Ryojiro** Pres. of Peers' School, Mem. House of Peers (nom. '16), Pres. Imp. Acad. of Art. etc.; b. 1869 in Miye-ken; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '92; held various junior posts at Central Government and in the provinces for some years after; as Councillor and Inspector of the Education Dept., '98; dispatched to Europe in '99 to investigate affairs of educational administration; a Bureau Director, '04; Vice-Minister, '11-16; Pres. Tohoku Univ., '17-22; present post in '23. Add. Ushigome Kita-machi, Tokyo. — p. 29 ([scan leaf 602](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n602/mode/1up))

**Fukuzawa, Ichitaro** Chancellor of Keio-gijuku; b. 1863, eldest son of the late Y. Fukuzawa; educated at the Keio and then at Yale Univ., U.S.A. Add. Mita, Tokyo. — p. 29 ([scan leaf 602](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n602/mode/1up))

**Gamo, Yasusato** Dir. of Fukushima Higher Com. Sch. since its creation in 1922; b. 1873 in Nagasaki-ken; grad. from the Tokyo Higher Commercial Sch. (Special class) in '98; visited England, Germany and U.S.A. to study commerce; Prof. of Kumamoto High Sch.; Dir. of Ehime Com. Sch.; Prof. Yamaguchi Higher Com. Sch. Add. Fukushima. — p. 29 ([scan leaf 602](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n602/mode/1up))

**Honda, Iwajiro** Dir. of Tokyo Higher Sericultural School; b. 1866 in Oita-ken; edu. at Tokyo Higher Agr. & For. School, class '88; was expert of Dept. of Agr. & Com. and Dir. of Tokyo Ser. Institute. Add. Nishi Sugamo-machi, near Tokyo. — p. 29 ([scan leaf 602](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n602/mode/1up))

**Ibaraki, Seijiro** Dir. of Urawa High School; b. 1876 in Ishikawa-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. (English Lit.); Prof. at 4th High Sch.; educational inspector; Dir. of Tokyo Musical Academy, Matsumoto High Sch. and Tokyo Girls' Higher Normal Sch. Add. Urawa, Saitama-ken. — p. 30 ([scan leaf 603](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n603/mode/1up))

**Imai, Keisho** Prof. at the Tokyo Academy of Music; b. 1871 in Yokohama; lost his sight when four years old and devoted himself to Koto Music; has taught at the Academy since '94; composed number of tunes. Add. Koj. Ichibancho, Tokyo. — p. 30 ([scan leaf 603](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n603/mode/1up))

**Ishizaka, Masanobu** Ph. B., Ph. D. (Johns Hopkins), Dir. of Aoyama Gakuin and Aoyama Jogakuin; b. 1860 in Tokyo; edu. at the Aoyama Gakuin College and after graduation went to the U.S.A. and studied at Albion Univ. and Johns Hopkins Univ.; returning home in '96 joined the teaching staff of his alma mater; Dean of the collegiate course and middle school course of the school in '06; became Dir. of the Aoyama Gakuin in '21; also Dir. of the Aoyama Jogakuin (girls school) since '27 Add. Aoyama Minami-cho, Tokyo. — p. 30 ([scan leaf 603](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n603/mode/1up))

**Isobe, Yaichiro** Principal & Prop. of the Kokumin Ei-Gakukai (National English Language Association) in Tokyo; b. 1861 in Oita-ken; studied English at Keio Gijuku for several years; organized the above English School in conjunction with a certain foreigner in '88. Add. Koj. Sambancho, Tokyo. — p. 30 ([scan leaf 603](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n603/mode/1up))

**Kawaguchi, Torao** Dir. & Prof. of Hiroshima Higher Technical School; b. 1871 in Fukuoka-ken; grad. in Civil Eng., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '95; sent to U.S.A. and France for study; civil engineer of Kumamoto-ken, '96; Prof. of the 5th High School, '00; next Dir. of Kumamoto Higher Tech. School. Add. Hiroshima. — p. 30 ([scan leaf 603](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n603/mode/1up))

**Konishi, Shimpachi** ex-Dir. of Tokyo Deaf and Dumb Sch.; b. 1854 in Niigata-ken; grad. from the Tokyo Higher Normal Sch., '79; instructor at Chiba Mid. Sch., and then Tokyo Women's Higher Normal Sch.; Dir. of Tokyo Deaf & Dumb School, '93-29. Add. Koishikawa Sasugaya-cho, Tokyo. — p. 30 ([scan leaf 603](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n603/mode/1up))

**Matsuda, Michiko** B.A., lady Director of Doshisha University (Kyoto) since '19; b. 1868 in Tango; studied at the Kyoto Prefectural Girls' High School and then at the Doshisha Girls' College; taught English at a certain mission school in Yokohama; further studied at the Bryn Mawr Coll., Penn.; teacher at Kobe Jogakuin, '99; Head of English Literature Dept., Doshisha Girls' College, and then the present post. Add. Doshisha, Kyoto. — p. 30 ([scan leaf 603](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n603/mode/1up))

**Matsu-ura, Chinjiro** Pres. of Seoul Imperial Univ.; b. 1872 at Uwajima; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '98; Secretary at Tokyo Prefectural Office and at the Department of Education, '98-11; sent to Germany, '07 to inquire into education matters; Dir. of Special Edu. Bureau, '12-24; Vice-Min. of Education, '24-27. Add. Seoul, Chosen. — p. 30 ([scan leaf 603](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n603/mode/1up))

**Miwata, Motomichi** adopted son of late Miwata Masako (lady educationalist) and Principal of Miwata Girls' High School; b. 1872 in Kagawa-ken; grad. from Philosophy and Sociology, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '01. Add. Kojimachi Sanbancho, Tokyo. — p. 30 ([scan leaf 603](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n603/mode/1up))

**Mori, Gaisaburo** Dir. of 3rd High School; b. 1866 in Ishikawa-ken; grad. from the Coll. of Science (mathematics), Tokyo Imp. Univ., '11; Lecturer of Tokyo Higher Commercial School; taught at Miye Middle School, '83; Prof. at Third High School, '95; Prof. at Peers' School, '09; studied in Germany, England and America, '09-11; Principal of Kyoto 1st Middle School, '11; the present post since '22. Add. Yoshida Hommachi, Kyoto. — p. 30 ([scan leaf 603](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n603/mode/1up))

**Murakami, Naojiro** D. Litt., Dir. of Tokyo Academy of Music; b. 186[illegible] in Oita-ken; edu. in history, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '95; Prof. of Spanish in Tokyo School for Foreign Languages, '00; Dir. of the same School, '08. Add. Nagasaki-machi, Tokyo. — p. 30 ([scan leaf 603](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n603/mode/1up))

**Nagaya, Junji** Dir. of Tokyo School of Foreign Languages; b. 1876 in Gifu-ken; grad. from Lit. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '97; was Prof. of 4th School and Hiroshima Higher Normal School; then School Inspector; sent abroad for observation of educational affairs, '27. Add. Nakano, Tokyo. — p. 31 ([scan leaf 604](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n604/mode/1up))

**Nakanome, Akira** Dir. Osaka Foreign Lang. School; b. 1874 in Miyagi-ken; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ. (German Literature), '03; studied in Europe; Prof. at 4th High Sch., Hiroshima Higher Normal Sch., Dir. of Matsuyama High Sch. and then present post (since '21); toured abroad on official mission, '27. Add. Osaka, — p. 31 ([scan leaf 604](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n604/mode/1up))

**Norisugi, Kaju** Dir. of Matsuye Higher School; b. in Toyama-ken; grad. from Lit. Tokyo Imp. Univ., '04; School-Inspector and then Secretary of the Education Dept.; the present post in '24. Add. Matsuye, Shimane-ken. — p. 31 ([scan leaf 604](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n604/mode/1up))

**Oshima, Gishu** Dir. of the Imperial Museum; b. 1871 in Tokyo; grad. in Philosophy, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '94; successively filled the posts of Educational Inspector, Dir. of the Tokyo Music Academy, Dir. of the 8th High School, etc., Dir. of Peeress' School, '20-24; present post in '24. Add. Azabu Hiro-o, Tokyo. — p. 31 ([scan leaf 604](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n604/mode/1up))

**Saito, Hidesaburo** educationist and English scholar, Pres. of Seisoku English Language School; b. 1866 in Miyagi-ken; edu. first at the Kobu Daigaku about five years, but left it unfinished; instructor at the 1st Higher Sch., '88 and the Kokumin Eigakkai; founded the present school in '96. Works: a number of text books on English grammar, an English-Japanese dictionary and other works. Add. Kojimachi Gobancho, Tokyo. — p. 31 ([scan leaf 604](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n604/mode/1up))

**Sato, Shosuke** D. Agr., Ph. D. (Johns Hopkins'), Pres. of Hokkaido Univ. (Sapporo) since its creation; b. 1855 in Iwate-ken; grad. from the then existing Foreign Language School, '76, and the Sapporo Agr. Coll., '80; further studied agri. economy at Johns Hopkins' and in Germany '82-87; Professor in the Sapporo Coll. on returning home; became its President, '94; American exchange prof., '14. Add. Sapporo. — p. 31 ([scan leaf 604](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n604/mode/1up))

**Shimoda, Utako** Madam. Dir. Jissen Girls' School and Pres. of Women's Patriotic Association since '20; a Court maid of honour when about 16 years old, and again after the death of her husband. An instructor of the Peeress' School; resigned in Nov. '07; and has since devoted herself chiefly to a private female school she founded about '99; visited Europe, '93-95. Add. Aoyama Kitamachi, Tokyo. — p. 31 ([scan leaf 604](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n604/mode/1up))

**Takata, Sanae** D.C.L., Crown Mem. House of Peers (nom. '15). President of Waseda Univ.; b. 1860 at Tokyo; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '82; has since been connected with Waseda Univ., which was started the same year by Marquis Okuma; was once one of the leading members of the Progressionists, but for years past kept aloof from active politics, devoting himself to affairs of the Univ.; was Director of the Commercial Bureau, For. Office, '96-97 and Senior Counsellor of Education, '98; was repeatedly returned to the House from Saitama-ken; toured in Europe and America, '14; Minister of Education in the Okuma Cabinet, '15-16. Add. Komagome, Tokyo. — p. 31 ([scan leaf 604](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n604/mode/1up))

**Tanaka, Hozumi** D.C.L., M.A. (Columbia), Chairman, Board of Dir. Waseda Univ.; b. 1875 in Nagano-ken; grad. from Waseda, '96 and further studied in America, England and Germany, '01-03; once connected with the Yomiuri and was also the editor-in-chief of the Nichi Nichi; toured abroad, '21-22. Add. Ushigome Benten-cho, Tokyo. — p. 31 ([scan leaf 604](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n604/mode/1up))

**Tasaki, Shinji** Dir. of Kobe University of Commerce; b. in Nagasaki-ken; grad. Tokyo Higher Com. School, class '01; Lecturer at the alma mater for about a year; sent to England for study, '02-06; Prof. at Nagasaki Higher Com. School, '06; at Kobe Higher Com. School, '08; visited Europe in '14 and England in '23; Dir. of Kobe High. Com. Sch., '25; present post, in Apr. '29. Add. Kobe Univ. of Commerce. — p. 32 ([scan leaf 605](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n605/mode/1up))

**Tomeoka, Kosuke** Pres. of Family School, Dir. of Central Association of Social Works; b. 1868 in Tokyo; grad. at Doshisha Theological School in '88, christian preacher for years; stud. in America, '95-99; visited Europe to study social works in '03; created Family School to reform fallen women. Add. Nishi-Sugamo, near Tokyo. — p. 32 ([scan leaf 605](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n605/mode/1up))

**Tsuda, Umeko** lady educationist and founder of the Joshi Eigaku-juku (Girls' English School) in Tokyo; b. 1865 in Tokyo, daughter of the late Tsuda Sen, an agriculturist; was among the first batch of girls sent to U.S.A. for study by Government, '71-82; teacher in the Peers' School and lecturer in the Women's Higher Normal School on returning home; has frequently visited America. Add. Kojimachi Goban-cho, Tokyo. — p. 32 ([scan leaf 605](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n605/mode/1up))

**Watanabe, Ryusei** Dir. Nagoya Higher Com. Sch.; b. 1866 in Niigata-ken; grad. from Tokyo Semmon Gakko (now Waseda Univ.), '87; studied Ethics in U.S.A. and Germany; was Prof. of Tokyo Academy of Music, Tokyo Higher Normal School, and Dir. of the former school, successively; was despatched to China in '13, and to Europe and America, '20. Add. Nagoya. — p. 32 ([scan leaf 605](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n605/mode/1up))

**Yasui, Tetsu** Pres. of Tokyo Women's College; b. 1870 in Tokyo; grad. from the Tokyo Female Higher Normal School, '90; studied in England, '96; was Prof. of Tokyo Female Higher Normal School for many years. Add. Uyenohara, Nakano-machi, near Tokyo. — p. 32 ([scan leaf 605](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n605/mode/1up))

**Yuhara, Gen-ichi** Dir. Tokyo High Sch.; b. 1863 in Saga; first studied Medicine at Tokyo Imp. Univ., but left it without finishing in '84; held post of prof. at various Gov. Schools or Dir. of Middle Schools; Dir. Imperial Academy of Music, '08-17; Dir. Tokyo Higher Girls' Normal Sch., '17-21; present post since '22. Add. Yoyogi Tomigaya, near Tokyo. — p. 32 ([scan leaf 605](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n605/mode/1up))

### Electric

**Aoyama, Rokuro** Dir. of the Teikoku Electric Light Co., Mng. of Tokyo Branch of L. J. Healing & Co., Ltd. (London); b. 1875 in Mie-ken; grad. from the now defunct Post & Telegraph School, '02 and entered Govt. service; then resigning the post entered Messrs. Healing as Mng. Add. Shiba Takanawa, Tokyo. — p. 32 ([scan leaf 605](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n605/mode/1up))

**Daido, Ryota** Dir. of Tokyo Electric Light Co., ex-Dir. of Tokyo Municipal Electric Office; b. 1879 in Shiga-ken; grad. in Law, Kyoto Imp. Univ. in '03; Private Secretary to Home Minister, '04; Councillor and then Secretary of the Dept. of Communications; Chief of the General Affairs Section of the Kobe Railway Control Bureau; Councillor and then Director of the Imp. Railway Board, '15-16; present post in '26. Add. Ushigome Haramachi, Tokyo. — p. 32 ([scan leaf 605](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n605/mode/1up))

**Fujioka, Ichisuke** Dr. Eng. (Japan), Pres. Iwakuni Elec. Tram. and Tokyo Elec. Cos.; b. in 1856; grad. from Tokyo Engineering College in '81; appointed its Professor, '84; retired '86 to start electric business; chief electrician to the Tokyo Electric Light Co. in '92 and founder of the Tokyo Street Railway Co.; has been to Europe and America several times for study, on business, etc. and was under Edison for some while. Add. Azabu Higashicho, Tokyo. — p. 32 ([scan leaf 605](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n605/mode/1up))

**Fukuzawa, Momosuke** ex-M.P., Pres. of Daido Elec. Power Co., etc., brother-in-law of the Fukuzawa brothers, being husband of their sister; b. 1864 at Kawagoye; studied at Keio; and further at a Commercial Coll. in U.S.A.; returning home in '89, he first entered Tokyo Ice Works, then Oji Paper Mill, and finally Hokkaido Colliery & Steamship Co., of which he was agent for Tokyo. Add. Shibuya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 33 ([scan leaf 606](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n606/mode/1up))

**Go, Seinosuke** 2nd Baron, Mem. House of Peers, Pres. of Tokyo Electric Light Co., and Oriental Steel Foundry, dir. of various Cos.; b. 1863 in Tokyo, son of late Junzo Go; studied in Europe; after several years in Govt. service he entered business circles; was chairman of Tokyo Stock Ex. till '24. Add. Koj. Kaminibancho, Tokyo. — p. 33 ([scan leaf 606](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n606/mode/1up))

**Hamaguchi, Tan** B.A. (Cambridge), founder of Inawashiro Hydro-Elec. Power Co.; b. 1872 in Wakayama-ken; grad. Keio Univ., '91 and Waseda, '94; further studied at Cambridge; M.P. in '04 from Wakayama-ken. Add. Koj. Shimo-rokubancho, Tokyo. — p. 33 ([scan leaf 606](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n606/mode/1up))

**Itami, Jiro** Dir. Daido Electric Power Co., Mem. Kobe Chamber of Commerce; b. 1863 in Tokyo, younger brother to the late Baron Haruo Itami; early entered the N.Y.K. being later promoted to be Mng. of its Kobe Branch. Add. Shimo-Yamate-dori, Kobe. — p. 33 ([scan leaf 606](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n606/mode/1up))

**Iwatare, Kunihiko** electrician and Mng.-Dir. of the Nippon Electric Co., (joint undertaking between Japanese and foreign capitalists in which the Western-Electric Co., U.S.A., are prominent); b. 1854 in Fukuoka; grad. from the former Govt. Engineering Coll., '82, and entered the present Co. on its formation about '93. Add. Azabu Fujimicho, Tokyo. — p. 33 ([scan leaf 606](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n606/mode/1up))

**Kawada, Takashi** Dir. of Keio Electric R'ly Co., &c.; b. 1870, eldest son of Takeshi Kawada (learned scholar of Chinese classics); studied at Michigan in '96. Add. Ushigome Wakamiya-cho, Tokyo. — p. 33 ([scan leaf 606](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n606/mode/1up))

**Masuda, Jiro** Pres. of Osaka Elec. Power Co., Vice-Pres. of Daido Elec. Power Co.; b. 1868 in Shizuoka-ken; engaged in printing business in his early years, besides shared in Daido Fertilizer Co., Tateyama Hydro-Elec. Co., etc. Shibuya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 33 ([scan leaf 606](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n606/mode/1up))

**Matsunaga, Yasuzayemon** Vice-Pres. of Toho Electric Power Co., Dir. of Toho Gas Co., etc.; b. 1875 in Iki; grad. from Keio Univ. in '00; entered the Bank of Japan., '00; afterwards established a firm and carried on coal business in Kobe and Osaka over ten years; founded an electric railway concern in Fukuoka, Kyushu in '90. Add. Shimoochiai, Tokyo. — p. 33 ([scan leaf 606](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n606/mode/1up))

**Ogino, Mototaro** Dir. Furukawa Elec. Industry Co.; b. 1874 in Okayama-ken and was adopted into the family; studied at Waseda and entered Furukawa Firm; branch manager at Osaka and Shanghai, and then Dir. of Business Section of the Head Office, etc.; stayed in England. Add. Koj. Ichi-Bancho, Tokyo. — p. 33 ([scan leaf 606](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n606/mode/1up))

**Okada, Bunji** Mem. House of Peers, Chairman Kokkatsu (International Film) Co.; b. 1874 in Yamagata-ken; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '08 and passed civil service exam. same year; was in service of provincial govts. before his app. to Governor of Tochigi-ken; then Governor of Karafuto, '14; Sup't. of Metropolitan Police, '16-18. Add. Aoyama Takagi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 33 ([scan leaf 606](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n606/mode/1up))

**Ota, Mitsuhiro** Pres. of Miye Electric Co., and of Kei-Han Electric R'ly. Co., Dir. of Daido Electric Power Co., Shin Kei-Han R'ly. Co.; b. 1874 in Yamaguchi; adopted son of Kosaburo Ota; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '98; Secy. of Imp. Railway Board and later entered the Kei-Han Electric Co., Ltd., as its Mng. Dir. in '06. Add. Kei-Han Office, Osaka. — p. 33 ([scan leaf 606](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n606/mode/1up))

**Toshimitsu, Tsurumatsu** Pres. of Kinugawa Hydro-Elec. Co., Dir. of Tokyo Gas Co., etc.; b. 1863 in Oita-ken; edu. at Meiji Univ.; became lawyer and struggled his way up to success; once M.P., but soon resigned to enter business; Dir. of the then Tokyo Railway Co.; is also Pres. of Odawara Express Elec. Railway Co. started '27. Add. Kami Osaki, near Tokyo. — p. 33 ([scan leaf 606](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n606/mode/1up))

**Uyehara, Shikazo** Dir. Keisei Electric R'ly. Co., Borneo Rubber Co., Japan Electro-Industrial Co., etc.; b. 1860 in Oita-ken; grad. from the Oita Normal School and Tokyo Semmon Gakko (forerunner of Waseda Univ.); passed the Exam. for Bar and began practice in Tokyo; once returned M.P. Add. Koishikawa Shin-Suwacho, Tokyo. — p. 34 ([scan leaf 607](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n607/mode/1up))

**Wakao, Shohachi** Crown Mem. House of Peers (since '27), Pres. of Tokyo Electric Light Co., Dir. of Naigai Kogyo, Tokyo Taxi-Cab, Nippon Seima, and other concerns; b. in 1873 and adopted into the family '96 by marriage; studied at the Chuo Daigaku. Add. Mita, Tokyo. — p. 34 ([scan leaf 607](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n607/mode/1up))

**Watanabe, Osamu** Pres. of Osaka Elec. Lamp Bulb Co., and Uwa Hydro-Elec. Co.; b. 1859 in Ehime-ken; grad. from Keiogijuku in 1881; Vice-Consul at Wonsan (Korea), '82; Comptroller of the Patent Bureau of the Agr. & Com. Dept., '89; Dir. of the Nagasaki Post and Telegraph Office, '91; returned to the Lower House seven times; Mayor of Sasebo City, '02-06; afterwards entered business. Add. Kojimachi-ku Hirakawa-cho, Tokyo. — p. 34 ([scan leaf 607](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n607/mode/1up))

### Engineering

**Arakawa, Bunroku** Dr. Eng., M.A. (Cornell), Prof. at Engineering Dept., Kyushu Imp. Univ.; b. 1878 in Yokohama; grad. from the Eng. Dept., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '00; lecturer at his alma mater the same year; sent to Germany, England and U.S.A. for study, '08-10; present post since '11. Works: Science of Electrical Engineering. Add. Jigyo-nishi-machi, Fukuoka city. — p. 34 ([scan leaf 607](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n607/mode/1up))

**Arisaka, Shozo** D. Eng., Director of Naval Arsenal, Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1867 in Tokyo; edu. at the University, class '90; sent by the Navy to France where he studied at Hotchkiss; devised a new plug for quickfires; sent twice to England on official business. Add. Tamagawa, Ebara-gun, Tokyo. — p. 34 ([scan leaf 607](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n607/mode/1up))

**Asano, Osuke** Dr. Eng., Emeritus Prof. of Waseda; b. 1859 at Okayama; edu. in electricity at the Govt. Eng. College, class '81; electrician to Department of Communications till '16; was despatched to Europe and America two or three times. Add. Azabu Kogai-cho, Tokyo. — p. 34 ([scan leaf 607](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n607/mode/1up))

**Chujo, Sei-ichiro** Architect, Hon. Mem. of Society of Architects (London), and of Royal Society of Arts (London); b. 1868 at Yonezawa; grad. at Eng. Coll. (Architecture), Tokyo Imp. Univ.; further studied at Cambridge (Eng.); on returning home entered Edu. Dept.; again studied in England, '03-07 opened Sone-Chujo Office, '08, besides his own. Add. Komagome Hayashi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 34 ([scan leaf 607](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n607/mode/1up))

**Furuichi, Koi** Baron (cr. 1919), Dr. Eng., Mem. Imperial Academy, Privy Councillor since '24; Emeritus Prof. Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1854 at Himeji; studied engineering in France, '75-80; was successively Director of the Public Works Bureau (Home Office); Vice-Minister of Communications, Director-General of the Government Railways and Director of Seoul-Fusan R'ly., '03-07; Pres. of Tokyo Subway Co. till '25. He is one of the best amateur “no” singers and actors. Add. Hongo Yumi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 34 ([scan leaf 607](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n607/mode/1up))

**Ito, Chuta** D. Eng., Architect, Emeritus Prof., Tokyo Imp. Univ. b. 1864 in Yamagata-ken; grad. from Architecture at the University, '92; despatched to China, India and Turkey in '02-05 to study his special subject. Add. Hongo Nishikatamachi, Tokyo. — p. 34 ([scan leaf 607](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n607/mode/1up))

**Kaneko, Noboru** Dr. Eng., Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1870 in Ehime; grad. in Mechanical Eng., Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '94; further studied his speciality in England and France; Prof. at 3rd High School, etc. Add. Kyoto Imp. Univ. — p. 35 ([scan leaf 608](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n608/mode/1up))

**Kujirai, Tsunetaro** D. Eng., Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1884 in Saitama-ken; grad. from Electrical Engineering Course of Tokyo Imp. Univ., '07; first a technical expert of communications; then Assist.-Prof. of Tokyo Imp. Univ.; stayed in Germany, England and America for study. Add. Hongo-ku, Tokyo. — p. 35 ([scan leaf 608](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n608/mode/1up))

**Kunisawa, Shimbei** D. Eng., ex-Chairman of Board of the South Manchuria R'ly. Co., '13-19; b. 1864 in Kochi-ken; grad. in '89 from Eng. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ.; engineer of the nationalized Kyushu R'ly. Co., '89; Eng. to the Railway Department, '92-05; Vice-Pres. of the South M. R'ly., '06-18; Pres. of same, '18-22. Add. Okubo, Tokyo. — p. 35 ([scan leaf 608](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n608/mode/1up))

**Makita, Tamaki** Dr. Eng., Mng.-Dir. of Mitsui Mining Co., Electro-Chemical Industry Co., Hokkaido Iron Works, etc.; b. 1871 in Osaka; grad. in Mining, Tokyo Imp. Univ.; entered Mitsui firm as expert, gradually rising to the present eminence. Add. Azabu Kita-Higakubo, Tokyo. — p. 35 ([scan leaf 608](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n608/mode/1up))

**Matsumura, Tsuruzo** D. Eng., Prof. at Eng. Coll. of the Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1871 in Hokkaido; grad. from the Eng. Coll. of the Tokyo Imp. Univ., '93; appointed Prof. at 3rd High School, '96; Assist.-Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ., '97; sent to Germany for study, '99; Prof. at Science and Eng. Coll. of the Kyoto Imp. Univ., '01; Prof. at Eng. Coll. of the same Univ. since '14. Add. Kyoto. — p. 35 ([scan leaf 608](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n608/mode/1up))

**Naito, Tachu** D. Eng., Prof. at Waseda Univ. since '10, Mem. of Govt. Earthquake Committee; b. 1886 in Yamanashi-ken; edu. in Architecture, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '10; further studied Ferro-concrete and allied construction at the Institute of Technology, Boston, '16-18. Works: Earthquake-proof Construction of Skeleton Structure, Aug. '24; The Theory of Structure and of Building Construction, 9th June, '25 (both in Japanese). Add. Waseda Univ. — p. 35 ([scan leaf 608](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n608/mode/1up))

**Nakamura, Tatsutaro** D. Eng., Emeritus Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1860 in Tokyo; edu. at now defunct Eng. College in '82; assist. architect in building the Imperial Palace, '83-86; Professor of the University combining a post in the Home Office; studied in Europe, '92-98; sent in '97 to Assam, India, to study architecture as affected by earthquake, and also to Naples in '09 on a similar mission. Add. Koishikawa Haramachi, Tokyo. — p. 35 ([scan leaf 608](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n608/mode/1up))

**Nakayama, Hidesaburo** D. Eng., (Civil Eng.), Emeritus Prof. at the Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1867 in Aichi-ken; grad. from the University with honours in '88; engineer to the Kwansai R'ly Co. for some years; studied further his speciality in Germany and other countries, '96-98. Add. Hongo Nishikata-machi, Tokyo. — p. 35 ([scan leaf 608](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n608/mode/1up))

**Naoki, Rintaro** D. Eng.; b. 1876 in Kobe; grad. from Civil Eng., Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '09; Engineer to the Tokyo City Office the same year; despatched abroad in '02 to inspect harbor construction work in Europe and America, returning home, '03; Chief of Public Work Section of the Tokyo City Office; then Eng. of the Finance Dept.; attached to the Construction Bureau, '06; in charge of the Yokohama harbor work, '08; Eng. to the Tokyo City Office, '11; Chief of the Osaka Harbor Work Office and also of the City Planning Office, till '24; Dir. of the Reconstruction Bureau, '24-25; adviser to the same, '25-26. Add. Reconstruction Bureau. — p. 35 ([scan leaf 608](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n608/mode/1up))

**Nishida, Hirotaro** D. Eng., Dir. and Prof. of the Kiryu Higher Tech. School, Prop. of the Nishida Laboratory; b. 1877 in Tokyo; grad. in applied chemistry, Tokyo Imp. Univ.; further studied at the School of Technology in Manchester, and Ecole de Chimie, Mulhouse, Alsace; was Prof. of the Nagoya High. Technical Coll.; Mng.-Dir. of the Nippon Celluloid Co. Add. Kojimachi Junichome, Tokyo. — p. 36 ([scan leaf 609](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n609/mode/1up))

**Okochi, Masatoshi** 2nd Visc. (suc. '07), D. Eng., Prof. at Eng. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '04-25; Mem. of House of Peers; b. 1878 at Toyohashi; grad. from the said college, '03; further studied in Germany and France, '08-11; sent to Tsingtao to prosecute scientific investigation there during the 1914 War; Senior Secretary of Navy, '20 and Head of Scientific Investigation Office since '21. Add. Uyeno Yanaka Shimizucho, Tokyo. — p. 36 ([scan leaf 609](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n609/mode/1up))

**Sakurai, Kotaro** D. Eng. (Architecture), Consulting Architect to the Mitsubishi Firm for whom he has designed a large number of buildings; b. 1870 in Tokyo; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., in Architecture, class '95. Add. Ichigaya Nakano-cho, Tokyo. — p. 36 ([scan leaf 609](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n609/mode/1up))

**Sato, Ko-ichi** D. Eng. (Architecture), Prof. at Waseda Univ. since '10; b. 1878 in Tochigi-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Architecture, class '10; travelled in Europe and America, '10-11; 1st prize designs of the Tokyo City Hall, etc. Add. Koishikawa Sasugaya-cho, Tokyo. — p. 36 ([scan leaf 609](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n609/mode/1up))

**Shiba, Chuzaburo** Baron, D. Eng., Prof. of Shipbuilding at Tokyo Imp. Univ., and consulting engineer to the Kawasaki Dockyard; b. 1872 in Kaga; grad. from the Tokyo Imp. Univ., '95, and afterward studied at the Central Technical College, London, and also at Humphrey, and Tennant's Works and finally at Berlin; undertook the designing of the Toyo Kisen Kaisha's sister steamers, Tenyo and Chiyo. Add. Hongo Akebono-cho, Tokyo. — p. 36 ([scan leaf 609](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n609/mode/1up))

**Takamatsu, Toyokichi** D. Eng., Emeritus Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., ex-Dir. of State Industrial Laboratory, etc.; b. 1855 in Tokyo; grad. at Chemistry, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '78; further studied at Owens Coll., Manchester, and at Berlin Univ., returning home in '82; Prof. at his alma mater and other institutions; retired in '05; the Pres. of Tokyo Gas Co., '05-15. Add. Hongo Higashikatamachi, Tokyo. — p. 36 ([scan leaf 609](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n609/mode/1up))

**Tawara, Kuni-ichi** D. Eng. (Metallurgy), Mem. of Imperial Academy, Emeritus Prof. of Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1872 in Shimane-ken; grad. from above institution, class '97, and after some years' further studies in Germany, was appointed Prof. at his alma mater. Add. Tokyo Imp. Univ. — p. 36 ([scan leaf 609](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n609/mode/1up))

**Tsukamoto, Yasushi** D. Eng., ex-Dean of the Eng. Coll. and Prof. of Architecture, Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1869 in Kyoto; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., '93; further studied in England, Germany and France, '99-02; Prof. at the alma mater on his return. Add. Koishikawa Hisakatacho, Tokyo. — p. 36 ([scan leaf 609](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n609/mode/1up))

**Watanabe, Kaichi** D. Eng., C.E., B.S., Pres. Ishikawajima Dockyard Co., Dir. Keihan Elec. Rly.; b. 1853 in Nagano-ken; grad. in '83; Govt. Eng. College; conferred C.E. and B.S. by Glasgow Univ., class '89; studied practical side under Sir John Fowler and other noted British engineers; elected member of Civil Engineering Society, London. Add. Akasaka Omote-cho, Tokyo. — p. 36 ([scan leaf 609](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n609/mode/1up))
