**Yagi, Hideji** D. Eng., Prof. Tohoku Imp. Univ.; b. in Kyoto; grad. in Elec. Eng., Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '09; was Prof. of the Sendai Higher Technical School; then studied in England. Add. Ryokakuin-cho, Sendai City. — p. 36 ([scan leaf 609](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n609/mode/1up))

### Industry

**Abe, Hidetaro** Aud. of Russo-Japanese Fishery Co., Ex-Sec. of the Bank of Chosen; b. 1878 in Ehime-ken; grad. from the Law Dept., Tokyo Imp. Univ., and on graduation entered the service of the Chosen Bank, in which he had charge of a Branch at Fusan and other places; later Sectional Chief of the head office; removed to the present post. Add. Hisakata-machi, Koishikawa, Tokyo. — p. 37 ([scan leaf 610](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n610/mode/1up))

**Adachi, So** Pres. of Japan Chemical Industry Co., Ltd.; b. 1870 in Tottori-ken; grad. from Keio Univ., '92; entered the Jiji Shimpo with which he was connected for about nineteen years; then became a Dir. of the Japan Conscription Ins. Co., Ltd. Add. Shirokane Sanko-cho, Shiba-ku, Tokyo. — p. 37 ([scan leaf 610](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n610/mode/1up))

**Akabane, Katsumi** Dir. S.M.R. since '22; b. 1869; grad. from Tokyo Higher Commercial School, '04 and took to business; Dept. Chief of the Mitsui Bussan till '21. Add. Dairen. — p. 37 ([scan leaf 610](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n610/mode/1up))

**Amano, Hichisaburo** Prop. of Amano Railway Car Works in Tokyo; b. 1874 in Kyoto; edu. at Doshisha and the Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Mechanical Engineering; is Mng.-Dir. of the Japan Car Works, Ltd., in which the interest of the Amano family is predominant. Add. Honjo Aioi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 37 ([scan leaf 610](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n610/mode/1up))

**Asano, Ryozo** Dir. of the Asano Cement Co. and several other undertakings started by his father Soichiro Asano; b. 1889, second son; edu. at Harvard in Economy, class '12. Add. Mita Tsunamachi, Tokyo. — p. 37 ([scan leaf 610](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n610/mode/1up))

**Ayukawa, Gisuke** mechanical engineer and partner of the Kuhara and the Kaijima interests, being connected with both families by marriage; b. 1880 in Yamaguchi-ken; edu. at Eng. Coll. of Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '03; served for some years at the Shibaura Eng. Works and then went abroad for study and inspection. Add. Ushigome Sanaicho, Tokyo. — p. 37 ([scan leaf 610](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n610/mode/1up))

**Danno, Reisuke** Man.-Dir. of Russo-Japanese Fishery Co., Ltd.; b. 1875 in Nagasaki-ken; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '99; was Mng. of Wakamatsu Branch of the Mitsui Bussan Kaisha; then Mng. of Sapporo Branch of Hokkaido Colliery Steamship Co., Ltd.; present post in '23. Add. Myogadani, Koishikawa, Tokyo. — p. 37 ([scan leaf 610](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n610/mode/1up))

**Eitaki, Hisakichi** Mng.-Dir. of Tokyo Building Co.; b. 1866 in Echigo; studied law and became Judge in '93 and transferred to the Foreign Office same year; attached to Consulate at Fusan in '93; Consul at Jinsen, Shanghai, Shashi, Sydney and Hankow between '94 and '05; Consul-Gen. at Shanghai, '05-09; then at Chientao till '12; Con.-Gen. at Honolulu, '12-14. Add. Tozuka, Tokyo. — p. 37 ([scan leaf 610](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n610/mode/1up))

**Fujiyama, Raita** Pres. of Nippon Sugar Refinery Co., etc., Dir. of various concerns; b. 1865 in Karatsu; grad. at Keio-Gijuku, '90; was in the service of Mitsui Bussan; then entered the present Co. after the notorious scandal culminating in the suicide of its former President; Chairman of Tokyo Chamber of Commerce till '25 as successor of Visc. Shibusawa. Add. Shiba Shirokane, Tokyo. — p. 37 ([scan leaf 610](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n610/mode/1up))

**Fujiwara, Ginjiro** Pres. and Mng.-Dir. of the Oji Paper Manufacturing Co. since '11; b. 1868 in Nagano-ken; grad. from Keio, '91 and then took to journalism in the provinces; entered the Mitsui Bank and was transferred to the Mitsui Bussan Kaisha and had charge of its Branches at Shanghai and Taihoku, '15. Add. Azabu Shin-ami, Tokyo. — p. 37 ([scan leaf 610](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n610/mode/1up))

**Fukuhara, Toshimaru** 2nd Baron, Mem. House of Peers; Dir. of Onoda Portland Cement Mfg. Co.; b. 1886 in Yamaguchi-ken; grad. from Eng. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '01; Lecturer at Kyoto Imp. Univ., '02-04; Army technical expert, '04-06; despatched to Europe and America on official mission, '08-09; was also on the faculty of Waseda Univ. Add. Yotsuya, Tokyo. — p. 37 ([scan leaf 610](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n610/mode/1up))

**Haga, Gonshiro** Lieutenant (ret.), Dir. Gov. Sericultural Institute at Nakano; b. 1867 in Saitama-ken; grad. in Agriculture, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '92. Add. Nakano, near Tokyo. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 611](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n611/mode/1up))

**Haneda, Inosuke** Prop. of Haneda Belt Mfg. Co.; b. 1871 in Kyoto; studied cotton thread belt with his brother and invented it which patented in '99, afterward completed manufacture of rubber belt and others. Add. Honjo Sotote-machi, Tokyo.. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 611](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n611/mode/1up))

**Hasegawa, Shogo** Dir. of Kisha-Seizo (Car Mfg.) Co.; b. 1872 in Osaka; grad. in Mechanics, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '96. Add. Tennoji, Osaka. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 611](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n611/mode/1up))

**Hattori, Kintaro** Crown Mem. House of Peers, Pres. of K. Hattori & Co., Ltd., that run Seikosha Watch and Clock Works, Dir. of First Mutual Life Ins. Co. and of Chosen Industrial Co., Ltd.; b. 1860 in Tokyo; established in business 1881 and incorporated '17. Add. Ginza Shichome, Tokyo. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 611](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n611/mode/1up))

**Hiraoka, Ki** pioneer of rolling-stock makers in Japan, ex-Vice-Pres. Osaka Loco. Works; b. 1855 in Tokyo; went to U.S.A. in '71, and entered a rolling-stock shop, N.Y., returning home about '80; was overseer of the works at Shimoasai, till about '90, and started the Hiraoka Rolling-Stock Works at Honjo, Tokyo, which was fused with Osaka Loco. Add. Kojimachi Nagatacho, Tokyo. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 611](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n611/mode/1up))

**Hirayama, Torajiro** Mng.-Dir. of Taiwan Sugar Mfg. Co.; b. 1870 in Hiroshima-ken; grad. at Tokyo Higher Com. Sch. in '99; entered Mitsui Bussan Co.; afterward transferred to Taiwan Sugar Co. Add. Tokyo. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 611](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n611/mode/1up))

**Hoshi, Hajime** Pres. of Hoshi Pharmaceutical Co.; b. 1874 in Fukushima; edu. at Tokyo Higher Com. School and later at Columbia Univ.; published monthly "Japan and America" in N. Y.; despatched to Europe to inspect industry; returning home in '07 started present Co.; was once elected M.P. Add. Shinagawa Gotenyama, Tokyo. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 611](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n611/mode/1up))

**Ikegai, Shotaro** Prop. of Ikegai Iron Works; b. 1869 in Chiba-ken; began machine manufacture in '90, now manufacturing internal combustion engine and printing machine, etc. Add. Mita Shikoku-cho, Tokyo. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 611](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n611/mode/1up))

**Ikeo, Yoshizo** Pres. of Kurobe Railway Co., Hida Cable Transport Co.; b. 1878 in Shiga-ken; grad. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ. in '04. Add. Kaminomiya-machi, Osaka. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 611](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n611/mode/1up))

**Inouye, Masaji** b. 1876 in Tamba; grad. from Politics, Waseda Univ.; further studied at Vienna Univ. and Berlin Univ.; a senior official of the Financial Dept., Korean Government, '04, and held various local posts; next secretaryship of the Korean Household Dept.; went to Europe and America on official mission, to inspect the colonial condition there, '00; resigned official post and went to South Sea Islands to start gum plantation. Add. Takata-machi, Tokyo. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 611](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n611/mode/1up))

**Ito, Kinsuke** Dir. Keio Univ., Nippon Flour Co., Chiyoda Life Ins. Co., etc.; b. 1865 in Yamaguchi; grad. Keio Univ. and for several years was on the staff of the Jiji; served at the Nippon Ginko, '98-06; visited Europe and America, '05 on official business; left the bank soon after his return, and restarted the defunct daily Nippon, '06, which was discontinued after some years. Add. Hakusan Goten-machi, Tokyo. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 611](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n611/mode/1up))

**Iwahara, Kenzo** Dir. Onoda Cement Co., Shibaura Iron Works, etc.; b. 1863 in Ishikawa-ken; grad. from Mitsubishi Nautical School, '83; Private Secretary to R. W. Irwin, adviser of the Union Transportation Co.,; then on a similar service to Mr. Masuda, of Mitsui Bussan Kaisha; had charge of the Co.'s London Office, '86-90; Mng. of the Osaka and Kobe branches, '90-95; then of the branch office at New York, '96; Mng.-Dir. of the Co's head office, '06; left in '14 being involved in the Naval Scandal. Add. Azabu Miyamura-cho, Tokyo. — p. 38 ([scan leaf 611](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n611/mode/1up))

**Kabayama, Aisuke** 2nd Count, Pres. of Japan Steel Works at Muroran; Pres. of Taisho Bank; Dir. of Horai Life Ins., Hakodate Dockyard, Copper Mfg. Cos.; b. 1865, son of the late Adm. Count Kabayama; studied economics in England. Add. Nagatacho, Tokyo. — p. 39 ([scan leaf 612](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n612/mode/1up))

**Katakura, Kanetaro** Pres. of Katakura Raw Silk Mfg. Co.; b. '63 in Nagano-ken; adopted by K. Katakura as son-in-law in '77, runs about 36 mills through the country, besides shared in the business of Katakura Life Ins. Co., Nagano Sav. Bank, 19th Bank, Takabata Railway Co., etc. Add. Hirano-mura, Nagano-ken. — p. 39 ([scan leaf 612](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n612/mode/1up))

**Kishi, Keijiro** D. Eng., Dir. Shibaura Engineering Works; b. 1868 in Wakayama-ken; grad. in Eng., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '95. Add. Kirigaya, Osaki, near Tokyo. — p. 39 ([scan leaf 612](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n612/mode/1up))

**Kobayashi, Sakutaro** Mng.-Dir. of Shibaura Iron Works; b. 1869 in Nagasaki-ken; edu. at a Technical Apprentice School; entered the Shibaura Works and was twice sent abroad to inspect technical industries; succeeded in improving or inventing mechanical devices and appliances and was awarded the Blue Ribbon Medal. Add. Osaki, near Tokyo. — p. 39 ([scan leaf 612](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n612/mode/1up))

**Kurosawa, Teijiro** Typewriter Manufacturer; b. 1875 in Tokyo; went to U.S.A. in his early years, and engaged by Eliot Hatch Co. typewriter manufacturers in New York for years; invented machine of Japanese script, now runs manufacturing mill at Kamata which is known by its welfare provision for labourers. Add. Kamata-machi, near Tokyo. — p. 39 ([scan leaf 612](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n612/mode/1up))

**Maki, Tetsu** Pres. of Ensuiko Sugar Co. and Karenko Timber Co., Dir. of the Taiwan Warehouse Co., etc.; b. 1866 in Niigata-ken; studied at Keio Univ.; was in the services of the Hokuetsu Railway Co. and Oji Paper Mill Co.; afterwards made a tour through Europe and America; entered the service of the Ensuiko Sugar Co. in '07 as its Mng.-Dir. Add. Ushigome, Tokyo. — p. 39 ([scan leaf 612](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n612/mode/1up))

**Makoshi, Kyohei** Pres. of Japan Brewery Co., Dir. of other cos., Crown Mem. House of Peers (nom. 1928); b. 1843 at Okayama; came to Tokyo in '70 to find work with almost empty purse; started a lodging house; became acquainted with Baron Takashi Masuda and was appointed clerk of an exp. and imp. firm at Yokohama; transferred to the Mitsui Bussan Co.; left it '88 to start the Yebisu Brewery at Meguro; twice elected M.P. but has since abandoned politics. Add. Shiba Sakuragawa-cho, Tokyo. — p. 39 ([scan leaf 612](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n612/mode/1up))

**Masuda, Taro** Man.-Dir. of Taiwan Sugar Mfg. Co., Nippon Artificial Fertilizer, Manju Life Ins. Co., Imperial Theatre, etc.; b. 1875 in Tokyo, eldest son of Takashi Masuda; educated in England; is also a popular amateur playwright. Add. Nihombashi Hama-cho, Tokyo. — p. 39 ([scan leaf 612](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n612/mode/1up))

**Matsukata, Kojiro** ex-Pres. of the Kawasaki Dockyard, the Kobe Gas Co., the Kyushu Electric Tramway Co., the Kawasaki Steamship Co., etc.; educated at the Peers' School and the Tokyo Imp. Univ.; further studied in Europe and America; after returning home became Lecturer of the Tokyo Imp. Univ., which post he soon resigned to enter the business world; elected M.P. for Kobe in '21; involved in the financial trouble of the Dockyard and resigned its presidency in '27. Add. Yamamoto-dori, Kobe. — p. 39 ([scan leaf 612](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n612/mode/1up))

**Matsuoka, Yoyu** ex-Pres. of South Manchuria R'ly Co.; b. 1880 in Yamaguchi-ken; edu. at Law, State Univ., Oregon, U.S.A.; passed Diplomatic Service Exam. in '04; Secretary of Kwantung Leased Territory; next at Foreign Office, Legation and then of Embassy; next Consul-Gen.; Vice-Pres. of South Manchuria R'ly Co., '27-29. Add. Tokyo. — p. 39 ([scan leaf 612](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n612/mode/1up))

**Mikimoto, Kokichi** Prop. of Mikimoto Pearl Store (dealer in and originator of culture pearls); b. 1857 at Miye-ken; early started cultivation of pearl oyster, establishing a station in Ago Bay, Toba, which has won many prizes and medals at home and foreign expositions. Add. Ginza, Tokyo. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 613](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n613/mode/1up))

**Minorikawa, Naosaburo** Prop. of Minorikawa Mill, manufacturers of sericultural machines; b. in Akita-ken; stud. sericulture at Nishigahara Sericultural Inst. in his early years; has invented many patent machines numbering about 150. Add. Tokyo. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 613](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n613/mode/1up))

**Miwa, Zembei** Prop. of the Marumiya Firm, manufacturer, wholesale dealer and exporter of toilet articles, drugs, soaps, etc.; b. 1871 in Tokyo and succeeded to his father's business in 1971; once Mem. of Tokyo Chamber of Commerce. Add. Nihombashi Tachibanacho, Tokyo. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 613](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n613/mode/1up))

**Mori, Shunrokuro** Dir. of the South Manchuria Railway Co.; b. 1878 in Fukushima-ken; grad. from the Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '02, passed the Higher Civil Service Examination, '02; entered the Finance Dept. as Secretary, '07; Dir. of the Banking Bureau of the same dept. in '16. Add. Yamashiro-cho, Dairen City. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 613](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n613/mode/1up))

**Moroi, Shiro** Mng.-Dir. of the Toa Flour Mill; b. 1869 in Saitama-ken; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '90; entered Keifu R'ly Co.; became Manager of the present Co. on its establishment in '06. Add. Hongo Yushima-Shinhanacho, Tokyo. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 613](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n613/mode/1up))

**Nagayo, Teizo** Dir. of Yokosuka Iron Works, Taihei Cotton Spinning, etc.; b. 1874 in Tokyo; studied at Peers' School; a clerk of Silk Exporting Co. at Yokohama; studied economics at Pennsylvania University, '03; served at the N.Y.K.'s Seattle Branch; the Chief of Mogi Trading Dept. and Mng.-Dir. of Mogi Co. till its liquidation. Add. Osaki, near Tokyo. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 613](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n613/mode/1up))

**Naito, Kyukan** Crown Mem. House of Peers, ex-Pres. of Nippon Petroleum Co., Dir. of Central Pet. Co., etc.; b. 1859 in Niigata-ken; elected Mem. of Niigata Pref. Assembly at his 20th year; M.P. in '94; organized Nippon Pet. Co. in '88; despatched on official mission to inspect petroleum business to America and Europe in '97 and again to U.S.A. in '04. Add. Azabu Zaimokucho, Tokyo. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 613](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n613/mode/1up))

**Nakagawa, Shosa** former Vice-Pres. of Tokyo Subway Co. (resigned '27); b. 1881; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '05; Per. Secretary to the Minister of Com. the same year; Secy. of the Govt. R'ly. Board till '20; Dir. Traffic Bureau, R'ly Dept., '20-24; Vice-Minister of Railways in '24. Add. Ushigome Kita-machi, Tokyo. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 613](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n613/mode/1up))

**Nakai, Reisaku** Chief of Government Iron Foundry; b. 1879 in Kumamoto; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '03; entered the Dept. of Agr. & Com. where he was Secy. of one Bureau after another; Dir. of Patent Bureau and then of Forestry Bureau; later Vice-Min. of Agr. & Com.; present post in '25. Add. Yawata, Fukuoka-ken. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 613](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n613/mode/1up))

**Nakajima, Kumakichi** Baron (2nd), Mem. House of Peers (since '04), Mng.-Dir. of Toyo Iron Foundry, etc.; b. 1872 in Yokohama; son of late Baron Nobuyuki Nakajima, the first Chairman of the House of Representatives and Minister to Rome; grad. from the Tokyo Higher Commercial School and entered the stock exchange, '97; private secretary to late Prince Katsura and Marquis Saionji, as Premiers, '02-05; travelled abroad, '05; is interested in all the undertakings of the Furukawa family. Add. Ushigome Yakuoji, Tokyo. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 613](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n613/mode/1up))

**Nakano, Teppei** Mng.-Dir. of Japan Petroleum Co., Ltd.; b. 186[illegible] in Niigata-ken; grad. from Law, Waseda Univ., '91, and has since been connected with the Co. Add. Nishi Okubo, Tokyo. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 613](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n613/mode/1up))

**Nakayama, Taichi** Mem. of Osaka Chamber of Commerce, Pres. of Platon Stationery Co., Prop. of Taiyodo Co., toilet manufacturer, and its chemical institute; b. 1881 in Yamaguchi-ken. Add. Naniwa-ku Mizusaki-cho, Osaka. — p. 40 ([scan leaf 613](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n613/mode/1up))

**Nakazato, Juji** Vice-Adm. (ret.), Pres. of the Karafuto Petroleum Co. Ltd. created in 1925 as semi-official organ for exploiting the oil field conceded by the Soviet Government in '24; b. 1871 in Yamagata-ken; grad. from the Naval Cadet School, '93; steadily promoted till he was appointed a senior staff officer of the General Staff as a Rear-Adm. in 1918; Vice-Adm., '24, and appointed Commander of the Maizuru Naval Port. Add. Aoyama Minami-machi, Tokyo. — p. 41 ([scan leaf 614](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n614/mode/1up))

**Nemoto, Sho** ex-M.P., Dir. of Karafuto Petroleum Oil Co. and Teikoku Oil Co., Aud. of Dye-Stuff Manufacturing Co.; b. 1851 in Ibaraki-ken; studied in America; sent by the Government to Mexico and Brazil to inquire into emigration affairs, '03; has actively espoused the cause of free education and anti-juvenile smoking and drinking. Add. Mita Shikoku-machi, Tokyo. — p. 41 ([scan leaf 614](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n614/mode/1up))

**Nezu, Kaichiro** Crown Mem. House of Peers (nom. '27), Pres. Union Beer Brewing Co., Dir. of Tobu R'ly Co., eto.; b. 1860 in Yamanashi-ken; took active part in village and provincial affairs; was one of the business-men who visited U.S.A. in the fall of '09 at the special invitation of American Chambers of Commerce; M.P. for some time. Add. Aoyama Minami-cho, Tokyo. — p. 41 ([scan leaf 614](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n614/mode/1up))

**Nomura, Ryutaro** D. Eng. (Japan), Pres. Tokyo Subway Co.; b. 1859 at Ogaki; grad. from Civil Eng., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '81; Eng. of Tokyo Prefectural Office, '81-86; studied in Europe and America as R'ly Engineer, '96-98; promoted to Chief-Eng., '10; Vice-Pres. of Imp. Govt. R'lys, '12; Pres. of the South Manchuria R'ly, '19-20. Add. Akasaka Shinsaka-machi, Tokyo. — p. 41 ([scan leaf 614](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n614/mode/1up))

**Obayashi, Kenshiro** Mng.-Dir. of the Obayashi-gumi, Ltd., Contractors for Civil Engineering and Architecture; b. 1885 in Fukushima-ken; grad. in architecture at Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '11. Add. Mikage-machi, Hyogo-ken. — p. 41 ([scan leaf 614](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n614/mode/1up))

**Okamoto, Sakura** Pres. of Toho Gas Co. (Nagoya) & Dir. of Tokyo Gas Co., etc.; b. 1878 in Hyogo pref.; grad. from engineering college, Tokyo Imp. Univ.; is authority on gas industry. Add. Nagoya. — p. 41 ([scan leaf 614](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n614/mode/1up))

**Okawa, Heizaburo** Pres. Fuji Paper Mill, etc.; b. 1861 in Tokyo; married daughter of Visc. Shibusawa. Made a tour of inspection in Europe and America several times. Add. Mukojima Kommecho, Tokyo. — p. 41 ([scan leaf 614](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n614/mode/1up))

**Okura, Kimmochi** Baron, ex-Dir. of the South Manchuria R'ly; 3rd son and heir of the late Gen. Okura; grad. from Civil Eng., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '04; Eng. in the R'ly Dept.; Vice-Chief of the Transportation Dept. the S.M.R. and then Dir. of the Co., '25-28; visited Europe and U.S.A., '29. Add. Dairen. — p. 41 ([scan leaf 614](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n614/mode/1up))

**Saito, Ryoei** D.L., Dir. of South Manchuria R'ly. Co.; b. 1880 in Fukushima-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., politics, class '08; passed Dip. and Con. Exam., '10; Eleve-Con. in China, '10; in Ottawa, '14; Con. at Fuchow, '15; Sec. at Head Office, '17; Jr. Commissioner at the 1st congress of League of Nations at Geneva; 2nd Emb. Sec. at Washington in '20; Sec. at Head Office, '22; Dir. of Commercial Bureau, '27; present post in July '27. Add. Dairen. — p. 41 ([scan leaf 614](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n614/mode/1up))

**Shiohara, Matasaku** Dir. of Sankyo Kabushiki Kaisha; b. 1880 in Yokohama; studied at Yokohama Commercial School and Yokohama English and Japanese Language School; started drug business in '99; went to America to study drug business. Add. Azabu Iigura-kata-machi, Tokyo. — p. 41 ([scan leaf 614](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n614/mode/1up))

**Shiraiwa, Ryuhei** Mng.-Dir. of Toa Industrial Co., Dir. of Nisshin (Japan-China) S.S. Co.; b. 1870 in Okayama-ken; studied at Nisshin Boeki Kenkyujo (Japan-China Trading Institute), Shanghai; attached to the Imperial Headquarters as Military Interpreter in the '94-95 War. Add. Aoyama Minami-cho, Tokyo. — p. 42 ([scan leaf 615](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n615/mode/1up))

**Soma, Hanji** M.A., Pres. of Meiji Sugar Co., Formosa; b. 1869 at Inuyama; non-commissioned officer, '85-90; studied at Tokyo Higher Technical School; then in Berlin and Michigan, devoting himself to chemical researches about sugar; taught at his alma mater, '03-04; chemist to Formosan Government-Gen.; Dir. of the present Co., est. '06. Add. Shiba Park, Tokyo. — p. 42 ([scan leaf 615](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n615/mode/1up))

**Takagi, Rokuro** Japanese Representative of Han-Yeh-Ping Iron & Coal Mining Co.; b. 1880 in Miyagi-ken; had only middle school education before entering Mitsui Bussan Kaisha in '00; was despatched to China by the Co. as a commercial student; took part in Boxers' Trouble as Military Interpreter; later served at Mitsui's Branches at Hankow and Shanghai; left the Co. in '11 on the establishment of the I. & C. Co. Add. Ushigome Nijukkicho, Tokyo. — p. 42 ([scan leaf 615](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n615/mode/1up))

**Takashima, Kikujiro** Dir. of Oji Paper Mill; b. 1875 in Fukuoka-ken; grad. from Tokyo Higher Commercial School, '00; entered the Osaka Shosen Kaisha; removed to Mitsui Bussan Kaisha, '03; to the present Co., '12. Add. Takinogawa, near Tokyo. — p. 42 ([scan leaf 615](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n615/mode/1up))

**Tanaka, Jiro** Mng.-Dir. of Nippon Petroleum Co.; b. 1873 at Saga-ken; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '98; passed the higher civil service examination; Chief of the Bureau of Post & Telegraphs of the Communication Dept., '15; represented Japan at the International Wireless Conference held at Berlin, '06 and '08; retired from the official service in '17. Add. Chojamaru, Kami Osaki, Tokyo. — p. 42 ([scan leaf 615](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n615/mode/1up))

**Tanaka, Son** Dir. of Oriental Compressor Co.; adopted son of Count Tanaka, ex-Minister of Imperial Household; b. 1864 in Fukushima, brother of Sei Iwagami; studied law and economics in France, '91-00; Lecturer at Peers' School, '01; M.P. from Kochi in '02. Add. Kojimachi Motozono-cho, Tokyo. — p. 42 ([scan leaf 615](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n615/mode/1up))

**Tawara, Yutaka** Dir. of Mitsubishi Paper Dept., etc.; b. 1871 in Hyogo-ken; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '95; entered the Mitsui Firm and remained in the service till '08; Govt. Commercial Commissioner in London, '08-10; and re-entered the Co. Add. Shibuya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 42 ([scan leaf 615](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n615/mode/1up))

**Tsukakoshi, Utaro** Mng.-Dir. of Nippon Chemical Fertilizer Mfg. Co.; b. 1879 in Tokyo; studied pharmacy and chemistry at Tokyo Pharmaceutical School and at the Institute of Technology, Boston, U.S.A.; appointed Chief of Dry Distillation Works, and subsequently of Chemical Laboratory of the Karafuto Govt.; left the official service, '12; formed Nippon Chemical Pulp Co. in '13 and later the present Co. Add. Ichigaya Tanimachi, Tokyo. — p. 42 ([scan leaf 615](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n615/mode/1up))

**Tsuneto, Noritaka** D. Agr., Pres. of Rasa Island Phosphate Co.; b. 1857 in Oita-ken; grad. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., '83; Expert at Dept. of Agr.; Prof. at Agr. & Forest. School, at Morioka, and then at Kagoshima; Expert of Formosan Govt.; retired from official service, '04. Add. Ushigome Wakamiya-cho, Tokyo. — p. 42 ([scan leaf 615](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n615/mode/1up))

**Uyemura, Chozaburo** Mng.-Dir. of Japan Beer Brewery Co., etc.; b. 1862 in Tokyo; was for some years an official of the Departments of Agr. & Com. and of Communications. Add. Aoyama Minami-cho, Tokyo. — p. 42 ([scan leaf 615](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n615/mode/1up))

**Watanabe, Katsusaburo** ex-Pres. of Oriental Development Co.; b. 187[illegible] in Okayama-ken; grad. Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '96 and has been in service of local Govt., lastly Gov. of Tokushima-ken; Dir. of Local Adm. Bureau, Home Office; Mayor of Yokohama, '24-25; Pres. of Oriental Development Co., '25-28. Add. Seoul. — p. 42 ([scan leaf 615](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n615/mode/1up))

**Yajima, Eisuke** Prop. of the Yajima-gumi, Pres. of the Yajima Filature Co., etc.; b. 1869 in Yamanashi-ken; was granted a silver medal by the Min. of Agr. & Com., 08, in recognition of his distinguished services to silk industry; was on the Committee of the Central Sericultural Association; Mem. of the House of Peers representing highest taxpayers of Yamanashi-ken, '09-15. Add. Midori-cho, Kofu City. — p. 43 ([scan leaf 616](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n616/mode/1up))

**Yamada, Sanjiro** Mng.-Dir. Asahi Glass Co. and Shoko Glass Co.; b. 1870 in Kagoshima-ken; grad. at Tokyo Higher Technical School, '96; sent to Belgium by the Dept. of Agr. & Com. in '00 to study the glass manufacture; served in Agr. & Com. Dept. for years, Toyo Glass Co., '05-07, Asahi Glass Co. since '08, visited Europe and America to inspect glass industry '08 and '12. Add. Aoyama Minami-cho, Tokyo. — p. 43 ([scan leaf 616](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n616/mode/1up))

**Yamanari, Kyoroku** Dir. of Toyo Sugar Mfg. Co.; b. 1873 in Okayama-ken; grad. in '91 from the Accountant's School, formerly attached to Tokyo Higher Commercial School, and entered the 34th Bank to be made Mng. of its Keelung Branch; removed in '[illegible]5 to the Bank of Taiwan and had charge of its Kobe and Osaka Branches; next Assistant-Chief of the General Affairs Dept. of its Head Office and finally a Mng.-Dir. till '20. Add. Nakano, near Tokyo. — p. 43 ([scan leaf 616](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n616/mode/1up))

**Yamaoka, Juntaro** Chairman of Dirs. of Osaka Iron Works, ex-Pres. of Osaka Chamber of Commerce; b. 1866 in Kanazawa; official of the Dept. of Communications, '92-98; entered O.S.K., '98; its Section Chief, '99; Manager, '08; Dir. and Vice-Pres., '14-20, combining several other posts. Add. Tennoji, Higashi-nari-ku, Osaka. — p. 43 ([scan leaf 616](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n616/mode/1up))

**Yoshida, Hideto** Mng.-Dir. of Nisshin Printing Co.; b. 1889 in Kumamoto-ken; grad. at Waseda Univ., '15; Mitsubishi Co. for years, and now shared in Nikka Pottery Co. and Tokyo Standard Shoes Mfg. Co. as Dir. Add. Hongo Yayoi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 43 ([scan leaf 616](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n616/mode/1up))

**Yuasa, Shichizaemon** Pres. of Yuasa Co., manufacturer of hardware, and Yuasa Dry Battery Co., Dir. of Shin Keihan Railway Co. and Teikoku Oil Co.; b. 1877 in Tokyo; adopted by Yuasa family as son-in-law. Add. Gojo Yanaginobaba, Kyoto. — p. 43 ([scan leaf 616](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n616/mode/1up))

### Journalism & Publishing

**Abiko, Kyutaro** a prominent Japanese journalist in San Francisco; b. 1864 in Niigata-ken; and m. sister of Miss Tsuda in '08; edu. at California University; runs the Nichi-bei (Japan-America) News, besides acting as Dir. of the Nichi-bei Bank, etc., and has also founded the Yamato Colony. Add. Nichi-bei Office, 'Frisco. — p. 43 ([scan leaf 616](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n616/mode/1up))

**Akizuki, Satsuo** Dir. of a Japanese daily at Seoul; b. 1858 in Miyazaki-ken; edu. at former Law Coll., Justice Dept. in '84; further studied in Belgium and Germany, '85-91; Counsellor of For. Office, '92; Consul at Fusan, '96; Legation Secretary at Paris, '99; to Petrograd in '02; Minister Resident to Sweden-Norway, '04; Minister at Brussels, '07; Amb. at Vienna, '09-14; Dir. of the Yomiuri for a while; placed on the Peace Envoy staff, '19. Add. Ushigome Benten-cho, Tokyo. — p. 43 ([scan leaf 616](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n616/mode/1up))

**Arakawa, Goro** Chief Edit. of Chugoku Shimbun (Hiroshima), M.P. and ex-Parl. Jr. Secy. of Communication Dept.; b. 1865 in Hiroshima; grad. from Kokugakuin and Nippon Univ.; established a girls' school in Hiroshima. Add. "Chugoku Shimbun," Hiroshima. — p. 43 ([scan leaf 616](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n616/mode/1up))

**Date, Gen-iohiro** Gen.-Mng. of former Toho News Agency; b. 1878 in Shimane-ken; grad. from the Doshisha Univ., Kyoto; entered the Kokumin Shimbun, '00; Kokusai News Agency, '15; Chief editor of the Yomiuri, '19; established the Eastern News Agency, '22; represented Japan at the international journalistic conference at Washington, '27; made tour several times in China and Europe and America and served in the Daily Telegraph, London, for several months, '10. Add. Shibuya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 43 ([scan leaf 616](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n616/mode/1up))

**Gonda, Yasunosuke** writer; b. 1887 in Tokyo; edu. at Foreign Language School (German course), and next at Tokyo Imp. Univ., in Literature, making fine art his speciality, class '13. Is also connected with Ohara Social Research Institute. Works: Economy and Civilization, Applied Art, etc. Add. Otsuka Kubocho, Tokyo. — p. 44 ([scan leaf 617](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n617/mode/1up))

**Hasegawa; Manjiro** (penname Nyozekan), journalist and socialist writer, b. 1875 in Tokyo; was once on the staff of the Nippon, Osaka Asahi and other journals; started a monthly organ "We" in '18 with his socialist friends. Works: London, Criticism of Modern State, Modern Society Criticized, etc. Add. Nakano, near Tokyo. — p. 44 ([scan leaf 617](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n617/mode/1up))

**Hiratsuka, Akiko** Miss, author and a "new woman"; b. 1886 in Tokyo; edu. at Ochanomizu Girls' High School and Women's Univ., in Domestic Economy. Works: Women's Life of Today, Men and Women of the Day, Rights of Women and Children. Add. Sendagaya, near Tokyo. — p. 44 ([scan leaf 617](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n617/mode/1up))

**Ichiriki, Kenjiro** Pres. of Kahoku Shimpo (Sendai); b. 1886 in Miyagi-ken; grad. at 2nd Higher School in '83; entered Kahoku Shimbun and became its Pres. Add. Sendai. — p. 44 ([scan leaf 617](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n617/mode/1up))

**Ishikawa, Kammei** formerly editor of the Jiji Shimpo, Mem. of Board of Council of Keio Univ.; b. 1859 in Mito; grad. from Keio Univ. where he taught at a time. Add. Azabu, Tokyo. — p. 44 ([scan leaf 617](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n617/mode/1up))

**Ishikawa, Sanshiro** socialist writer; b. 1876 in Saitama; edu. at the Chuo Univ. in Law, class '02; has taken active part in socialist movement resulting in his flight to Europe in '13; returned home in '20; again went abroad in '20. Works: Philosopher Carpenter, Western Socialist Movement, New Researches in Japanese Mythology, etc. — p. 44 ([scan leaf 617](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n617/mode/1up))

**Itakura, Takuzo** Prof. at Keio Univ., Editor of the Jiji Shimpo; b. in Hiroshima-ken; grad. from Keiogijuku, '03; visited Europe and America several times. Add. Hiratsuka Togoshi, near Tokyo. — p. 44 ([scan leaf 617](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n617/mode/1up))

**Ito, Yonejiro** Pres. of the Japan Times since '25; b. 1861 in Uwajima; grad. from the Osaka Semmon Gakko, and going over to America in '8[illegible] studied at the Michigan Univ.; returned home, '94; entered the N.Y.K., '96; Agent at Shanghai and London; Mng.-Dir. of the Co., '16-20; Pres. till '24. Add. Kamakura. — p. 44 ([scan leaf 617](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n617/mode/1up))

**Kawakami, Paul Kiyoshi** political writer; b. 1874 in Kochi-ken; edu. at Aoyama Gakuin Coll. and Kokumin Eigakkai (English Language School in Tokyo); was on the editorial staff of the Yorozu, '01-03; went to U.S.A. in '03 and studied at Iowa Univ. where he was granted fellowship; was connected with the East & West Newsagency (Chicago) for some years; was special correspondent for the Tokyo Nichi-Nichi, '23-26; has written a number of works on Japan's foreign relations. Add. Washington, U.S.A. — p. 44 ([scan leaf 617](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n617/mode/1up))

**Komatsu, Midori** Journalist connected with "Chugai Shogyo"; b. 1865 in Tokyo; grad. from Michigan Univ., '90; Translator and Secretary of the Foreign Office till '07; Dir. Foreign Affairs Bureau of Korean Government, '11-17. Add. Shiba Park, Tokyo. — p. 44 ([scan leaf 617](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n617/mode/1up))

**Kushida, Tamizo** social writer and member of the Ohara Social Institute at Osaka which sent him to Germany and England to study his speciality, '20-23; was once connected with the Osaka Asahi; edu. at elective course, Tokyo Imp. Univ., Law. Add. Ohara Institute, Tokyo. — p. 44 ([scan leaf 617](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n617/mode/1up))

**Maida, Minoru** D.L., B.A., M.A., journalist connected with the Tokyo Asahi and specializes in Diplomacy, a thesis which won him the doctorate from Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1878 in Kurume; edu. at Kokumin English School and Oregon State Univ., class '00, and further at Iowa and California [illegible] Universities. Lecturer on Diplomacy at the Gov. Coll. of Commerce. Works: Latest Diplomacy of the World, etc. Add. Zoshigaya, Tokyo. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 618](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n618/mode/1up))

**Masuda, Gi-ichi** M.P., Prop. of "Jitsugyo-no-Nihon" and various popular magazines, Dir. of several Cos.; b. 1869 at Takata; grad. from Waseda Semmon Gakko, '93; toured round the world, '14. Add. Koishikawa Haramachi, Tokyo. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 618](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n618/mode/1up))

**Matsumoto, Kumpei** M.P., a political writer, ex-Parl. Counsellor of the Navy Dept.; b. 1870 in Shizuoka-ken; studied in U.S.A.; was returned to the Lower House four times. Add. Omori Shimmei-yama, Tokyo. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 618](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n618/mode/1up))

**Matsushita, Yusaburo** Pres. of Yamato Shimbun Pub. Co., Ltd., a mine-owner; b. 1890 in Tokyo; grad. at the German Law Course of the Law College of Tokyo Imp. Univ.; on the death of his adoptive father, succeeded to the presidency of Yamato Shimbun. Add. Ushigome Minami-Enoki-cho, Tokyo. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 618](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n618/mode/1up))

**Mitsunaga, Hoshio** Pres. of the Nippon Dempo Tsushinsha (Japan Telegraphic News Agency), Ltd.; b. 1866 in Kumamoto-ken; commenced his career as a politician; then a staff writer of the Osaka Asahi and acted as its war correspondent during the Japan-China war; later an official of the Formosan Government-General; afterwards established, with Shinji Gondo, the Nippon Dempo Tsushinsha. Add. Harajuku, Sendagaya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 618](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n618/mode/1up))

**Miyake, Yasu-ko** Mrs., author; b. 1890 in Kyoto; edu. at Ochanomizu Girls' School, and is a well-known contributor to journals; has written a number of stories. Add. Tokyo. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 618](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n618/mode/1up))

**Motoyama, Hikoichi** Pres. of the Osaka Mainichi and Tokyo Nichi-Nichi, Dir. of Nankai R'ly. Co., Meiji Life Ins. Co., &c.; b. 1853 in Kumamoto; grad. from Keio-gijuku; once served in the Fujita Firm; established the Osaka Mainichi in '92. Add. Osaka Mainichi, Osaka. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 618](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n618/mode/1up))

**Murayama, Ryuhei** Pres. of the Asahi Shimbun (Tokyo and Osaka); b. 1850 at Wakayama; has by his shrewd business management raised the two Asahi to the present stage of importance; once sat in Parliament for Osaka-fu. Add. Osaka Asahi Office, Osaka. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 618](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n618/mode/1up))

**Murobuse, Takanobu** social and democratic writer; b. 1889 in Izu. edu. at Meiji Univ. which he left unfinished; started journalistic career connected with the Jiji, the Asahi, and other dailies and periodicals; sent to Europe by the Kaizo magazine (Reconstruction) in '20 and succeeded in bringing Einstein over to lecture. Works: Lecture on Democracy; About Capitalism; Guild Socialism, Decay of Civilization, etc. Add. Omori, near Tokyo. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 618](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n618/mode/1up))

**Noma, Seiji** publisher of monthly magazines of largest circulation; b. 1879 in Gumma-ken; grad. at the Institute for Training of Teachers middle school, '04; teacher of Okinawa middle school for years; is since '09 publishing magazines and now nine monthly magazines are published by him the widest circulation of which is "King" for over 1,500,000 per month. Add. Koishikawa Otowa, Tokyo. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 618](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n618/mode/1up))

**Ohashi, Shintaro** Crown Mem. House of Peers, Prop. of the Hakubunkan, a well-known publishing house, Pres. of Seoul Elec. Co., etc.; b. 1863, eldest son of the late Sahei, founder of the house; studied at the Dojinsha and was his father's right-hand man in carrying the publishing business to the prosperity it attained; founded the Ohashi Library; sat for a while in the House of Representatives for Tokyo; travelled abroad in '09; was on the businessmen's party that visited England in '21. Add. Koj. Kami-Rokubancho, Tokyo. — p. 45 ([scan leaf 618](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n618/mode/1up))

**Oka, Minoru** D.L., Dir. of the Tokyo Nichi-Nichi; b. 1873 in Nara-ken; grad. Politics, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '98; passed higher civil service exam. same year; Councillor of Legislation Bureau and then Secy. of the Dept. of Agr. & Com.; later Dir. of Trade & Ind. Bureau, the same Dept.; Chief Commissioner at the Liege World Exh. in '95; attended various international economic and labor conferences; retired from Govt. service and entered the Tokyo Nichi-Nichi Shimbun, '25. Add. Shirokane Sanko-cho, Tokyo. — p. 46 ([scan leaf 619](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n619/mode/1up))

**Sahara, Tokusuke** Shanghai correspondent of the Jiji, Asahi, and Mainichi; Dir. of the Shanghai Mercury, Ltd.; b. 1874 in Tokyo; edu. at Keio Univ.; on graduation joined the editorial staff of the Jiji Shimpo; despatched to Shanghai as its special correspondent, '99; became Sub-Editor of Shanghai Mercury (English daily), '04; Dir. of the Shanghai Mercury, Ltd., '17; is a member of the Inst. of Journalists in England since '07. Add. Miller Road, Shanghai. — p. 46 ([scan leaf 619](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n619/mode/1up))

**Shiba, Teikichi** former Edit. of the Yorodzu Choho, M.P.; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., in English literature (elective course), '94, and also at Oxford; was for long editorial staff writer of the Yorodzu; later its Mng.-Editor and then Chief Editor; started the Tokyo Taisei Shimbun, '25; returned to the Lower House in '26 by-election and again in '28 election. Add. Yoyogi Sanya, Tokyo. — p. 46 ([scan leaf 619](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n619/mode/1up))

**Shimomura, Hiroshi** D.L., Mng.-Dir. of the Tokyo Asahi; b. 1875; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '98, and was long connected with postal service as Dir. Postal Money Order Board; Dir. of Civil Adm., Formosa, '15-21; became connected with the Asahi, '21. Add. Tokyo Asahi. — p. 46 ([scan leaf 619](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n619/mode/1up))

**Sugimura, Kotaro** (nom de plume Sojinkan), author and journalist on the staff of the Tokyo Asahi; b. 1872 in Wakayama; first was school teacher and then translator at U.S. Embassy, before he entered Asahi in '04; travelled several times in the West; has written a number of books. Add. Abiko, Chiba-ken, near Tokyo. — p. 46 ([scan leaf 619](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n619/mode/1up))

**Takekoshi, Yosaburo** Crown Mem. House of Peers (nom. '22), author and journalist; b. 1865 in Tokyo; studied at the Dojinsha and Keio-gijuku; was on the staff of the Jiji; Pers. Sec. of Marquis Saionji, '98; app. in '21, Chief Compiler of Imp. Household Dept.; was M.P. before he entered the Dept. Works: "History of 2,500 Years of Japan" and other works. Add. Higashi-Okubo, near Tokyo. — p. 46 ([scan leaf 619](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n619/mode/1up))

**Tokutomi, Iichiro** journalist, founder of the Kokumin Shimbun, Crown Mem. House of Peers (nom. '11); b. 1863 at Kumamoto; edu. at the Doshisha; published about '86 "The Future Japan"; issued in '87 a monthly magazine "The Friend of the Nation"; started in '90 the "Kokumin" (Nation), a daily paper and an English monthly the "Far East"; Counsellor of the Home Office, '97; visited Europe and America, '96; founded a philanthropic institute Aoyama Kaikan by donating his own mansion, '22; retired from journalism early in '29 severing his connection with the "Kokumin" of which he was proprietor and editor since its foundation. Works: "Yoshida Sho-in", "History of Modern Japan", etc. Add. Omori, Tokyo. — p. 46 ([scan leaf 619](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n619/mode/1up))

**Uyeno, Seiichi** Vice-Pres. of the Asahi Shimbun, Tokyo and Osaka; b. 1882 in Osaka; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '07 and took to journalism. Add. Osaka Asahi, Osaka. — p. 46 ([scan leaf 619](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n619/mode/1up))

**Yamada, Waka** lady writer interested in social and labor problems; b. 1878 in Kanagawa-ken; edu. in American schools for six years supporting herself by hard work. Works: Translation of Magdalene Mark's "Women", Youthful Love and Intellectual Awakening, Female Emancipation and Sex Education, Woman, Man and Mother, Investigation of White Slavery, etc. Add. Yotsuya Minami-iga-cho, Tokyo. — p. 46 ([scan leaf 619](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n619/mode/1up))

**Yamagata, Iso-o** former Edit. of the Seoul Press; b. 1869 in Shiga-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; was long on the staff of the Yorodzu before taking up editorship of the Seoul Press in '10; toured abroad, '23. Add. Takata, Tokyo. — p. 47 ([scan leaf 620](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n620/mode/1up))

**Yanagida, Kunio** editorial adviser of the Asahi Shimbun, Lecturer of the Keio Univ.; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ.; was once Chief Secretary of the House of Peers; is an authority on folklore. Add. Keio Univ., Tokyo. — p. 47 ([scan leaf 620](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n620/mode/1up))

**Yano, Fumio** Vice-Pres. of Osaka Mainichi Shimbun, author, and retired diplomat; b. 1850 in Oita prefecture; grad. from Keio Gijuku, '70; Secretary to the Treasury, '17; resigned in '81 and took charge of the Hochi Shimbun; took part in the formation of the Kaishinto (progressive party); made tour in Europe and America, '86-88; Master of Ceremonies and Chief of the Bureau of Imperial Tombs, '90-96; Minister to China, '97-98; placed on the retired list in '99. Works: "Keikoku Bidan". "Ukishiro Monogatari" (a marine novel); "New Society," etc. Add. Harajuku, Tokyo. — p. 47 ([scan leaf 620](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n620/mode/1up))

**Zumoto, Motosada** ex-M.P., journalist, Chairman of International Press Association; b. 1862 in Tottori; grad. from Sapporo Agr. Coll., '84; was long on the staff of the Japan Mail; entered civil service for a short while and was twice appointed Per. Sec. to the late Prince Ito when he was Premier; a resident correspondent of the London Standard during the Russo-Japanese War; started the Japan Times as the only English paper edited by Japanese in '97; conducted the Seoul Press, '06-09; started in '09 the Oriental Information Agency in New York City; but returned to Tokyo in '10 to resume control of the Japan Times, '10-14; started in '16 the Herald of Asia which was suspended after the Earthquake; was attached to the Siberian Expeditionary Army, '22; made a tour to U.S.A. with Baron Shibusawa in '15 and in '21. Add. Herald Office, Kojimachi-ku Uchisaiwaicho 1-chome, Tokyo. — p. 47 ([scan leaf 620](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n620/mode/1up))

### Labour

**Akamatsu, Katsumaro** staff member of the Japan Federation of Labour; b. 1894 at Tokuyama; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Law, class '19; was once writer of the Kaiho and the Oriental Economist (Tokyo). Works: Social Revolution, etc. Add. Ebaramachi, near Tokyo. — p. 47 ([scan leaf 620](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n620/mode/1up))

**Arahata, Katsuzo** socialist and labor leader; b. 1887 at Yokohama; after finishing common school he earned his bread as messenger boy, apprentice, pedlar of Socialist literature. Later he undertook with others the publication of labor and socialist organs and has been imprisoned three times on charge of disturbing public order. Has translated works of Sidney Webb and Bernard Shaw, published "A Socialist Dictionary," etc., besides issuing some original works. — p. 47 ([scan leaf 620](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n620/mode/1up))

**Aso, Hisashi** a labor leader and Executive of the Japan Labor-Farm Party; b. 1891 in Oita-ken; edu. at Third High Sch. and Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Law; on the staff of the Tokyo Nichi-Nichi, '17-19; joined the Yu-ai-kai Labor Ass'n. and has since taken prominent part in upholding the cause of labor. Add. Komagome Kichijoji, Tokyo. — p. 47 ([scan leaf 620](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n620/mode/1up))

**Kagawa, Toyohiko** Socialist worker and friend of labourers; b. 1889 in Kagawa-ken; studied at the Meiji Gaku-in but left it unfinished; at Princeton, '14-17; returned home to work for labourers; married a Christian girl, his follower and assistant; attended the Christian Convention at Boston, Dec. '24, and lectured in England, Germany and India, '24-25. Works: "Shisen-wo-koete" (English title "Before the Dawn"), his autobiographical novel, '21, Taiyo-wo-irumono" (Shooting at the Sun), '22; "Kabe-no-Koe" (Voice from the Wall), '24. Add. Fuki-ai-Shimmachi, Kobe. — p. 47 ([scan leaf 620](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n620/mode/1up))

**Katayama, Sen** M.A., B.D., pioneer socialist in Japan, now on the Executive Committee of the Third International of the Soviet Government; b. 1860 in Tsuyama; edu. at an American university; after returning home established Kingsley Hall and started an earnest labor and socialist propaganda which invited active persecution from the Government and made him flee to America about fifteen years ago. Works: Labor Movement in Japan (Chicago), Autobiography. Add. Moscow. — p. 48 ([scan leaf 621](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n621/mode/1up))

**Morito, Tatsuo** member of the Ohara Social Research Institute at Osaka; b. 1885 in Hiroshima-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Law, class '10; appointed Assist.-Prof. at his alma mater; incriminated in '20 for having published Kuropotkin's socialistic theories and imprisoned; sent to Europe by the Institute and stayed there, '21-23. Works: History of Socialist Views, Sketch of Kuropotkin, etc. Add. Ohara Institute, Osaka. — p. 48 ([scan leaf 621](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n621/mode/1up))

**Nakanishi, Inosuke** socialist, labor leader and writer; b. 1893 in Kyoto-fu; with no regular education, worked his way as mechanic and journalist; has been sentenced three times for disturbing order; an active member of the Transport Labor Union. Works: Growth in the Red Soil, Look Behind Your Back, etc. Add. Yoyogi Sanya, near Tokyo. — p. 48 ([scan leaf 621](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n621/mode/1up))

**Ouchi, Hyoe** Mng. of Ohara Social Research Institute, Assis.-Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., his alma mater which post he had once to resign for having inserted an article on Kuropotkin in a monthly organ of the Univ. Economic Dept. of which he was a nominal editor; restored to the chair when he returned home from his two years' sojourn abroad. Works: Conflict between Labor and Capital in British Parliament. Add. Okubo Hyakunin-machi, Tokyo. — p. 48 ([scan leaf 621](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n621/mode/1up))

**Sakai, Toshihiko** (nom de plume Kosen), socialist; b. 1870 in Fukuoka-ken; studied at Tokyo Higher School; started in life as a newspaper man; published for some time a socialistic organ; rose to prominence as a socialistic pacifist during the Russo-Japanese War; was imprisoned on charge of disturbing social order on several occasions. Several translations and original works. Add. Koj. Motozono-cho, Tokyo. — p. 48 ([scan leaf 621](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n621/mode/1up))

**Sano, Gaku** a socialist and ex-lecturer at Waseda; fled to Russia in '23 being implicated in a "Communist" movement; b. 1892 in Oita-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., in Politics, class '17; further studied at the Univ. Hall. Works: Marx or Kant (translation), Investigation of Russian Economy (vol. 1), etc. Add. Moscow. — p. 48 ([scan leaf 621](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n621/mode/1up))

**Suzuki, Bunji** Labor leader, Pres. of the General Federation of Labor in Japan; b. 1885 in Miyagi-ken; grad. from Politics, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '90. Before he organized in '12 the Yu-ai-kai, the first Friendly Society after Western model, he served at a printing shop, a newspaper office and was also manager of the Japan Unitarian Association. Represented Labor at Geneva Conference (1924). Add. Totsuka Suwa, near Tokyo. — p. 48 ([scan leaf 621](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n621/mode/1up))

**Suzuki, Mosaburo** labor leader; b. '93 in Aichi-ken; grad. at politics, Waseda Univ.; writer for Hochi, '15-19, for Taisho Nichi-Nichi, '19-20, for Tokyo Nichi-Nichi and Osaka Mainichi, '22-28; visited America, Europe and Russia; joined in '28 the organization of the Proletarian Party of which he is chief secretary. Add. Kamata-machi, near Tokyo. — p. 48 ([scan leaf 621](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n621/mode/1up))

**Takahashi, Kamekichi** financial writer; b. 1892 at Tokuyama; was a shop boy till about '20; edu. at Waseda Univ., in Commerce, class '16; entered the service of Kuhara Mining Co.; was on the staff of the Oriental Economist, '18-26. Works: Practical Knowledge of Economy. Add. Aoyama, Tokyo. — p. 49 ([scan leaf 622](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n622/mode/1up))

**Yamakawa, Hitoshi** socialist leader whose magazine article has been a signal for “leftward” movement in Japan, and who has been imprisoned four or five times on charge of disturbing peace, violating press law, etc.; b. 1880 at Kurashiki; edu., only common school; has been active writer in the cause of socialism and started in '19 with his wife a monthly. Works: Lenin and Trotky, Outline of Marx's Capitalism, Labor Movement and Socialism. Is an invalid from consumption. Add. Tarumi, Suma, near Kobe. — p. 49 ([scan leaf 622](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n622/mode/1up))

**Yamakawa, Kikue** wife of above; b. 1890 in Tokyo; studied at Tsuda English College; has written or translated a number of works, besides acting as assistant to her husband. — p. 49 ([scan leaf 622](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n622/mode/1up))

### Lawyers

**Adachi, Shinzaburo** Lawyer in Osaka; b. 1874 in Okayama-ken; grad. from the Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '02, and appointed a probationary judicial officer; left the service soon after and called to the Bar, practicing in Osaka. Add. (Office) 29, Masago-cho, Kita-ku, Osaka. — p. 49 ([scan leaf 622](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n622/mode/1up))

**Aoki, Tetsuji** D.L., Lawyer & Patent Attorney; b. 1876 in Gifu-ken; grad. from Keio, '97; passed the examination for the Bench, '98; a Probationary Judge at Kobe for a few months; dispatched by the alma mater to study Commercial Law at Berlin and Gottingen, '99-02; Prof. at Keio, but left it in '08 to open law office. Add. Shiba Shirokane-Imazato, Tokyo. — p. 49 ([scan leaf 622](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n622/mode/1up))

**Ema, Shun-ichi** Barrister, ex-M.P., mesmerist; b. 1861; studied politics at a private univ. in Tokyo; admitted to the Bar, in '93; afterwards elected Pres. of the Tokyo Barrister's Association; Vice-Pres. of Tokyo Prefectural Assembly, '89. Add. Koishikawa Hakusan-maye, Tokyo. — p. 49 ([scan leaf 622](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n622/mode/1up))

**Fujisawa, Ikunosuke** Lawyer, M.P., adviser to the Minsei party; b. 1859 in Sendai; studied law and became lawyer in '80; distinguished himself in connexion with the Fukushima affair in '83; elected mem. of Sendai Municipal Assembly, and then of Miyagi Prefectural Assembly, of which he was once Chairman; Parliamentary Secy. of Communication Dept., '15; later removed to Home Dept in same capacity; Min. of Com. & Ind., '26-27. Add. Uyenohara, Nakano, near Tokyo. — p. 49 ([scan leaf 622](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n622/mode/1up))

**Hanai, Takuzo** D.L., Barrister, Mem. House of Peers (nom. '22); b. 1868 in Hiroshima-ken; grad. from a private law school in Tokyo, '85; passed Barristers' Examination a year or two later; is an acknowledged authority in criminal affairs; M.P. for several terms and was once Vice-Pres. of House of Representatives. Has written several popular essays on Criminal Law. Add. Kojimachi Nakarokubancho, Tokyo. — p. 49 ([scan leaf 622](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n622/mode/1up))

**Hatoyama, Hideo** D.C.L., Lawyer, formerly Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1884 in Tokyo, younger brother of Ichiro Hatoyama; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., '08; further studied in Germany and France. Add. Koishikawa Kobinata Dai-machi 3-chome, Tokyo. — p. 49 ([scan leaf 622](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n622/mode/1up))

**Hatoyama, Ichiro** Lawyer, M.P. & ex-Chief Secy. of the Cabinet; b. 1883, eldest son of late Dr. Kazuo Hatoyama. Grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '07; began practice at father's office and at same time taught at Waseda; was for years Chairman of Tokyo Municipal Assembly; seceded from Seiyukai when it split and joined Seiyu-Honto Party; returned to Seiyukai in '26; Chief Secretary of Cabinet under Premier Tanaka, '27-29. Add. Koishikawa Otowa-machi, Tokyo. — p. 49 ([scan leaf 622](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n622/mode/1up))

**Horiye, Sen-ichiro** D.L. (Japan), L.L.M. (Michigan), Lawyer, Lecturer at Central Univ. (Tokyo), etc.; b. 1876 in Hiroshima-ken; edu. at Tokyo Hogaku-in (now Central Univ.), class '99, the National English Ins., '01, and at Michigan, '07; passed the Judicial & Barrister's Exam., '02 and served at Dr. Kishi's Office for some years. Works: Does Suicide Nullify Insurance Contracts?, etc. Add. Ushigome Nando-cho, Tokyo. — p. 50 ([scan leaf 623](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n623/mode/1up))

**Isobe, Sho** Lawyer, M.P. & ex-Parl. Councillor of the Justice Dept. ('28-29); b. 1875 in Fukui, adopted by Shiro Isobe; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '99. Add. Honjo Kamezawa-cho, Tokyo. — p. 50 ([scan leaf 623](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n623/mode/1up))

**Iwata, Chuzo** D.L., Lawyer & Dir. of Sumitomo Trust Co. and 3 other concerns; b. 1875 in Yamaguchi-ken; edu. in Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '98; was long a lecturer on English law at Waseda and other universities; was legal adviser to the Bank of Japan, the Nippon Yusen Kaisha, etc. Add. Aoyama Takagi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 50 ([scan leaf 623](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n623/mode/1up))

**Keto, Katsumoto** D.L., Lawyer; b. 1874 in Hyogo-ken; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '98; Assist. Prof. of Kyoto Imp. Univ., '00; Dean of Law College, '14; opened practice '16. Add. Morigu, Hyogo-ken. — p. 50 ([scan leaf 623](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n623/mode/1up))

**Kishi, Sei-ichi** D.L., Barrister, Dir. of Osaka Gas Co., legal adviser to several Govt. and private institutions; b. 1867 at Matsuye; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '87 and began practice in Tokyo; a patron of physical training and Pres. of Japan Physical Culture Association. Add. Shiba Isarago, Tokyo. — p. 50 ([scan leaf 623](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n623/mode/1up))

**Masujima, Rokuichiro** D.L., Lawyer (Barrister of Middle Temple '83), b. 1857 at Hikone; one of the pioneer graduates of Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ. which he finished in '77; opened with several friends the English Law School (now Central Univ.); is a consulting lawyer to many Japanese and foreign establishments. Add. Kojimachi-ku Uchisaiwai-cho, Tokyo. — p. 50 ([scan leaf 623](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n623/mode/1up))

**Miyaoka, Tsunejiro** Lawyer; b. 1866 in Osaka; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '87 and entered Foreign Office, serving at Washington and then Berlin, and then at the Head Office; Min. Plen. in '09; retired and started general practice; app. special correspondent of Carnegie Endowment for International Peace; elected hon; mem. of American Bar Ass'n. and Canadian Bar Ass'n. in '18; visited London and Paris, '24 as guest of the Bar of England and the Bar of Paris; is an associate mem. of the Association of the Bar of the City of New York and local counsel in Japan for U.S. Steel Corporation and other American corporations; also counsel for the N.Y.K., Tokyo Marine & Fire Ins. Co. and several other Japanese corporations. Add. Aoyama Minami-cho, Tokyo. — p. 50 ([scan leaf 623](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n623/mode/1up))

**Nagashima, Washitaro** D.L., Barrister; b. 1868 in Okayama-ken; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '90; Junior Councillor of Justice Dept. which post he resigned '93 and began practice; travelled abroad and was Japanese delegate at the International Parliamentary Conference in '09. Add. Sendagaya Harajuku, Tokyo. — p. 50 ([scan leaf 623](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n623/mode/1up))

**Nakamatsu, Morio** Patent Attorney & Barrister; b. 1865 in Wakayama; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '91; entered the Dept. of Agr. & Com. as Councillor and Patent Examiner; then Sectional Chief of the Bureau; Dir. of Patent Bureau, '09-13. Add. Omori near Tokyo. — p. 50 ([scan leaf 623](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n623/mode/1up))

**Seki, Naohiko** Lawyer, Crown Mem. House of Peers; b. 1857 at Wakayama city; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '83; once editor of the Tokyo Nichi-Nichi; Mem. of Tokyo City Assembly; repeatedly elected M.P. since '90; Deputy Speaker of the Lower House in '29-36 sessions. Add. Kyobashi Kobikicho 1-chome, Tokyo. — p. 50 ([scan leaf 623](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n623/mode/1up))

**Sugawara, Kenji** D.L., Lawyer, mem. of the Osaka Bar; b. 1884 in Hyogo-ken; grad. from German Law, Kyoto Imp. Univ., class '09; Probationary Judge the same year; passed Judicial Service Exam. in '02 and appointed Judge of the Tokyo District Court; at the same time taught at several private law institutions; assist.-prof. at the alma mater, '05; sent abroad for further study, '15-18; Prof. at the alma mater with which he was connected till '24. Add. Okazaki Irie-cho, Kyoto. — p. 51 ([scan leaf 624](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n624/mode/1up))

**Uzawa, Somei** D.L., Barrister, Crown Mem. House of Peers, ex-M.P., Dir. of the Meiji Univ.; b. 1872 in Chiba; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '99; has been practising since then, being reputed as an expert in criminal cases. Add. Sendagaya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 51 ([scan leaf 624](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n624/mode/1up))

**Yamanouchi, Kakusaburo** D.C.L., Lawyer, M.P.; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '98; was Dir. of the Tokyo Court of Appeal, and then Vice-Minister of Justice. Add. Sugamo-machi, near Tokyo. — p. 51 ([scan leaf 624](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n624/mode/1up))

### Learned Persons

**Abe, Jiro** Dr. Litt., Prof. at Tohoku Imp. Univ. (Sendai); b. 1883 in Yamagata-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Philosophy, class '07 and further at the post-graduate course and in Europe (about two years). Works: Essentials of Ethics, Fine Arts, Criticism on some of Nietzsche's Works, etc. Add. Sendai. — p. 51 ([scan leaf 624](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n624/mode/1up))

**Abe, Yoshinari** Prof. at Seoul Imp. Univ.; b. 1883 at Matsuyama; edu. at First High School and Tokyo Imp. Univ., in Occidental Philosophy, class '09. Works: Ancient and Medieval Philosophy in Europe, Modern European Philosophy, Kant's Ethical Philosophy, etc. Add. Seoul. — p. 51 ([scan leaf 624](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n624/mode/1up))

**Amano, Tameyuki** D.L., Economist, Dir. Waseda Business School; b. 1859 in Saga-ken; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., '82; M.P. at 1st and 2nd sessions; was on board of directors of Waseda Univ. till '17; once editor and proprietor of the “Oriental Economist,” a thrice-a-month journal. Wrote “Principles of Economics” and other books. Add. Kojimachi-ku Iida-machi, Tokyo. — p. 51 ([scan leaf 624](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n624/mode/1up))

**Anesaki, Masaharu** Litt. D., Prof. at Literature Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., Chief Librarian of the Tokyo Imperial Univ., Secretary of Imperial Academy, Mem. House of Peers (elected '26); b. 187[illegible] in Kyoto; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., in Philosophy, class '96; further studied at Kiel, British Museum and Benares, '00-03; Exchange Prof. at Harvard, '12-15. Add. Koishikawa Hakusan Gotenmachi, Tokyo. — p. 51 ([scan leaf 624](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n624/mode/1up))

**Asakawa, Kan-ichi** Prof. Yale University; b. about 1870; grad. from Waseda and early went to America for study; grad. from Yale and has since been lecturer and then Prof. of Japanese History at Yale. Add. Yale. — p. 51 ([scan leaf 624](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n624/mode/1up))

**Atobe, Sadajiro** D.C.L., Prof. at Law Coll., Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1872 in Fukui-ken; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '99; further studied in Germany; Assist.-Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ., '05; full Prof. in '06. Add. Shimokamo, Kamikyo-ku, Kyoto. — p. 51 ([scan leaf 624](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n624/mode/1up))

**Awazu, Seiryo** D.L., Pres. of Nippon Dosan & Fire Ins. Co., ex-Prof. Tokyo Imp. Univ., Waseda Univ., etc., insurance expert; b. 1871 in Kyoto; edu. in Law at Tokyo Imp. Univ., '94; has since been engaged in insurance business at the Dept. of Agr. & Com., as organizer of a number of life insurance cos., besides undertaking teaching; attended the World's Insurance Congress held at New York, '03. Add. Chofu-machi, near Tokyo. — p. 51 ([scan leaf 624](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n624/mode/1up))

**Daikuhara, Gintaro** D. Agr., Pres. of Kyushu Imp. Univ.; b. 186[illegible] in Nagano-ken; grad. from Agricultural Chemistry at Tokyo Imp. Univ., '94; served in the Dept. of Agr. & Com. as technical official either at head office or at the Nishigahara Experimental Institute; held similar post at the Korean Gov.-General till '25; Pres. of Kyushu Imp. Univ. since '26. Add. Fukuoka. — p. 52 ([scan leaf 625](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n625/mode/1up))

**Fujii, Kenjiro** Dr. Litt., Prof. in Ethics at Kyoto Imp. Univ. since '20; b. 1872 in Yamagata-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '98, and also Munchen and Berlin, '05-07; was for some years connected with Waseda Univ. Works: Principles of Subjective Ethics, National Ethics. Add. Kyoto Imp. Univ. — p. 52 ([scan leaf 625](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n625/mode/1up))

**Fujii, Oto-o** D. Litt., Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1867 in Hyogo-ken; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., '94; was professor at the govt. high schools, '98-11; present post, '11. Add. Kyoto. — p. 52 ([scan leaf 625](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n625/mode/1up))

**Fujioka, Katsuji** D. Litt., Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1872 in Tokyo; grad. from Litt. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '97; further studied in Germany, '01; after returning home was appointed Assist.-Prof. of his alma mater; full-Prof., '10. Add Hongo Nishikata-machi, Tokyo. — p. 52 ([scan leaf 625](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n625/mode/1up))

**Fukada, Kosan** D. Litt., Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ., b. 1878 at Kanazawa; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., in Philosophy, class '02, and further studied in England, Germany and France. Works: Translation of Kant's Critique, etc. Add. Kyoto Imp. Univ. — p. 52 ([scan leaf 625](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n625/mode/1up))

**Fukuda, Tokuzo** D.L., Prof. at Tokyo Univ. of Commerce, Mem. Imp. Academy; b. 1874 in Tokyo; grad. from the Tokyo Higher Commercial School, class '94; was dispatched by the Govt. to Germany, France, and Italy to make further study in economics; again visited Europe and America, '25-26. Works: several works on Economics. Add. Nakano-machi, Tokyo. — p. 52 ([scan leaf 625](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n625/mode/1up))

**Fukuoka, Hidei** 2nd Visc., L.L.B., M.L. (Yale), ex-Prof. at Tokyo School for Foreign Languages and Peers School, etc.; b. 1871 in Kochi-ken; went to America and studied at Washington, Lee, Cumberland and Yale Universities. Add. Koishikawa Kanatomi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 52 ([scan leaf 625](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n625/mode/1up))

**Gorai, Kinzo** (pen-name Sosen), Writer and Prof. at Waseda; b. 1875 in Ibaraki-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., in French Law, class '00, also in France and Germany, '04-09; Edit. of the Yomiuri, '14; Edit. of the monthly magazine Taikan (Marq. Okuma's organ at the time) in '18. Works: France and Frenchmen, Political Ethics, Future of Social Revolution, etc. Add. Waseda Univ. — p. 52 ([scan leaf 625](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n625/mode/1up))

**Harada, Tasuku** L.L.D. (Edinburgh), D.D. (Amherst), ex-Pres. of Doshisha, Kyoto, ('07-19); Professor at Hawaiian University (since '20); b. 1863 in Kumamoto; studied at Foreign Language School in Kumamoto, and later at the Doshisha; entered Yale and grad. from Divinity School, class '91; travelled through Europe in '92; pastor in Tokyo, Kyoto and Kobe; editor of Rikugo Zasshi and the “Christian World”; Pres. of the Christian Endeavor Union of Japan, '93-06; attended the world convention in London, '00 and similar religious meetings abroad; lectured at Harvard, Yale and other Universities in America, '09. Add. Honolulu. — p. 52 ([scan leaf 625](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n625/mode/1up))

**Haruki, Ichiro** D.L., Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., Mem. Imperial Academy; b. 1870 at Tokyo; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., '94; further studied Roman Law in Germany, '00-04; Prof. Kyoto Imp. Univ., '05; transferred to Tokyo later. Add. Hongo Nishikata-machi, Tokyo. — p. 52 ([scan leaf 625](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n625/mode/1up))

**Hatano, Seiichi** D. Litt., Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ. since '17; b. 1877 at Matsumoto; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., in Philosophy, class '99; studied in Germany, '04-06; Prof. at Waseda till '17 after his return home. Works: Origin of Christianity, History of Western Philosophy, Spinoza, etc. Add. Kyoto Imp. Univ. — p. 52 ([scan leaf 625](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n625/mode/1up))

**Hattori, Unokichi** D. Litt., Pres. Seoul Imp. Univ., Mem. Imp. Academy, & Emeritus Prof. Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1867 in Fukushima-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., Philosophy, '87-90; studied Chinese classics in China and method of teaching in Germany, '99-07; Prof. at alma mater on return; Prof. at Peking Univ., till '09; Prof. at Harvard, U.S.A., '15. Add. Totsuka-machi, Tokyo. — p. 53 ([scan leaf 626](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n626/mode/1up))

**Hayashi, Hirotaro** 2nd Count, Mem. House of Peers & Lecturer at the Lit. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., Pres. of the Imperial Education Ass'n.; b. 1868; grad. in Philosophy from Tokyo Imp. Univ.; Prof. at Tokyo Higher Commercial Sch. and Master of Ceremonies for a time; chairman of Educational Investigation Committee since '22. Add. Yoyohata Hatagaya, Tokyo. — p. 53 ([scan leaf 626](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n626/mode/1up))

**Hayashi, Kimio** Dr. Economics, Prof. at Waseda Univ.; b. 1883 in Okayama-ken; grad. at law, Waseda Univ., '05; Kuhara Co. for years, visited Europe. Works: Democratic Movement in Industry, History of International Labor Movement, Principles of Social Policy, etc. Add. Yoyohata-machi, near Tokyo. — p. 53 ([scan leaf 626](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n626/mode/1up))

**Hayashi, Kiroku** Pres. of Keio Univ.; b. 1872 at Karatsu; edu. at Keio in Literature and further studied Diplomacy at Paris, '01-04; M.P. in '20 and Senior Secretary of For. Office. Works: “Modern European Diplomacy,” etc. Add. Shibuya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 53 ([scan leaf 626](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n626/mode/1up))

**Hijikata, Yasushi** D.L., Crown Mem. House of Peers (nom. '22), Emeritus Prof. of Tokyo Imp. Univ., Mem. Imperial Academy, & Prof. at Chuo Univ.; b. 1859; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '82; studied in England, returning home '94; then Prof. at the Law College of his alma mater; later Dean of the law department retiring from the post in '22. Add. Kojimachi Ichibancho, Tokyo. — p. 53 ([scan leaf 626](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n626/mode/1up))

**Hiranuma, Yoshiro** D.L., Dean of Commercial Dept. Waseda Univ.; b. 1864 in Tsuyama; was in journalism for some years; educational work at Sendai High School, Osaka Com. School, etc.; Prof. at Waseda since '04. Add. Waseda Tsurumaki-cho, Tokyo. — p. 53 ([scan leaf 626](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n626/mode/1up))

**Honjo, Eijiro** Dr. Economics (Japan), Prof. & Dean of College of Economics, Kyoto Imp. Univ.; after graduating at Kyoto Imp. Univ. studied in Europe, '21-22. Works: History of Socialism in Japan, Researches on Economic History, etc. Add. Kyoto Imp. Univ. — p. 53 ([scan leaf 626](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n626/mode/1up))

**Horikiri, Zembei** M.P., Prof. at Keio; b. in Fukushima-ken; edu. at Keio, class '03; studied finance in Harvard, Cambridge and Berlin Univ.; on the editorial staff of the Jiji; Private Sec. to the Premier in the Takahashi Ministry; Parl. Counsellor of Dept. of Com. & Agr., '25. Add. Ichigaya Tamachi, Tokyo. — p. 53 ([scan leaf 626](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n626/mode/1up))

**Hotari, Ri-ichiro** Ph.D. (Chicago Univ.), Lecturer at Waseda Univ.; b. 1881 in Fukuoka-ken; edu. at Tokyo Hogakuin, South California Univ., and Chicago, devoting himself to the study of Divinity and Philosophy; Lecturer at Waseda after returning home; was sentenced to imprisonment for his socialistic article in a magazine but the execution was suspended. Works: Religion and Humanity, Humanitarian Poet Browning, Translation of Dewey's Works, etc. Add. Waseda Univ. — p. 53 ([scan leaf 626](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n626/mode/1up))

**Hozumi, Shigeto** Baron (suc. in '26), D.L., Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1883 in Tokyo, eldest son of the late Baron Nobushige Hozumi; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Law, class '08, and at Bonn, Paris, Cambridge, etc., '11-16 studying law of family, inheritance, etc. Works: Elements of Jurisprudence, Outline of Family Law, Russian Revolution and Marriage, On Divorce, etc. Add. Ushigome Haraikata-machi, Tokyo. — p. 53 ([scan leaf 626](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n626/mode/1up))

**Ichikawa, Sanki** D. Litt., Prof. of English and Philology, Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1886 in Tokyo; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., in Philology, class '09; further studied in England, '12-15; given chair of English and English Literature at the alma mater in Jan. '16; obtained the Doctorate, '23. Works: Studies in English Grammar, An English Pronouncing Dictionary for Japanese Students; Notes on Shakespeare's Othello, Hamlet, etc., Sheridan's The Rivals and The School for Scandal; Stevenson's Treasure Island. Add. Tokyo Imp. Univ. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 627](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n627/mode/1up))

**Idei, Moriyuki** Prof. at Waseda Univ.; b. 1892 in Tochigi-ken; edu. at Waseda and Chicago Univ. where he studied economics, '17-20; visited U.S.A. in '26. Add. Waseda Univ. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 627](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n627/mode/1up))

**Ichimura, Sanjiro** D. Litt., Emeritus Prof. Tokyo Imp. Univ. & Mem. Imperial Academy; b. 1864 in Ibaraki-ken; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., '89; Prof. at Peers' School and then of the Tokyo Imp. Univ. Add. Totsuka-machi, Tokyo. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 627](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n627/mode/1up))

**Igarashi, Tsutomu** D. Litt., Prof. at Waseda Univ., since '00; b. 1874 at Yonezawa; edu. at Waseda, in Literature, class '95; has ever since been connected with the alma mater. Works: New History of National Literature, New Treatise on Composition, etc. Add. Waseda University. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 627](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n627/mode/1up))

**Inouye, Hide** Mrs., Prof. of the Nippon Women's Univ.; b. 1877 in Hyogo-ken; grad. from Nippon Women's Univ. and went to U.S.A. for further study and grad. from Teachers' Coll., Columbia Univ. Add. Takata-machi, Tokyo. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 627](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n627/mode/1up))

**Inouye, Tetsujiro** D. Litt., Emeritus Prof. of Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1855 in Fukuoka-ken; pursued philosophical study in Germany and France, '84-90; Professor at College of Literature, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '90-23, of which he was a Dean; was Mem. of Imperial Academy till '26; elected Mem. House of Peers in '25, but resigned in '26. Works: “Life of Buddha,” the “Oriental Philosophy,” etc. Add. Koishikawa Omote-cho, Tokyo. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 627](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n627/mode/1up))

**Ishihara, Ken** D. Litt., Prof. at Tohoku Imp. Univ. (Sendai); b. 1882 in Tokyo; edu. at Waseda Univ. and Tokyo Imp. Univ.; further studied philosophy of religions in Europe. Add. Sendai. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 627](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n627/mode/1up))

**Ishikawa, Bungo** Prof. Tokyo Univ. of Commerce; b. 1877 in Tokyo; grad. from Tokyo Higher Commercial School, '97; further studied in Belgium where he grad. from Brussels College of Commerce; on returning home appointed on the teaching staff of his alma mater. Add. Koishikawa Yedogawa-cho, Tokyo. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 627](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n627/mode/1up))

**Ishikawa, Rinshiro** Prof. at Tokyo Higher Normal School; b. 1879 in Tochigi-ken; grad. from English Lit. of Tokyo Imp. Univ. in '03; took the post-graduate course. Add. Koishikawa Ohtsuka, Tokyo. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 627](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n627/mode/1up))

**Ishiwata, Bin-ichi** D.L., Mem. House of Peers; b. 1859 in Tokyo; studied law in Germany, '86-90; Judge of Tokyo Local Court; Counsellor of Dept. of Justice; Public Proc. of Tokyo Appeal Court; Lecturer of Law at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; Dir. of Civil and Criminal Bureau; Vice-Min. of Justice in '03; then Chief Sec. of Cabinet, '06-08. Add. Sugamo, Tokyo. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 627](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n627/mode/1up))

**Ito, Jujiro** Ph. D. (Pennsylvania), ex-Prof. at Waseda Univ.; b. 1878 in Hyogo-ken; grad. from Waseda, class '03; graduate student Univ. of Chicago, '05-06; same '06-08, (Harrison scholar, U.
