Penn., '06-07; Harrison, jur. fellow, same U., '07-08). Works: “Carriage by Sea,” and “Study of Commerce,” etc. Add. Zoshigaya, Tokyo. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 627](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n627/mode/1up))

**Izumi, Akira** D.C.L., Prof. at Meiji Univ.; b. 1873 in Hokkaido; grad. from the Sapporo Agr. College; further prosecuted his studies at the Lake Forest, Wisconsin, and Columbia, '07-12; was once Prof. at Tokyo Higher Technical School. Add. Shimohoya, Toyotama-gun, Tokyo. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 627](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n627/mode/1up))

**Jimbo, Kaku** English scholar and Prof. at Tokyo Higher Normal School; b. 1883 in Tokyo, being younger brother of late Dr. Kotora Jimbo, geologist (d. '24); grad. from Tokyo Higher Normal School, '05, and next Lit. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '08. Add. Tokyo Higher Normal School. — p. 54 ([scan leaf 627](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n627/mode/1up))

**Kada Tetsuji** Prof. at Keio Univ.; b. 1895 in Tokyo; grad. at Keio Univ., '19; studied sociology in Germany, '23-26. Works: Economic Value, William Morris, Modern History of Sociology, etc. Add. Keio Univ., Tokyo. — p. 55 ([scan leaf 628](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n628/mode/1up))

**Kakehi, Katsuhiko** D.L., Prof. at Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ. since '03; b. 1872 in Nagano-ken; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ.; further studied at Berlin Univ., Germany, '98-03; is a Shinto enthusiast. Add. Ushigome Kita-cho, Tokyo. — p. 55 ([scan leaf 628](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n628/mode/1up))

**Kambe, Masao** D.L., Prof. at Coll. of Economics, Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1877 in Aichi-ken; grad. from Politics, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '00; sent abroad for study twice in '04-08 and '10-15; Prof. at Waseda till '02; Assist.-Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ., '07; lectured at Kiel Univ., Germany. Works: Lectures on Political Economy, Redemption of National Debts, History of Socialism and Social Movement, etc. Add. Nishida-machi, Kyoto. — p. 55 ([scan leaf 628](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n628/mode/1up))

**Kanai, En** D.L. (Japan), Mem. Imp. Academy & Emer. Prof. Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1865 in Shizuoka-ken; grad. at Coll. of Literature (Economics), Tokyo Imp. Univ.; Professor at his alma mater, '90; dispatched to Europe, '11. Add. Totsuka-machi, Tokyo. — p. 55 ([scan leaf 628](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n628/mode/1up))

**Kaneko, Umaji** Litt. D., Prof. at Waseda Univ.; b. 1869 in Nagano-ken; grad. at Waseda Univ., '94; then studied Philosophy at Leipzig and Berlin, '01-03. Works: “Situation of Mental Current in Europe,” “Outline of Philosophy,” etc. Add. Shibuya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 55 ([scan leaf 628](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n628/mode/1up))

**Kato, Masaharu** D.L., Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1871 in Nagano, adopted by late Mr. Masayoshi Kato (Vice-Pres. N.Y.K.); grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '97; further studied Bankruptcy in Germany and France. Add. Kojimachi Motozono-cho, Tokyo. — p. 55 ([scan leaf 628](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n628/mode/1up))

**Kawakami, Hajime** D.L. (Japan), former Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ., Author; b. 1879 in Yamaguchi-ken; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '02 and further studied in England; Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ., '07-27. Wrote “Principles of Economics,” “Gold, Trust & Prices,” etc. Add. Tokyo. — p. 55 ([scan leaf 628](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n628/mode/1up))

**Kawata, Shiro** D.L., Pres. of Osaka Univ. of Commerce; grad. in Law, Kyoto Imp. Univ., class '07; connected with the Kokumin Shimbun for a while; then Assist.-Prof. at his alma mater and finally full Prof.; sent abroad for study to Germany, England, etc., '11-16, specializing in economics. Works: Development of Family System, Spirit of Capitalism, Women Problem, Brazil and Colonization, etc. Add. Osaka Univ. of Com., Osaka. — p. 55 ([scan leaf 628](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n628/mode/1up))

**Kawazu, Susumu** D.C.L., Dean of Coll. of Economics at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1875 in Tokyo, son of the late S. Kawazu (former Min. of Communications); edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., and further studied in Germany and England, '01-14. Works: “Principles of Political Economy,” etc. Add. Nakano, near Tokyo. — p. 55 ([scan leaf 628](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n628/mode/1up))

**Kiga, Kanju** D.L., Ph. D. (Leipzig), Dean of Economics Dept., Keio Univ.; b. 1873 in Shizuoka-ken; edu. at Keio Univ., '95; Prof. of Economics at his alma mater since '95; further studied in Germany, '98-20. Add. Shibuya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 55 ([scan leaf 628](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n628/mode/1up))

**Kimura, Shigeharu** M.A. (Harvard), Dir. of Nagasaki Higher Commercial School; b. 1874 in Osaka-fu; grad. from Harvard Univ., where he studied mediaeval history of Europe under Prof. Emerton; was for some time Prof. at St. Paul's Univ. (Rikkyo Daigaku); next Prof. at Yamaguchi Higher Commercial School, and then that of Tokyo Univ. of Commerce; present post in '23. Add. Nagasaki City. — p. 55 ([scan leaf 628](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n628/mode/1up))

**Kita, Reikichi** Prof. at Daito Bunka Institute & Lecturer at Meiji Univ.; b. 1885 in Niigata-ken; edu. at Waseda in Philosophy, class '08, and further studied in America and Germany for over 4 years, returning home in '22. Works: Digest of Bergson's Philosophy, From Philosophy to Politics, etc. Add. Iogi-machi, near Tokyo. — p. 56 ([scan leaf 629](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n629/mode/1up))

**Kitazawa, Shinjiro** Ph. D. (Johns Hopkins); b. 1887 in Tokyo; grad. from Waseda Univ. in Commerce, class '10; further studied at Johns Hopkins (U.S.A.) for four years besides three in South Univ., North Carolina, specializing in social problems and economy. Works: Latest Labor Movement, Problems for Laborers, etc. Add. Waseda Univ. — p. 56 ([scan leaf 629](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n629/mode/1up))

**Kobayashi, Ushisaburo** D.L., Economist and Financier, Dir. of the Japan Steel Foundry, etc.; b. 1865 in Gumma; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '94; Secretary at the Legislative Bureau, '96-07; Chief of Financial Bureau, Formosa, '07-10; M.P., '12-15; Financial Attache to Japanese Legation, Peking, '17. Wrote a number of works on finance and economy. Add. Hongo, Tokyo. — p. 56 ([scan leaf 629](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n629/mode/1up))

**Koizumi, Shinzo** Prof. at Keio Univ.; b. 1888 in Tokyo; grad. from Keio Univ., in Politics, '01; further studied in England and elsewhere, '12-16. Works: Studies on Social Problems. Economic Science and Social Ideas, Value and Socialism, etc. Add. Keio Univ. — p. 56 ([scan leaf 629](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n629/mode/1up))

**Konishi, Masutaro** Russian scholar; b. 1862 in Okayama-ken; studied Russian under Nicolai, a pioneer Russian missionary, and further studied philosophy at Kief and Moscow; once received religious instruction from Tolstoi; is now connected with Kyoto Imp. Univ. and Doshisha; has written “Russia” etc., besides translating into Russian several Chinese Classics. Add. Doshi-sha, Kyoto. — p. 56 ([scan leaf 629](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n629/mode/1up))

**Kozai, Yoshinao** Dr. Agr., Ph. D. (Leipzig), Emer. Prof. of Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1865 in Kyoto-fu; grad. from Agr. Coll. '85; Prof. at the alma mater, '89-95; studied at Leipzig, '95-99; Agriculturist to the Govt. Experimental Farm at Nishigahara (Tokyo), '03 and then its chief, '06; Dean of Coll. of Agriculture in '11-20; Pres. of Tokyo Imp. Univ., '20-29. Add. Tokyo Imp. Univ. — p. 56 ([scan leaf 629](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n629/mode/1up))

**Kumamoto, Kenjiro** English scholar, Prof. of English at Waseda Univ.; b. 1867 in Osaka; studied Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '87-89; English teacher at Himeji and next at Osaka Mid. Sch.; Prof. at 3rd High School, '95-98; at Tokyo Higher Normal, '94-98; Prof. at the Peers' School, '98-21; toured abroad in '19. Add. Zoshigaya, near Tokyo. — p. 56 ([scan leaf 629](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n629/mode/1up))

**Kume, Kunitake** Litt. D., Historian, ex-Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. & Waseda Univ.; b. 1844 in Saga; was on suite of the late Prince Iwakura's mission to America and Europe in '72; was a member of the Compilation Bureau of Cabinet, and afterward Prof. at 1st High School, but his article about the origin of the Japanese race obliged him to resign. Add. Meguro, Tokyo. — p. 56 ([scan leaf 629](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n629/mode/1up))

**Kuroita, Katsumi** D. Litt., Prof. at Litt. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1874 in Nagasaki-ken; grad. from the Lit. Coll. of Tokyo Imp. Univ., '96; further studied at the Univ. Hall; went to Europe for study. '08; after his return home appointed to present post. Add. Shibuya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 56 ([scan leaf 629](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n629/mode/1up))

**Kuwada, Kumazo** D.L., Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1868 in Tottori-ken; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '83; further studied in Europe, '96-98; Parl.-Secy. of Education, in '15; Mem. of House of Peers as highest-tax-payer for Tottori-ken, '01-17; represented Japan at Geneva Red Cross Conference, '21. Works: “Industrial Economy,” “The General Trend of Social Problems in Europe,” and “Factory Law and Labor Insurance,” etc. Add. Sendagi Hayashi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 56 ([scan leaf 629](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n629/mode/1up))

**Kuwaki, Gen-yoku** Litt. D., Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ. in '06, in psychology; further studied at the Univ. Hall and later in Europe and America. Works: Outline of Philosophy, Ten Lectures on Current Thoughts, Kant, Descartes, Five Great Philosophers, etc. Add. Ushigome Kita-cho, Tokyo. — p. 57 ([scan leaf 630](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n630/mode/1up))

**Kuwahara, Hitsuzo** D. Litt., Prof. at Litt. Coll. of Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1871 in Fukui-ken; grad. from Litt. Coll. of Tokyo Imp. Univ., '96; studied in the University Hall; appointed Prof. at 3rd High School; Prof. at the Tokyo Higher Normal School; then present post. Add. Kyoto Imp. Univ., Kyoto. — p. 57 ([scan leaf 630](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n630/mode/1up))

**Makino, Ei-ichi** D.L., Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1878 at Takayama; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '03; Lecturer at the alma mater, '03; Procurator at the Tokyo District Court, '05; reappointed at the alma mater, '07; studied in Germany, Italy, England, chiefly criminal law, '08-13. Works: Criminal Law of Japan, Criminal Procedure, etc. Add. Tokyo Imp. Univ. — p. 57 ([scan leaf 630](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n630/mode/1up))

**Makino, Kikunosuke** D.C.L., President of the Court of Cassation; b. 1866 in Tokyo; grad. from Law Coll. of Tokyo Imp. Univ., '91; served as Judge at Shizuoka and Yokohama District Courts; Judge at Court of Cassation in '19; then Sectional Chief; present post '28; attended International Conference on the Bills of Exchange Law at the Hague as the Japanese representative, '01. Add. Ushigome Kita-cho, Tokyo. — p. 57 ([scan leaf 630](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n630/mode/1up))

**Mano, Bunji** Emeritus Prof. of Tokyo and Kyushu Imp. Universities, Mem. Institution of Mechanical Engineers, London; b. 1861 in Shizuoka; grad. from Mech. Eng., former Eng. College, '81; further studied at Glasgow University; studied practical side of the science at Armstrong Works; returning home in '90, was appointed Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; sent to Paris on the occasion of World's Fair; Dir. of Technical Education Bureau, '01-13, while retaining the chair in the University; Pres. of Kyushu Imp. Univ., '13-26. Add. Yoyogi, Tokyo. — p. 57 ([scan leaf 630](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n630/mode/1up))

**Masuda, Tonosuke** Emeritus Prof. of Waseda Univ.; b. 1865 at Tsu; studied English at Tokyo English School, '84-86; taught English at Kokumin English School (Tokyo) for a few years; founded with W. Eastlake a private English School and taught there, '91-92; was editorial writer of Ise Shimbun when only 17; Prof. of English literature at Waseda Univ., '94-26. Works: A. New English-Japanese Dictionary; Lectures on English Rhetoric. Add. Hongo Maruyama Shimmachi, Tokyo. — p. 57 ([scan leaf 630](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n630/mode/1up))

**Matsudaira, Yasutaka** Marquis, head of the old Daimyo of Fukui; Pres. Japan Agr. Society; b. 1867; studied in Germany, '84; then at Cirencester Agr. Col., England; established a model exp. farm at his hereditary estate in Fukui. Add. Koi. Sekiguchi, Tokyo. — p. 57 ([scan leaf 630](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n630/mode/1up))

**Matsumoto, Bunzaburo** Litt. D., Emeritus Professor of Kyoto Imp. Univ., Mem. Imperial Academy; b. 1869 in Kanazawa; grad. from Litt. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '93 and further studied at Berlin in '99; Lecturer at Waseda, Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., then Prof. of Kyoto Univ., '06; Dean of Litt. College, '08; visited India, '18; retired in '29. Add. Kyoto Imp. Univ. — p. 57 ([scan leaf 630](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n630/mode/1up))

**Matsumoto, Joji** D.L., Mem. House of Peers & Mem. Imperial Academy; b. 1877 in Saitama-ken; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ. and passed Higher Civil Service Exam., '00; Counsellor of Dept. of Agr. & Com., '01 combining professorship at his alma mater; dispatched by the Govt. to England, Germany and France to study commercial law, '06; Dir. S.M.R. Co., '19-23; Dir. of Legislative Bureau, '24. Add. Chofu-machi, near Tokyo. — p. 57 ([scan leaf 630](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n630/mode/1up))

**Matsumoto, Matataro** D. Litt., Ph. D. (Yale Univ.), Mem. Imperial Academy; b. 1865 in Gumma-ken; grad. from Lit., Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '93; studied Experimental Psychology in Yale Univ., '96-98; then at Leipzig Univ., '98-00; Prof. at Tokyo Higher Normal School; Lecturer and afterward Professor at Literature College of Tokyo Imp. Univ. Add. Koishikawa Daimaichi, Tokyo. — p. 57 ([scan leaf 630](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n630/mode/1up))

**Matsunami, Nin-ichiro** D.L., ex-Prof. Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1857 in Osaka; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., '93; studied in Europe for years; Instructor at Naval Cadet Sch. and next at alma mater; attended International Conference regarding maritime law several times; was on the staff of the Peace Envoy, '19. Add. Ushigome Naka-machi, Tokyo. — p. 58 ([scan leaf 631](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n631/mode/1up))

**Mayeda, Keiun** D. Litt., Pres. of Ryukoku Univ. (Kyoto); b. 1857 in Miye-ken; studied Chinese classics and Buddhism; became Educational Superintendent to Lord Abbot of Hongwanji Temple, '91; Superintendent of Hongwanji Daigaku (Buddhist Univ.), '97; Lecturer at the Literature Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '00; Lecturer at Toyo Univ. and then the present post. Add. Kyoto. — p. 58 ([scan leaf 631](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n631/mode/1up))

**Mikami, Sanji** Historian, D. Litt., Chief of Compilation Board, Imp. Household Dept., Emeritus Prof. of Tokyo Imp. Univ., Mem. Imp. Academy; b. 1865 in Hyogo-ken; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., '89; further studied at the Post-Grad. Course; Lecturer at the same Coll., '91; Prof. at Girls' Higher Normal School, '92; Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. (Litt. Coll.), '92-25; sent to Germany to attend the Conference of the International Oriental Society at Hamburg, '02. Add. Komagome Hayashi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 58 ([scan leaf 631](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n631/mode/1up))

**Minobe, Tatsukichi** D.L., Mem. Imperial Academy, Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1873 in Hyogo-ken; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '97; studied in Germany, France and England, '99-01. Add. Koishikawa Takehaya-cho, Tokyo. — p. 58 ([scan leaf 631](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n631/mode/1up))

**Mitsuma, Shinzo** D.L., Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. and Tokyo Univ. of Commerce; b. 1879 in Yamagata-ken; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '05; stayed in Germany and Italy for study. Add. Azabu Naka-machi, Tokyo. — p. 58 ([scan leaf 631](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n631/mode/1up))

**Miura, Kaneyuki** D. Litt., Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1871 in Shimane-ken; grad. from Lit. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '93; appointed Compiler of Japanese history, '05; present post since '09. Add. Kyoto Imp. Univ. — p. 58 ([scan leaf 631](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n631/mode/1up))

**Miyake, Yonekichi** D. Litt., Mem. Imp. Academy, Dir. Tokyo Univ. of Lit. & Sci., Court Councillor; b. 1860 in Wakayama-ken; studied at Keio, '72-75; Assist.-Prof. at Higher Normal School, '81-86; made an inspection tour abroad, '86-88; is now Prof. & Dir. of Tokyo Higher Normal School; was once a Commissioner of the Imp. Household and Dir. of the Imp. Museum. Add. Koishikawa Haramachi, Tokyo. — p. 58 ([scan leaf 631](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n631/mode/1up))

**Miyake, Yujiro** D. Litt., Author & Journalist; b. 1860 at Kanazawa; grad. at Philosophy, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '81; visited the South Seas and in '03 made a trip round the world; is a conspicuous figure in the world of letters and journalism, and has written many works, chiefly philosophical and literary; was editor of the fortnightly magazine, 'Nihon oyobi Nihonjin', but left it several years ago and started his own organ 'Gakan.' Add. Yoyogi Hatsudai, Tokyo. — p. 58 ([scan leaf 631](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n631/mode/1up))

**Morimoto, Kokichi** D.L., Ph. D. (Johns Hopkins). Prof. at Hokkaido Imp. Univ.; b. 1877 at Maizuru; grad. at Hokkaido Imp. Univ., '01; further studied at Baltimore University specializing in Economy, Politics and History; engaged in university extension lectures at Chicago and Boston; is an authority on social problems and is manager of the Tokyo Cultural Life Society which has built the first apartment building in Tokyo. Works: Problems of Living, New Efficiency of Living, Investigation of Middle Class, Investigation of Standard of Living (in English), etc. Add. Sapporo. — p. 58 ([scan leaf 631](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n631/mode/1up))

**Murakami, Senjo** D. Litt., Emer. Prof. Tokyo Imp. Univ., Mem. Imp. Academy; Principal of Toyo Girls' High School; b. 1851 in Fukui-ken; is a well known scholar of Buddhism. Add. Koishikawa Hayashi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 59 ([scan leaf 632](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n632/mode/1up))

**Naito, Torajiro** D. Litt., Mem. Imp. Academy, Emer. Prof. Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. in Akita-ken; grad. from Lit. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ.; was on the staff of the Osaka Asahi Shimbun and its Peking correspondent for several years; is a well known authority on Chinese history. Add. Kamikyo-ku, Kyoto. — p. 59 ([scan leaf 632](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n632/mode/1up))

**Nakamura, Shingo** D.L. (Japan), Prof. at Tokyo Univ. of Commerce & Waseda Univ.; b. 1870 in Niigata Prefecture; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '90; further at the University Hall and then in England, Germany and France (International Law and History of Diplomacy). Add. Yoyogi Sanya, near Tokyo. — p. 59 ([scan leaf 632](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n632/mode/1up))

**Niida, Masutaro** D.C.L., ex-Dean of Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ.; lawyer; b. 1868 in Fukushima-ken; grad. at Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '93; sent to Germany for study, '97-00; Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ. and then of his alma mater in '08; opened law office, '21. Add. Koishikawa Kago-cho, Tokyo. — p. 59 ([scan leaf 632](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n632/mode/1up))

**Niho, Kamematsu** D.C.L., Emer. Prof. of Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1868 in Ise; grad. from Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '93; studied in Germany, '97-00. Add. Nishi-tsuge-mura, Miye-ken. — p. 59 ([scan leaf 632](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n632/mode/1up))

**Ninagawa, Arata** D.L., Mem. of Board of Governors of the League of Red Cross Societies, Adviser to Japan Red Cross Society, Dir. of the League of Nations Association of Japan; grad. from the Law College of Tokyo Imp. Univ.; was once Legal Adviser to the Korean Govt.; attached to the Japan Red Cross Society since '18. Add. Shibuya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 59 ([scan leaf 632](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n632/mode/1up))

**Nishida, Ikutaro** D. Litt., Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1870 in Ishikawa-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. (elective course), in Philosophy, class '94; taught at the Yamaguchi and the Fourth High School before his appointment to the present post. Works: What Is Righteousness?, Problems of Consciousness, Idealistic Philosophy of the Day, Art and Morals, etc. Add. Kyoto Imp. Univ., Kyoto. — p. 59 ([scan leaf 632](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n632/mode/1up))

**Noda, Yoshio** D. Litt., ex-Dir. of Osaka High Sch.; b. 1873 in Fukuoka-ken; grad. at Philosophy, Tokyo Imp. Univ.; studied in England, Germany and U.S.A.; Prof. at Hiroshima Higher Normal Sch.; Educational Inspector; Dir. of Nara Girls' Higher Normal Sch., Osaka High School, etc. Add. Osaka. — p. 59 ([scan leaf 632](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n632/mode/1up))

**Nogami, Toshio** Litt. D., Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1882 in Sado; edu. at First High School and Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Psychology, class '06, and further in Europe and at Leipzig under Wundt from '13 to '16 and was the last Japanese under him at that time; crossed to America where he attended Dr. Stanley Hall's class at Clark Univ. Works: Impression of Education in Europe and America, Lectures on Juvenile Psychology, etc. Add. Kyoto Imp. Univ., Kyoto. — p. 59 ([scan leaf 632](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n632/mode/1up))

**Ogawa, Masataka** Emer. Prof. Tohoku Imp. Univ.; b. 1865 in Ehime-ken; grad. from Science Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '89; Prof. of 1st High School and Tokyo Higher Normal School; Prof. at Tohoku Imp. Univ. Add. Sendai. — p. 59 ([scan leaf 632](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n632/mode/1up))

**Okakura, Yoshisaburo** ex-Prof. at Tokyo Higher Normal School, an English scholar; b. in 1865; grad. at Philology, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '88; was sent to Korea to investigate Korean language; next to England to study method of teaching English, '01-04; lecturer at the Lowell Institute, Boston, '09. Works: “The Spirit of Japan”, “The Life and Thought of Japan,” etc. Add. Naka-arai, Nerima, near Tokyo. — p. 59 ([scan leaf 632](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n632/mode/1up))

**Onozuka, Kiheiji** D.C.L., Pres. and Prof. of Tokyo Imp. Univ., Mem. House of Peers, Mem. Imp. Academy; b. 1871 in Niigata-ken; grad. from Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '95; further studied politics at the Universities of Heidelberg and Berlin and then at the Paris Political College, '97-01; elected Mem. House of Peers, '27; Pres. of Tokyo Imp. Univ., '2[illegible]. Add. Koishikawa Kobinatadaimachi, Tokyo. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 633](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n633/mode/1up))

**Oshima, Kintaro** D. Agr., ex-Prof. at Hokkaido Imp. Univ., Dir. of Formosan Central Research Institute; b. 1871 in Nagano-ken; grad. from Sapporo Agr. Coll., '93; appointed Assist.-Prof. of alma mater, '95; sent to Germany and America, in '98, studying agr. chemistry at Halle, Gottingen, Berlin, and Wesleyan Univ. (U.S.A.), '98-03. Add. Formosa. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 633](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n633/mode/1up))

**Otsuka, Yasuji** D. Litt., Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1868 in Gumma-ken; grad. from Lit. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ.; further studied in Germany, France and Italy, '96-00. Add. Ushigome Ichigaya Sadowara-cho, Tokyo. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 633](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n633/mode/1up))

**Sano, Zensaku** D.L., Dir. and Prof. of Tokyo Univ. of Commerce; b. 1873 in Tokyo; grad. from the above in '94; proceeded twice to Europe and America, and studied further in England, Belgium, Germany, etc.; represented Japan in the Pacific Conference held at Honolulu, '22. Add. Sendagaya, Tokyo. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 633](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n633/mode/1up))

**Sasaki, Soichi** D.L., Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. in Tottori in 1878; grad. from Kyoto Imp. Univ., in Law, class '03; studied in Europe, '09-12. Works: Administration, Constitutional and Unconstitutional, etc. Add. Kyoto Imp. Univ. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 633](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n633/mode/1up))

**Satake, Sango** D.L., Mem. House of Peers (nom. '27); b. 1880 at Ogaki; grad. from German Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '05; Counsellor of the Legislation Bureau, '06; official of the South Manchuria R'ly. Co., '80; Counsellor and later Bureau Director of the Railway Dept., '12-18; Mng. Dir. of a Private Rly. Co. in Osaka till '24; then Dir. of the Legislation Bureau; studied state railway problems mostly in Germany, '08-71. Add. Koishikawa Kago-machi, Tokyo. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 633](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n633/mode/1up))

**Sato, Ushijiro** D.C.L., Dean of Law & Lit. Coll., Tohoku Imp. Univ. since '21; b. 1877 in Yamagata-ken; studied politics at Kyoto Imp. Univ.; sent to Germany, France and England to further his studies, '98-02; Prof. in the present Univ. on returning home. Add. Sendai. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 633](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n633/mode/1up))

**Sejimo, Kiyomichi** D.C.L. (Columbia & Catholic Univ. 1904), barrister; b. 1865 in Sado; grad. from the now defunct English Law School, '88; passed the Barristers' examination, '88; became lawyer the next year. Add. Kanda, Imagawakoji, Tokyo. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 633](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n633/mode/1up))

**Shida, Kotaro** D.L., ex-Prof. at Tokyo University of Commerce; b. 1868 in Chiba Prefecture; grad. at Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '98; appointed Prof. at his alma mater, '06; engaged as Prof. and Adviser to Chinese Govt. to compile Commercial and Civil Law, '08-12; long connected with Kyosai Life Ins. Co. Add. Koishikawa Kobinatadai-machi, Tokyo. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 633](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n633/mode/1up))

**Shiozawa, Shotei** Ph. D. (Michigan), D.L. (Japan), Prof. at Waseda Univ.; b. 1874 at Mito; grad. from Waseda in '95 and from Wisconsin, '00; further studied at Berlin and Halle, '01-02, his speciality being political economy; Pres. of Waseda Univ., '21-23. Add. Waseda Univ. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 633](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n633/mode/1up))

**Shiratori, Kurakichi** Historian, D. Litt., Mem. Imperial Academy, Emeritus Prof. at Peers' School and Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1865 in Chiba; grad. in '99 from Lit. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ.; further studied in Germany and Hungary, '00-05; is an authority on Mongolian dynasties and allied subjects; was tutor to the Crown Prince (present Emperor). Add. Kami-Meguro, Tokyo. — p. 60 ([scan leaf 633](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n633/mode/1up))

**Suehiro, Iwataro** D.L., Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1888 in Yamaguchi-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. and also at Western Univ. for about three years; took active part in the relief work in connection with the earthquake disaster of 1923. Works: Law of Obligations, Law of Things, etc. Add. Koishikawa Takehaya-cho, Tokyo. — p. 61 ([scan leaf 634](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n634/mode/1up))

**Suehiro, Shigeo** D.L., Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1874 in Uwajima; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., in Law, class '99; studied diplomacy in Europe, '01-05; Lecturer at Kyoto Imp. Univ., '01; studied Political History in Germany, England, etc., '01-05; Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ., '06; visited U.S.A. and Mexico to investigate immigration conditions in '14; Dean of the Law Coll., '19-22. Works: Japanese in U.S.A., etc. Add. Kyoto Imp. University. — p. 61 ([scan leaf 634](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n634/mode/1up))

**Sugimori, Kojiro** Prof. at Waseda Univ.; b. 1881 in Shizuoka-ken; edu. at Waseda, in Literature, class '06; further studied philosophy as student sent by Government in England and Germany, '13-19. Works: The Principle of the Moral Empire (in English), Resurrection of Men, Foundation of New Society, etc. Add. Waseda Univ. — p. 61 ([scan leaf 634](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n634/mode/1up))

**Sugimoto, Etsuko** Mrs., Prof. of History of Japanese civilisation at Columbia Univ. (U.S.A.); grad. from Aoyama Jogakuin; went to Cincinnati (Ohio) in '97 with her husband, and studied by herself while dealing in grocery; widow in '10; became Prof. at Columbia Univ., '20. Add. Columbia Univ., New York. — p. 61 ([scan leaf 634](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n634/mode/1up))

**Takahashi, Kazutomo** Prof. at Keio Univ.; b. 1862 in Kawagoye; entered Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., but left in '86; went to U.S.A. and grad. from Michigan State Univ.; attended to business in the States and Canada before he returned home in '97 to join the Japan Times of which he was chief editor for many years; Secretary-Translator in the Foreign Office, '22-24. Add. Tsunohazu, Tokyo. — p. 61 ([scan leaf 634](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n634/mode/1up))

**Takahashi, Seigo** Ph. D. (Columbia), Prof. at Waseda Univ.; b. 1891 in Miyagi-ken; grad. from Waseda Univ., in Politics, class '11. Works: Political Idea in Europe, Development of Social Institutions in Europe, Construction of Cities, Scientific Study of Politics, Outline of Politics. Add. Waseda Univ. — p. 61 ([scan leaf 634](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n634/mode/1up))

**Takahashi, Sei-ichiro** Prof. at Keio Univ.; edu. at Keio Univ. and further studied in Europe specializing in History of Economics. Works: Studies in History of Economics, History of European Economics. Add. Keio Univ., Tokyo. — p. 61 ([scan leaf 634](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n634/mode/1up))

**Takakusu, Junjiro** M.A. (Oxf.), D. Litt., Emer. Prof. Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1865 in Bingo; edu. at Oxford, '88, and studied Sanskrit; further studied in Germany, returning home, '97; Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; Personal Secy. to the Minister of Communications and later Pres. of Foreign Language School, '00-08; stayed in London with Viscount Suyematsu during the Russo-Japanese War; sent abroad, '10-12; translated several Sanskrit and Pali works. Add. Koishikawa Sekiguchi Daimachi, Tokyo. — p. 61 ([scan leaf 634](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n634/mode/1up))

**Takano, Iwasaburo** D.C.L., Dir. of Ohara Social Research Institute, Statist and labor expert; b. 1871 in Tokyo; studied Law at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; Assist.-Prof. at the alma mater, '99; sent to France, Germany and U.S.A. for studying statistics; full Prof. and doctorate in '03; again toured abroad, '09; elected Labor Delegate to Washington in '18 but resigned; left Govt. service in '19. His wife is a German by birth. Add. Osaka. — p. 61 ([scan leaf 634](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n634/mode/1up))

**Takaoka, Kumao** D. L., D. Agr., Dean of Hokkaido Imp. Univ. (Sapporo); b. 1875 in Shimane-ken; grad. from Sapporo Agr. Coll., class '95; Assist.-Prof., '97; studied in Germany, '00-04, specializing in Agr. Economics. Works: Agriculture in Hokkaido, Agricultural Politics, Agricultural Education in the U.S.A., etc. Add. Sapporo. — p. 61 ([scan leaf 634](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n634/mode/1up))

**Takata, Yasuma** D.L., Prof. at Kyushu Imp. Univ. and Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1883 in Saga-ken; grad. at philosophy, Kyoto Imp. Univ., '10, stud. Sociology and Economics at post-graduate course of the same Univ.; Works; Principles of Sociology, Study of Modern Community, Society and State, Outline of Sociology, etc. Add. Kyushu Imp. Univ. — p. 62 ([scan leaf 635](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n635/mode/1up))

**Taki, Sei-ichi** D. Litt., Prof. of Oriental Art, at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1873, a son of Taki Katei, a master-painter in his day; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ. in '96, and further studied art criticism in England and France; has been a regular correspondent to the London Studio and edits an art journal Kokka. Add. Kami-Osaki, Tokyo. — p. 62 ([scan leaf 635](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n635/mode/1up))

**Tanabe, Chuyu** Prof. at Senshu and Rikkyo Univs.; b. 1891 in Nagasaki; edu. at Morning Star School (Tokyo), 1st High School and Tokyo Imp. Univ. (in Economy, class '16). Works: Translation of Cole's World of Labor; Fundamental Idea of Cole's Sociology, etc. Add. Rikkyo Univ., Tokyo. — p. 62 ([scan leaf 635](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n635/mode/1up))

**Tanabe, Gen** D. Litt., Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1875 in Tokyo; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., in Philosophy, class '98; Assist.-Prof. at Tohoku (Sendai) Univ. till '19; sent abroad for further study, '22-24. Works: Latest Natural Science, Studies on Science, etc. Add. Kyoto Imp. Univ. — p. 62 ([scan leaf 635](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n635/mode/1up))

**Tomonaga, Sanjuro** D. Litt., Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1871 in Nagasaki-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., in Philosophy, class '98, and further studied at Heidelberg, Leipzig, and other places. Works: Kant's Pacifism, etc. Add. Kyoto Imp. Univ. — p. 62 ([scan leaf 635](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n635/mode/1up))

**Toyoshima, Naomichi** D.C.L.; b. 1871 in Tokyo; grad. from Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '95; Pub. Proc., '97; attached to the Supreme Court, '07; Counsellor of Justice Dept., '11; has also been Lecturer at Waseda Univ. Add. Hongo Akebono-cho, Tokyo. — p. 62 ([scan leaf 635](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n635/mode/1up))

**Tsuboi, Kumazo** D. Litt., Emeritus Professor of Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1858 at Osaka; grad. in Litt. of Tokyo Imp. Univ.; sent by Govt. to Europe for study; Prof. at his alma mater, '91; Mem. Imp. Acad. Add. Hongo Yayoi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 62 ([scan leaf 635](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n635/mode/1up))

**Tsuchita, Tsutomu** (nom de plume Kyoson) philosophical writer; b. 1891 in Niigata-ken; grad. at Tokyo Higher Norm. Sch. and at Lit. Kyoto Imp. Univ., stud. specially philosophy and biology at the post-graduate course of above Univ. Works: Biological philosophy, Contemporary Thoughts of Japan and China, On Literature, Problems of Love, Outline of Modern Philosophy, etc. Add. Shinmachi-kashira, Kyoto. — p. 62 ([scan leaf 635](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n635/mode/1up))

**Tsumura, Hidematsu** D.L., Economist, Dir. of the Kuhara Firm, Ltd.; b. 1876 in Wakayama-ken; grad. from the Tokyo Higher Com. School and post-graduate course, class '99; further studied in Germany and America, '99-03; Prof. at the Kobe Higher Com. School on returning home; entered the Kuhara Firm in '20. Works: Principles of National Economics, New Principle of Political Economy, Commercial Policy, etc. Add. Uchisaiwai-cho, Tokyo. — p. 62 ([scan leaf 635](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n635/mode/1up))

**Ukita, Kazutami** D.L., Historian and Educationist, Prof. at Waseda Univ.; b. 1860 in Kumamoto; studied English under Capt. Janes of U.S.A.; grad. at Doshisha, '79; engaged for some while in religious work and journalism; Prof. at his alma mater; entered Yale '92 and studied philosophy and politics; again Prof. at the Doshisha, '97. Add. Takata, Tokyo. — p. 62 ([scan leaf 635](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n635/mode/1up))

**Urabe, Hyakutaro** D.L. (Keio), Prof. at Keio Univ.; b. 1869 in Fukuoka-ken; grad. from Keio, '95; was on the editorial staff of the Jiji, '95; later Prof. at his alma mater and Chief Editor of the Nippon. Add. Keio Univ., Tokyo. — p. 62 ([scan leaf 635](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n635/mode/1up))

**Uyeda, Kazutoshi** D. Litt., Philologist, Emeritus Prof. of Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1867 in Aichi-ken; grad. at above Coll. in '88; sent to France and Germany to prosecute further study; once acting Dir. of Tokyo Foreign Language Sch.; Counsellor and Bureau Chief of the Edu. Dept.; Dean of the Lit. Coll. till '20, represented Japan in the meeting of the International Univ. Club held in Brussels in '23; was at a time Dir. of Jingu Shinto Institute at Ise. Add. Shitaya Yanaka Shimizu-cho, Tokyo. — p. 63 ([scan leaf 636](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n636/mode/1up))

**Uyeda, Teijiro** D.L., Prof. at Tokyo Univ. of Commerce; b. 1879 in Tokyo; grad. from Tokyo Higher Commercial School, '02; studied in Germany and England, '05-09, as student sent by Government; Govt. Delegate to the 1st Inter. Econ. Conf. held at Washington, and to the 2nd held at Geneva, '27. Add. Tokyo Univ. of Commerce. — p. 63 ([scan leaf 636](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n636/mode/1up))

**Wakamiya, Unosuke** Sociologist; b. 1872 in Toyama-ken; studied in American Catholic Univ., '00-04, and London School of Economics and Political Science, '04-05, studying as self-supporting student. Add. Shimo-meguro, Tokyo. — p. 63 ([scan leaf 636](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n636/mode/1up))

**Washio, Shogoro** Ph. D. (Harvard); b. 1883 in Awaji; travelled in Germany and France, '11-12, as scholarship student of Harvard; again travelled abroad with Visc. Goto in '19 when he studied social and labor conditions in England; now staff writer of the Japan Advertiser. Add. Takaido-machi, near Tokyo. — p. 63 ([scan leaf 636](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n636/mode/1up))

**Yabuki, Keiki** D. Litt., ex-Dir. of Social Affairs Bureau, Tokyo City; b. in Tokyo; grad. from Litt. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '09; further studied in England; then Assist.-Prof. of Tokyo Imp. Univ. and also Prof. of Shukyo Univ. Add. Nishi Sugamo, Tokyo. — p. 63 ([scan leaf 636](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n636/mode/1up))

**Yahagi, Eizo** D. L., Prof. at the Coll. of Economics, Tokyo Imp. Univ., Pres. of Teikoku Agricultural Ass'n.; b. 1869 in Saitama-ken; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., '95; further studied at the Univ. Hall specializing in Agr. Economy; Lecturer at Tokyo Higher Com. School, '96; Auditor of Industrial Bank, '97-98; Lecturer and then Assist.-Prof. at Agr. Coll., Imp. Univ., till '00; sent to Europe for study, '00-08; represented Japan at the International Agr. Conference at Rome, '06; on returning home was given chair of Agr. Policy at the Agr. Coll. and of Economics at the Law Coll. which dual position he still occupies; sent on an inspection tour to Europe and America, '11-13. Works: Real Estate Banks, Collected Essays on Agr. Economics. Add. Sendagaya, Tokyo. — p. 63 ([scan leaf 636](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n636/mode/1up))

**Yamada, Saburo** D.L., Prof. at Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1869 in Nara-ken; edu. at Tokyo Semmon Gakko (now Waseda Univ.) and then Law Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '96; further studied at the Univ. Hall and in Germany, England, etc., '97-01; Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. on his return. Add. Ushigome Benten-cho, Tokyo. — p. 63 ([scan leaf 636](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n636/mode/1up))

**Yamakawa, Hashio** D.C.L., ex-Dir. of Legislation Board; b. 1873 in Nagasaki-ken; grad. from Politics, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '98; studied International Law in the Univ. Hall; passed Higher Civil Service Exam.; was Councillor of the Navy Dept. and also of the Foreign Office; as Naval Interpreter attended 2nd International Peace Conference at Berne; Dir. of Treaty Bureau, '23-25; Dir. of Legislation Bureau, '25-27. Add. Aoyama Minami-cho, Tokyo. — p. 63 ([scan leaf 636](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n636/mode/1up))

**Yamasaki, Kakujiro** D.L., Prof. at Coll. Economics, Tokyo Imp. Univ., Mem. Imp. Academy; b. 1868 in Shizuoka-ken; edu. at Economics Course, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '89, and further at the post-graduate course, and in Germany till '95; Prof. at Tokyo Higher Com. School, '97; Assist.-Prof. at his alma mater, '02; full Prof., '06; Dean of the Economics Coll., '20-23. Works: An Economic Study of the English Cotton Industry, Principles of Economics, Principles of Banking, etc. Add. Koishikawa Hara-machi, Tokyo. — p. 63 ([scan leaf 636](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n636/mode/1up))

**Yanagi, So-etsu** Prof. at Doshisha, an authority on philosophy of religions and Oriental art; b. 1889 in Tokyo; edu. at Peers' School and Tokyo Imp. Univ., Psychology, class '13. Works: Science and Life, William Blake, Truth about Religion, Miracles in Religion, etc. Lectured at Harvard on Oriental art in '29. Add. Yoshida, Kyoto. — p. 64 ([scan leaf 637](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n637/mode/1up))

**Yasugi, Sadatoshi** Prof. at Tokyo School of Foreign Languages; edu. in Philology, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '01; then stayed in Russia to study the Russian language. Add. Nagasaki-machi, near Tokyo. — p. 64 ([scan leaf 637](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n637/mode/1up))

**Yokota, Hideo** D.L. (Japan), Pres. of Meiji Univ.; b. 1862 in Nagano-ken; grad. from Law, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '88; sat at Tokyo, Chiba and Hakodate District Courts, and Tokyo and Hakodate Appeal Courts; Judge of the Supreme Court, '01-24; was sent abroad on official mission, '09; Pres. of the Supreme Court, '24-27. Add. Nakano, near Tokyo. — p. 64 ([scan leaf 637](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n637/mode/1up))

**Yoneda, Shotaro** D. Litt., Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1873 in Nara-ken; edu. at General Theological Seminary (New York), Columbia and at some European Univs. specializing in sociology. Works: Investigation of Modern Social Thoughts, Contemporary Thoughts and Civilization, Philosophy of History by Rickert, etc. Add. Kyoto Univ. — p. 64 ([scan leaf 637](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n637/mode/1up))

**Yoshida, Kumaji** D. Litt., Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1874 in Yamagata-ken; grad. from Lit., Tokyo Imp. Univ.; further studied in Europe and America, '04-07; Prof. at Tokyo Female Higher Normal School, '04; present post since '16. Add. Koishikawa Hakusangoten-machi, Tokyo. — p. 64 ([scan leaf 637](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n637/mode/1up))

**Yoshino, Sakuzo** D.L., Political writer; b. 1878 in Miyagi-ken; grad. from Politics at Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '04; further studied in Europe; was for several years in the service of the Chinese Govt.; Prof. of Politics at Tokyo Imp. Univ. for several years; was for some time contributor to the Tokyo Asahi; is a writer of socialistic leaning. Add. Komagome Shimmei-cho, Tokyo. — p. 64 ([scan leaf 637](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n637/mode/1up))

### Literature

**Akita, Ujaku** Dramatist, mem. of Japan Social Federation; b. 1883 at Aomori-ken; edu; at Aomori 1st Middle Sch. and Waseda Univ. in English Literature, class '07. Add. Zoshigaya, near Tokyo. — p. 64 ([scan leaf 637](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n637/mode/1up))

**Arishima, Ikuma** foreign style painter and novelist, bro. of Takeo Arishima whose suicide created a sensation; b. 1882 at Yokohama; edu. at Tokyo Foreign Language School where he studied Italian and next learned oil painting for several years in Paris and Rome. Works: a number of novels, Reminiscence of Cezanne, etc. Add. Kojimachi Shimo-niban-cho, Tokyo. — p. 64 ([scan leaf 637](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n637/mode/1up))

**Fujimori, Seikichi** Novelist; b. 1892 at Suwa; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in German Literature; has written a number of stories. Add. Zoshigaya, near Tokyo. — p. 64 ([scan leaf 637](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n637/mode/1up))

**Homma, Hisao** Writer and Prof. at Waseda Univ.; b. 1886 at Yonezawa; edu. at Waseda in English Literature, class '09. Works: Current Thoughts and Literature, Life and Art, Essentials of Ellen Kay, etc. Add. Waseda Univ. — p. 64 ([scan leaf 637](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n637/mode/1up))

**Ihara, Toshiro** (nom de plume Seiseien), Dramatic critic; a writer on the staff of Miyako Shimbun; b. 1870 in Matsue city; stud. at First High Sch.; connected with the Niroku, Waseda Bungaku and then Miyako. Works: History of Japanese Drama, History of Modern Drama, etc. Add. Aoyama Minami-cho, Tokyo. — p. 64 ([scan leaf 637](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n637/mode/1up))

**Ikuta, Koji** (pen-name Choko), Novelist and critic; b. 1882 in Tottori-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., in Lit., class '06. Works: Enko and After, Environment, Tendency of Modern Literature and Thoughts, etc. Add. Kamakura. — p. 65 ([scan leaf 638](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n638/mode/1up))

**Ikuta, Seihei** (nom de plume Shungetsu), Poet & novelist; b. 1892 in Tottori-ken; once studied at Waseda Univ.; Works: Autumn of Soul, Sentimental Spring, Collection of short poems, Kingdom of consolation, Clear Sky, Ecstasy, Attracting Souls, and many other essays and translations. Add. Ushigome Benten-cho, Tokyo. — p. 65 ([scan leaf 638](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n638/mode/1up))

**Ishimaru, Gohei** Novelist and religious writer; b. 1886 in Osaka; grad. at History, Waseda Univ., '08; teacher of middle and girls' schools for years, and then entered literary circles. Works: Priest Shinran the Man, Suffering Shinran, Arts and creation of life, System of creative thought of human life, etc. Add. Samukawa, Chiba City. — p. 65 ([scan leaf 638](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n638/mode/1up))

**Iwaya, Suyewo** (pen-name Sazanami), Author, novelist, the Hans Andersen of Japan; b. 1870 in Tokyo; edu. at German Association School and Lit. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ.; further studied in Germany, '98-03, filling at the same time chair of Japanese in the Oriental Language School, Berlin; has written and translated quite a large number of juvenile stories. Add. Takanawa, Tokyo. — p. 65 ([scan leaf 638](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n638/mode/1up))

**Kato, Nobuharu** (nom de plume Chocho); b. 1886 in Tottori-ken; grad. at English Lit., Waseda Univ., '09; visited Hawaii in '19; Works: Modern Literary Thoughts, Travel in Hawaii, Crusade, Translation of B. Shaw's Woman's Guide of Socialism and others. Add. Tamagawa, near Tokyo. — p. 65 ([scan leaf 638](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n638/mode/1up))

**Kato, Kazuo** magazine writer and novelist; b. 1887 in Wakayama-ken; edu. at Meiji Gakuin where he finished Divinity, class '10; was next connected with the Unitarian work but has lately adopted literary career. Add. Shinji-mura, Kanagawa-ken. — p. 65 ([scan leaf 638](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n638/mode/1up))

**Kawaji, Makoto** (nom de plume Ryuko) Poet; b. 1888 in Tokyo; grad. at Tokyo Fine Arts Academy; edited “New Home” at Genbunsha, and “Taiyo” and “Photograph Magazine” at Hakubunkan for years. Works: Poem, Roadside Flowers, Walking man, First Love, Voice of Dawn, etc. and Appreciation of modern arts, Study of poem writing, etc. Add. Kamakura. — p. 65 ([scan leaf 638](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n638/mode/1up))

**Kikuchi, Kan** Novelist; b. 1889 in Kagawa-ken; grad. at Lit., Kyoto Imp. Univ. in '16; writer for Jiji and Osaka Mainichi for years, leads Bungei Shunju, literary magazine since '23. Works: Kingdom of mind, Defamer, Sparks, New jewels, Beyond revenge, Time and love, Relatives, etc. Add. Kojimachi Uchisaiwai-cho, Tokyo. — p. 65 ([scan leaf 638](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n638/mode/1up))

**Kimura, Ki** critics; b. 1895 in Okayama-ken; grad. at Waseda Univ. in '17; editorial work at Ryubunkan and Shunjusha for years, leads “Echo” literary magazine in '26, visited England in '28. Works: Outline of world literature, translation of “Quo Vadis,” etc. Add. Nishiokubo, near Tokyo. — p. 65 ([scan leaf 638](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n638/mode/1up))

**Kitahara, Ryukichi** (nom de plume Hakushu), Poet; b. 1885 in Fukuoka-ken; stud. at Waseda Univ. Works: Collection of poems, Japanese flute, Reed leaves, Collection of short poems, Memories, etc. Add. Setagaya, near Tokyo. — p. 65 ([scan leaf 638](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n638/mode/1up))

**Koda, Nariyuki** D. Litt., a master novelist (nom de plume “Rohan”): b. 1867, a son of an artist family. His younger brother, Naritomo, is a grad. of the Litt. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ.; two sisters Miss Nobu, and Mrs. Ando (her younger sister) were b. at Tokyo, and both showed a great talent for music from childhood; sent abroad by the Gov. to study music, '87-95; then both were app. Prof. of the Tokyo Music Academy, but have resigned since. Works: “Gojunoto,” “Futsuka Monogatari,” etc. besides some historical works. Add. Koishikawa Omote-machi, Tokyo. — p. 65 ([scan leaf 638](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n638/mode/1up))

**Koizumi, Tetsu** writer on art; b. 1886 in Tokushima; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. which he left unfinished; once edited a literary magazine Shirakaba and was also on the teaching staff of Keio Univ. Add. Shibuya-machi, Tokyo. — p. 66 ([scan leaf 639](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n639/mode/1up))

**Komaki, Omi** (real name Omi Tanigoma), literary writer; b. 1894 at Tsuchizaki; edu. at the Morning Star School (Tokyo) and at Paris Univ.; attended conference at Geneva in '23 as adviser to the Government delegates; published a literary magazine while he was connected with the Information Bureau (Foreign Office). Add. Akasaka Hinoki-cho, Tokyo. — p. 66 ([scan leaf 639](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n639/mode/1up))

**Masamune, Tadao** (nom de plume Hakucho), Novelist; b. 1879 in Okayama-ken; grad. at Lit., Waseda Univ. in '02; writer for Yomiuri for years; visited America in '28. Works: Dusty Wind, To Where, Clay Doll, Two Families, Sunset, The Setting Sun, etc. Add. Oiso-machi, Kanagawa. — p. 66 ([scan leaf 639](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n639/mode/1up))

**Mikami, Otokichi** Novelist; b. 1891 in Saitama-ken; stud. at Waseda Univ. Works: Mist of love sentiment, Empty youth, Gloomy passion, Sweet ages, Vice of youth, Torrent Stream, The Sun, Flamy Sky, and other translations. Add. Ichigaya Sanai-cho, Tokyo. — p. 66 ([scan leaf 639](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n639/mode/1up))

**Mishima, Shodo** Visc., 3rd son of the late Yataro Mishima (Gov. of the Bank of Japan); b. 1897 in Tokyo; edu. at the Peers School; has since devoted himself to literary work and also to questions of social service; is an active manager of the Boy Scout Movement in Japan and attended the World Conference at Copenhagen in '24. Works: “Tragedy at Teradaya,” “Miss and I,” etc. Add. Sendagaya, Tokyo. — p. 66 ([scan leaf 639](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n639/mode/1up))

**Miyajima, Shinzaburo** lecturer at Waseda Univ.; b. 1892 in Tokyo; grad. at English Lit., Waseda Univ. in '15; visited England. Works: Pioneer of modern civilization, Study of reconstructive thoughts, Translation of Sixteen Writers of England and America, Sacrifice, etc. Add. Nishiokubo, near Tokyo. — p. 66 ([scan leaf 639](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n639/mode/1up))

**Mushakoji, Sane-atsu** Novelist and founder of a “New Community” in the far-off Hyuga; b. 1885; edu. at Peers' School and Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Literature, but left unfinished. Works: Life in the New Village; His Sister, etc. Add. Tokyo. — p. 66 ([scan leaf 639](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n639/mode/1up))

**Nakamura, Kichizo** Novelist and dramatist; b. 1877 in Shiga-ken; grad. from Lit., Waseda Univ., '03; further studied in Princeton and Columbia Univs., '06-09. Add. Sugamo Miyanaka, Tokyo. — p. 66 ([scan leaf 639](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n639/mode/1up))

**Noboru, Naotaka** (Pen-name Shomu), Russian scholar and writer; b. 1888 in O-shima, Kagoshima; edu. at the Orthodox Church Divinity School, class '03; connected with the Osaka Asahi, '05; Instructor of Russian at Waseda and military schools, '12-16; in the service of the Home Office, '19; visited Europe, '13. Works: Russian Thoughts and Literature of Today, Russia and Russians, Russian Revolution and Socialist Movement, Russian Proletariat Literature, Master Writers of Russia, etc. Add. Kamakura. — p. 66 ([scan leaf 639](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n639/mode/1up))

**Noguchi, Yonejiro** known as Yone Noguchi in literary circles in both America and England, Lecturer at Keio Univ.; b. 1875 at Aichi-ken; studied at Keio Univ., and in U.S.A., where he was patronized by Joaquin Miller but mostly self-taught as far as his literary ability is concerned; delivered lectures in England in '14. Publications: “From the Eastern Sea,” “Lafcadio Hearn in Japan,” “Hiroshige,” “Korin,” etc. Add. Nakano, near Tokyo. — p. 66 ([scan leaf 639](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n639/mode/1up))

**Onoye, Hachiro** D. Litt., master writer of the Mannyo style of script. Prof. at Tokyo Women's Higher Normal School and other institutions; b. 1876 in Tsuyama; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in Literature, class '00. Works: Poetical Works, History of Japanese Literature. Add. Koishikawa Haramachi, Tokyo. — p. 66 ([scan leaf 639](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n639/mode/1up))

**Saijo, Yaso** Poet, Lecturer at Waseda Univ.; b. 1892 in Tokyo; grad. at English Lit., Waseda Univ. in '15, and stud. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; studied in France, '24-27. Works: Alluvial Gold, Still Morning, White Peacock, Study of Modern English Poetry, etc. Add. Yodobashi, near Tokyo. — p. 67 ([scan leaf 640](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n640/mode/1up))

**Sasagawa, Taneo** (nom de plume Rimpu), Commissioner of the Home Office, Dir. of the Narita Mid. and Girls Schools, historian and essayist; b. 1870 in Tokyo; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. in History, class '95; was connected with Prince Mori's Historical Compilation Office, Sansho-do's Encyclopaedia Japonica, Meiji, Senshu and other private universities; is also known as art critic. Add. Hongo Nishi Katamachi, Tokyo. — p. 67 ([scan leaf 640](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n640/mode/1up))

**Sasaki, Nobutsuna** D. Litt., Poet, Lecturer at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1872 in Miye-ken; grad. from Lit. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '88; has written a Japanese anthology of poems; was awarded Imp. Academy prize in 1917. Add. Komagome Nishikatamachi, Tokyo. — p. 67 ([scan leaf 640](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n640/mode/1up))

**Shimamura, Tamizo** ex-Lecturer at Waseda Univ.; b. 1888 in Tokyo; grad. at English Lit., Waseda Univ., '09; lecturer at the alma mater, '16-25. Works: Drama, Yashamaru, Seijuro, and Principles and Practice of Modern Drama. Add. Ushigome Waseda-machi, Tokyo. — p. 67 ([scan leaf 640](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n640/mode/1up))

**Shimazaki, Haruki** (nom de plume Toson), a novelist and man of letters; b. 1872 in Nagano-ken; edu. at Meiji Gakuin (Methodist school). Works: Toson's Collected Poems, “Hakai”, “Iye” and other novels; Complete works issued in '22. Add. Azabu Iigura, Tokyo. — p. 67 ([scan leaf 640](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n640/mode/1up))

**Takahama, Kiyoshi** (pen-name Kyoshi), Haiku poet; b. 1874 in Iyo; left the Third High School unfinished; has since been taking prominent part in the haiku poetry and as a man of letters. Add. Kamakura Haranodai. — p. 67 ([scan leaf 640](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n640/mode/1up))

**Takebayashi, Sei-ichi** (nom de plume Musoan), Novelist; b. 1880 in Sapporo; grad. at Lit. Tokyo Imp. Univ.; visited France twice and stayed there. Works: For Marriage, and translation of Sappho, Sarnin, and others. Add. Paris, France. — p. 67 ([scan leaf 640](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n640/mode/1up))

**Tanizaki, Junichiro** Novelist; b. 1886 in Tokyo; grad. at 1st High Sch. and stu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ. one year; visited China in '18 and '26. Works: Tatoo, Devils, Between god and man, Love of simpleton, Sorrow of mermaid, Because I love her, etc. Add. Motoyama-mura, Hyogo-ken — p. 67 ([scan leaf 640](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n640/mode/1up))

**Tanizaki, Seiji** Novelist & Prof. at Waseda Univ.; b. 1890 in Tokyo, brother of above; grad. at English Lit., Waseda Univ. in '13. Works: Meeting and parting, Age for marriage, Love seekers, Love of life and death, Cheak upon the ground, etc. Add. Ushigome Kikui-cho, Tokyo. — p. 67 ([scan leaf 640](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n640/mode/1up))

**Toshima, Yoshio** Lecturer at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1890 in Fukuoka-ken; grad. at French Lit., Tokyo Imp. Univ. Works: Challenge, Prosperity of Mankind, translation of Hugo's Les Miserables and Joan Christoph of Roman Roland, and others. Add. Hongo Sendagi-cho, Tokyo. — p. 67 ([scan leaf 640](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n640/mode/1up))

**Tsubouchi, Shiko** actor, dramatist, and literary writer, nephew of Dr. Tsubouchi; b. 1887 in Nagoya; edu. at Waseda Univ. in English Literature, class '09; stayed in England for six or seven years; after returning home he was for a while an actor of a new school; then an instructor at the alma mater; now adviser of the Takarazuka Opera School near Osaka; is famous as a dancing master. Works: Translation of Moliere's complete works, Masterpieces of Ibsen, and some original works. Add. Takarazuka Kageki School. — p. 67 ([scan leaf 640](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n640/mode/1up))

**Tsubouchi, Yuzo** Litt. D., Author and a man of letters; b. 1859 in Aichi-ken; has been connected with Waseda Univ., from its inception in '82, being now its Emeritus Prof.; grad. Litt. Coll. of the Tokyo Imp. Univ., '82; is more widely known in public as pioneer novelist and dramatist and a Shakespearian scholar of Japan, having completed translation of his plays; has also written several works on ethics; organized in '11 the Bungei-kyokai but dissolved it in '13. Add. Ushigome-Yocho-machi, Tokyo. — p. 67 ([scan leaf 640](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n640/mode/1up))

**Tsuchii, Rinkichi** (nom de plume Bansui), Prof. at 2nd (Sendai) High School; b. 1871 in Sendai; grad. Litt., Tokyo Imp. Univ.; further studied literature in Europe, '01-04; has written a number of poetical works; translated Sartor Resartus. Add. Sendai. — p. 68 ([scan leaf 641](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n641/mode/1up))

**Yamamoto, Yuzo** ex-Lecturer Waseda Univ., dramatist; b. 1887 in Tochigi-ken; grad. at German Lit., Tokyo Imp. Univ. Works: Collection of Dramas, Colleagues, Waves, etc. Add. Kichijoji, near Tokyo. — p. 68 ([scan leaf 641](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n641/mode/1up))

**Yosano, Akiko** Mrs., a poetess; b. 1878 in Sakae where she finished a girls school; has been with her husband, Kan Yosano, a leader of new poetry movement and has a large number of poetical works to her credit; travelled abroad in '14. Add. Koj. Fujimicho, Tokyo. — p. 68 ([scan leaf 641](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n641/mode/1up))

**Yosano, Kan** Poet, Prof. at Keio Univ.; b. 1873 in Kyoto; edu., common school, and studied poetry under the late Naobumi Ochiai and Surgeon-Gen. Mori; travelled in Europe '11-14. Works: collected poems. Add. Koj. Fujimicho, Tokyo. — p. 68 ([scan leaf 641](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n641/mode/1up))

**Yoshie, Takamatsu** (pen-name Kogan), Prof. at Waseda Univ. from which he grad. in Literature, class '05; further studied in France, '16-20. Works: Modern Civilization and Literature, Love and Art, Translation of Maupassant, etc. Add. Waseda Univ. — p. 68 ([scan leaf 641](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n641/mode/1up))

### Medicine & Surgery

**Adachi, Buntaro** Dr. Med., Emeritus Prof. Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1865 in Shizuoka-ken; grad. from the Medical Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '94; Ass.-Prof. at his alma-mater and Prof. at 3rd High School till '98; studied anatomy in Germany, '99-04. Wrote in German an exhaustive treatise on the “Arterial System of Japanese.” Add. Kamikyo Yoshida-machi, Kyoto. — p. 68 ([scan leaf 641](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n641/mode/1up))

**Akutsu, Saburo** Dr. Med., Prop. of Akutsu Surgical Hospital; b. 1873; grad. at Med. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '98; further studied in Germany; became chief of Surgical Dept. of Juntendo Hospital. Add. Kanda Awaji-cho, Tokyo. — p. 68 ([scan leaf 641](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n641/mode/1up))

**Araki, Torasaburo** M.D. (Strasburg), ex-Pres. Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1866 in Gumma-ken; edu. at Special Course, Med. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '89; studied physiology at Strasburg Univ., Germany, '89-91; Prof. at 3rd High School, '95; transferred to Kyoto Imp. Univ.; Dean of its Med. Coll., and Pres. of the Univ., '15-29. Add. Kyoto. — p. 68 ([scan leaf 641](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n641/mode/1up))

**Asahi, Kenkichi** M.D., Prof. at Med. Dept., Kyushu Imp. Univ.; b. 1874 in Kyoto-fu; grad. from Med. Dept., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '97; then studied in Germany and on his return home appointed Ass.-Prof. at Kyushu Imp. Univ.; is proprietor of an Ear, Nose & Throat Hospital. Add. Fukuoka city. — p. 68 ([scan leaf 641](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n641/mode/1up))

**Chiba, Shin-ichiro** M.D., Pres. of Chiba Hospital (private) in Tokyo for diseases of Ear, Nose and Throat; b. 1880 in Tokyo; edu. at Med. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '05, and further studied his speciality at the post-grad. course and next at Strasburg Univ., and Frankfort Hospital, '08-11; served at the Junten-do Hospital, '08-20 and then established the present Hospital. Add. Nihombashi Hama-cho, Tokyo. — p. 68 ([scan leaf 641](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n641/mode/1up))

**Chiwaki, Morinosuke** L.L.D. (Loyola Univ., Chicago), Pres. of Tokyo Dental Coll.; b. 1870 at Hitachi; grad. from Keio Univ., and was for some years on the staff of the Tokyo Shimpo; grad. from Dr. Takayama's Dental School, and after practice for some years established the present school. Add. Tokyo Dental Coll., Kanda. — p. 69 ([scan leaf 642](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n642/mode/1up))

**Dohi, Keizo** M.D., Dermatologist, Emeritus Prof. Tokyo Imp. Univ., Chairman of the Japanese Derm. Society; grad. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., '91 and Univ. Hall; studied his speciality in Austria, Germany, and France, '94-98; is corresponding member of the Vienna ('02), Berlin ('03), and Paris Derm. Society ('11); recommended hon. mem. of the Italian Derm. Society, '06; is Editor of the Japanese Journal of Derm. and Urinology; was for many year Prof. at his alma mater. Add. Koj. Shimo-Nibancho, Tokyo. — p. 69 ([scan leaf 642](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n642/mode/1up))

**Fujikawa, Yu** D.M., D. Litt., Prof. at Toyo, Keio and Japan Women's Universities & ex-Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1865 in Hiroshima-ken; grad. from Med. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '89; further studied in Germany; was once lecturer on pathology at Kyoto Imp. Univ. Add. Kamakura Yukinoshita. — p. 69 ([scan leaf 642](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n642/mode/1up))

**Futaki, Kenzo** M.D., Expert of Epidemic Diseases Research Institute, Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1873 in Akita-ken; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ. Add. Hongo Motofujicho, Tokyo. — p. 69 ([scan leaf 642](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n642/mode/1up))

**Hata, Benzo** M.D., Prof. of Surgery & Dean of Med. Coll., Hokkaido Imp. Univ.; b. 1879 in Fukui; grad. from Medicine, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '02; after serving as Assis. at the alma mater's hospital and next at the Sapporo Hospital, was sent for study to Berlin and Prague in '12; on return home in '14 appointed Prof. at Hokkaido Imp. Univ. and Dean of its Med. Dept. Add. Sapporo. — p. 69 ([scan leaf 642](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n642/mode/1up))

**Hata, Sahachiro** M.D., co-worker of Dr. Erlich in the discovery of No. 606 or Salvarsan; b. 1872 in Iwami; grad. with honors from Med. Dept. of Okayama High School, '95; was Dr. Kitazato's assistant at the Epidemic Investigation Laboratory, '98-07; studied at Berlin and Frankfort, '07-11; Dept. Chief of Keio Hospital. Add. Nakameguro, Tokyo. — p. 69 ([scan leaf 642](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n642/mode/1up))

**Hayashi, Haruo** M.D., Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., Dir. of the Infectious Diseases Investigation Laboratory; b. 1874 in Aichi-ken; studied at the Tokyo Imp. Univ.; its Assist.-Prof., '00 and Prof., '09. Add. Shitaya Yanaka, Tokyo. — p. 69 ([scan leaf 642](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n642/mode/1up))

**Higuchi, Shigeji** M.D. (Japan and Rostock, Germany), Obstetrician & Gynecologist; b. 1876 in Niigata-ken; edu. at Medicine, First High School, class '99, and further at Breslau, Rostock, Halle, Berlin and London between '00 and '11 with some break spent at home; now Prof. at Tokyo Jikei-kai Medical Coll. and is connected with its hospital; also practices at his Higuchi Hospital at Shiba Tamura-cho. Add. Nishikubo Shiroyama, Tokyo. — p. 69 ([scan leaf 642](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n642/mode/1up))

**Hirai, Masamichi** M.D., Surg.-Gen.; b. 1863 in Wakasa; edu. in Medicine, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '89; attached to Otsu Regiment, '90; studied in Germany, '97-00; Dir. of Tokyo Garrison Hos. and Vice-Pres. of Red Cross Hos., '06; then its Pres., '09-20. Add. Ushigome Yarai, Tokyo. — p. 69 ([scan leaf 642](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n642/mode/1up))

**Hirayama, Kinzo** M.D., Dir. of a private Gastro-enteric Hospital in Tokyo; b. 1876 in Ibaraki-ken; edu. at Coll. Med., Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '03; served as assistant at the alma mater, '03-05; was next appointed Vice-Pres. of the Hospital founded by Dr. Nagayo; studied at Heidelberg, Berlin, etc., '07-10. Add. Koi. Hara-machi, Tokyo. — p. 69 ([scan leaf 642](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n642/mode/1up))

**Hirayama, Narinobu** Baron (cr. '25), Pres. of Japan Red Cross since '20, Privy Councillor; b. 1850 in Kagoshima; was once in the Dept. of Finance and of Home Affairs; nominated Mem. of House of Peers, '93; Steward to Prince Arisugawa, '07. Add. Koishikawa Hara-machi, Tokyo. — p. 69 ([scan leaf 642](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n642/mode/1up))

**Hirota, Tsukasa** M.D., specialist in Children's Diseases, Emeritus Prof., Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1856 in Kochi-ken; edu. at Coll. Med. of said institution, class '80; served at the Kumamoto Med. School and its hospital, '81-83; further studied at Strasburg, '85-88; Prof. at his alma mater, '89-21; was once Physician-in-Ordinary to the Court. Add. Iidamachi Rokuchome, Tokyo. — p. 70 ([scan leaf 643](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n643/mode/1up))

**Inada, Ryukichi** M.D., Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., since '18; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., '00; after studying at the post-grad. course, studied in Germany, '02-05; Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ., '05-18; now in charge of the Inada Ward, Univ. Hospital, Tokyo. Add. Univ. Hospital, Tokyo. — p. 70 ([scan leaf 643](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n643/mode/1up))

**Inouye, Michiyasu** M.D., Oculist, Prop. & Dir. of Inouye Hospital; b. 1867 in Hyogo-ken; grad. from Med. Coll. of Tokyo Imp. Univ., '90; Prof. at 3rd High School in Kyoto, '95; est. his own Hospital, '04; is also noted as an “uta” poet and attached to the National Verse Office of Court. Add. Kojimachi Uchisaiwai-cho, Tokyo. — p. 70 ([scan leaf 643](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n643/mode/1up))

**Inouye, Tatsuji** M.D., Oculist; b. 1881 in Tokyo; studied his speciality at Tokyo Imp. Univ., Leipzig and Royal Ophthalmic Hospital, London; has published a number of monographs on eye-diseases; maintains his own hospital. Add. Surugadai, Tokyo. — p. 70 ([scan leaf 643](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n643/mode/1up))

**Inouye, Toyotaro** Oculist, Dir. of Tokyo Eye Hospital; b. 1861 in Matsuye; finished the local medical school in '85; also studied at Tokyo and then in Germany, '91-95. Add. Kojimachi Iida-machi, Tokyo. — p. 70 ([scan leaf 643](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n643/mode/1up))

**Irisawa, Tatsukichi** M.D.; b. 1867 in Niigata-ken; grad. from Med. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ.; further studied in Germany; Prof. at his alma mater, '88-94; meanwhile Dir. of Komagome Hospital; later Chief Physician-in-Ordinary to the Imperial Household, which post he resigned in '27. Add. Hongo Kinsuke-cho, Tokyo. — p. 70 ([scan leaf 643](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n643/mode/1up))

**Ishihara, Hisashi** M.D., Prof. of Dentistry at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1866 in Saitama; edu. at Coll. Med., above institution, class '94; further studied his speciality in U.S.A. and Germany, '99-03; meanwhile served as assist. at the alma mater; on returning home was appointed to the present chair. Add. Hongo Nezu-Suga-cho, Tokyo. — p. 70 ([scan leaf 643](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n643/mode/1up))

**Ishihara, Shinobu** M.D., Prof. of Ophthalmology, Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1879 in Tokyo; edu. at Coll. Med., Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '05; Surgeon Lieut., '06; after going through the post-graduate course was appointed Prof. at Military Medical College, '09; Resident Surgeon in Germany, '12, and studied his speciality at Jena, Freiburg, etc., returning home in '14 on the outbreak of the War; Dir. of Tokyo 1st Garrison Hospital and Prof. at the said College till '20; transferred to the present post later. Add. Sendagaya, Tokyo. — p. 70 ([scan leaf 643](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n643/mode/1up))

**Ishikawa, Teikichi** M.D., Dir. of Sugamo Brain Hospital (private); b. 1870 in Yamagata-ken; edu. at Med. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '95; Vice-Pres. of Yamagata Prefectural Hospital, '96; Pres. Shizuoka Pref. Hospital, '99; resumed study at the post-grad. course of his alma mater for about one year, and at the same time attended lectures on Psychology at Coll. of Literature; medical expert at the Metropolitan Police Office; founded his own hospital, '13. Add. Sugamo Nobyo-in, Tokyo. — p. 70 ([scan leaf 643](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n643/mode/1up))

**Ito, Hayazo** M.D., ex-Prof. Kyoto Imp. Univ.; b. 1865 in Tottori; grad. Medicine, Kyoto Imp. Univ., class '00; had charge of his adopted father's hospital at Tottori, then of the Sapporo Hospital, '90-99, meanwhile studying in Germany for two years; also had charge of the University Hospital, Kyoto. Add. Tottori. — p. 70 ([scan leaf 643](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n643/mode/1up))

**Iwase, Yu-ichi** M.D., Prof. of Obstetrics & Gynecology at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1875 in Tokyo; edu. at above institution, class '00; Assistant at the alma mater on graduation; instructor at Okayama Medical College, '02-04; Ass.-Prof. at the alma mater, '04-06; further studied in Germany and France, '0[illegible]-10; Prof. at the alma mater since his return. Add. Tokyo Imp. Univ. — p. 70 ([scan leaf 643](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n643/mode/1up))

**Kanasugi, Eigoro** M.D., Otorhinolaryngologist, Prop. of Kanasugi Hospital, Crown Mem. House of Peers since '22; b. 1865 in Chiba-ken; edu. in Germany, '81-91; founded his own Hospital; represented Japan at the International Otonasopharyn Conference at Vienna, '08; was once an M.P. Add. Kanasugi Hospital, Kanda Surugadai, Tokyo. — p. 71 ([scan leaf 644](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n644/mode/1up))

**Katayama, Kuniyoshi** M.D., Emeritus Prof. Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1855 in Shizuoka-ken; studied medical jurisprudence in Austria and Germany, '84-88, and has since been Prof. at the Med. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ. Add. Hongo Nishikata-machi, Tokyo. — p. 71 ([scan leaf 644](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n644/mode/1up))

**Kikkoji, Shaku** M.D., in charge of Gastro-Enteric Dept., Kyoundo Hospital, Tokyo; b. 1878 in Tochigi-ken; edu. in Medicine, Kyoto Imp. Univ., class '06; further at its post-graduate course and finally at Berlin Univ., '08-12; Assist. at the alma mater, '06-08; has been in the service of the Hospital since his return home. Add. Kyoundo Hospital, Tokyo. — p. 71 ([scan leaf 644](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n644/mode/1up))

**Kimura, Tokuya** M.D., Pres. of Izumibashi Charity Hospital donated by the Mitsui Family, Lecturer at Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1871 in Niigata-ken; edu. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '99, and further at Berlin, Munchen, etc., '10-04, specializing in Lung Diseases. Add. Izumibashi Hospital, Kanda, Tokyo. — p. 71 ([scan leaf 644](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n644/mode/1up))

**Kinoshita, Seichu** M.D., Pres. of Kinoshita Obstetric & Gynecological Hospital, Tokyo; b. 1869 in Wakasa; edu. in Medicine, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '94; further studied his speciality in Germany, '97-98; Prof. at Osaka Prefectural School of Medicine, '99; assist.-Prof. and then full Prof. at Tokyo Imp. Univ., '99-17. Add. Kinoshita Hospital, Kojimachi, Tokyo. — p. 71 ([scan leaf 644](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n644/mode/1up))

**Kiribuchi, Kyoji** M.D., Dir. of Kiribuchi Optical Hospital; b. 1870 in Tokyo; grad. from Med. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '95; further studied in Germany for three years; is one of the noted ophthalmologists. Add. Shitaya-ku, Tokyo. — p. 71 ([scan leaf 644](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n644/mode/1up))

**Kitajima, Taichi** M.D., Bacteriologist & Prof. at Keio Univ., Mem. of the Kitazato Institute; b. 1862 in Kanazawa; grad. from Med. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., '94; was on the staff of the Infectious Disease Institute, Home Dept., till '97; further studied his speciality in Germany, '97-01; again appointed expert to the Home Dept., '01-14; meanwhile sent to Philippines and Italy for invetsigation; appointed to the faculty of the Keio in '14 where he occupies chair of Bacteriology. Add. Nishi-okubo, Tokyo. — p. 71 ([scan leaf 644](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n644/mode/1up))

**Kitazato, Shibasaburo** Baron (created '15), M.D., Pres. of Kitazato Pathological Laboratory, Dean of Med. Coll. Keio Univ.; b. 1859 at Kumamoto; studied in Germany under Prof. Koch, '85-91; discovered the germs of tetanus, diphtheria, pest (in conjunction with Dr. Aoyama), etc.; had charge of the Govt. Blood-Serum Laboratory; was granted the Red Eagle by the Kaiser, '09; retired from Govt. service to open his own laboratory in '14. Add. Azabu Nakano-cho, Tokyo. — p. 71 ([scan leaf 644](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n644/mode/1up))

**Koganei, Ryosei** M.D., Emeritus Prof. Tokyo Imp. Univ., his speciality being brain diseases; b. 1858 at Nagaoka; edu. at Medicine, Tokyo Imp. Univ., '70; studied at Berlin, '81-85; was connected with the University till he resigned in '22. Add. Hongo Akebonocho, Tokyo. — p. 71 ([scan leaf 644](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n644/mode/1up))

**Kure, Ken** M.D., Prof. at Med. Coll. Tokyo Imp. Univ.; grad. from Tokyo Imp. Univ., '07; further studied at Prague Univ., '11-13; served as assistant at his alma mater, '08-10; Lecturer at same in '14 and also Chief Physician of the Mitsui Charity Hospital; Prof. at Med. Coll., Kyushu Imp. Univ., '21; transferred to the present post, '24. Add. Med. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ. — p. 71 ([scan leaf 644](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n644/mode/1up))

**Kure, Shuzo** M.D., Emer. Prof. Tokyo Imp. Univ.; b. 1865 in Tokyo; grad. Medicine at Tokyo Imp. Univ., '90; studied in Germany, '97-00; is a specialist on insanity. Add. Hongo Yushima Shinhanacho, Tokyo. — p. 72 ([scan leaf 645](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n645/mode/1up))

**Matsuo, Yoshio** 2nd Baron, Military pharmaceutist (ret.), 2nd son of late Shinzen Matsuo (1st Baron & Governor of Bank of Japan); b. 1876 in Tokyo; grad. from Chiba Medical School in '97 and was appointed pharmacist which he left a few years after. Add. Akasaka Shinsaka, Tokyo. — p. 72 ([scan leaf 645](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n645/mode/1up))

**Matsushita, Teiji** M.D. (Japan and Halle); b. 1875 in Kagoshima; edu. at the Fifth (Kumamoto) High School, class '94; further studied at Freiberg, Giessen, Halle, etc., '95-20; Prof. at Kyoto Imp. Univ., '03-20, occupying chair of Hygiene, Micro-biology, etc. Works: (in Japanese) Parasitic Diseases, Wonderful Habits and Sights of Animals, etc. Add. Aoyama Minami-cho, Tokyo. — p. 72 ([scan leaf 645](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n645/mode/1up))

**Minami, Daiso** M.D., Pres. of Minami Gastro-Enteric Hospital, Tokyo; b. 1878 in Fukushima-ken; edu. in Medicine, Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '04; further at the post-grad. course and in Germany, '10-13; served as assistant at his alma mater in '04 and next as regular staff member of the Stomach & Intestine Hospital, Tokyo. Add. Minami Hospital, Kobiki-cho, Tokyo. — p. 72 ([scan leaf 645](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n645/mode/1up))

**Miura, Kinnosuke** M.D., Emeritus Prof. Tokyo Imp. Univ., ex-Physician-in-Ordinary to the Court; b. 1864 in Tokyo; grad. Med. Coll. Tokyo Imp. Univ., '87; further studied in Germany and America, '89-92; Ass't.-Prof. in his alma mater on returning; full prof. in '95; accompanied the Peace Envoys, '18 as an unofficial member, and the Crown Prince when he visited Europe in '21. Add. Kanda Surugadai, Tokyo. — p. 72 ([scan leaf 645](https://archive.org/details/japan-year-book-1930/page/n645/mode/1up))

**Miwa, Shintaro** M.D., specialist in Children's Diseases, Dir. of Enjudo (private) Hospital, Tokyo; b. 1870 in Kanagawa; studied at Med. Coll., Tokyo Imp. Univ., class '94; further in Germany, '95-98; served as assist. at first at his alma mater; was lecturer there on returning home; Prof. at the same, '01-15; resigned to begin practice, '15. Add.
